### [1] [Searches for GeV-Scale ALPs at RHIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07739)
*Kaori Fuyuto,Claudio Andrea Manzari,Hitoshi Murayama*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Ultra-peripheral Au+Au collisions at RHIC can probe axion-like particles (ALPs) via the resonant process $γγ \to a \to γγ$, leveraging the $Z^4$-enhanced two-photon luminosity and low photon energy thresholds. Using 1.9 nb⁻¹ of PHENIX data, the study estimates sensitivity to ALP masses between 2–5 GeV and couplings $g_{aγγ} \gtrsim 4\times10^{-4}~\text{GeV}^{-1}$, exploring uncharted parameter space. Larger datasets could significantly improve reach, urging dedicated analyses of existing RHIC data.\n\n
RHIC 上的超外围 Au+Au 碰撞可通过共振过程 $γγ \to a \to γγ$ 探测类轴子粒子（ALP），利用重离子碰撞中 $Z^4$ 增强的双光子光度和 RHIC 可实现的低光子能量阈值。基于 PHENIX 实验已收集的 1.9 nb⁻¹ 数据，该研究估计对质量在 2–5 GeV 范围内、耦合强度 $g_{aγγ} \gtrsim 4\times10^{-4}~\text{GeV}^{-1}$ 的 ALP 具有灵敏度，探索了此前未被覆盖的参数空间。更大的数据集可显著提升探测能力，因此亟需对 RHIC 上已有的超外围碰撞数据开展专门分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to exploit the unique capabilities of ultra-peripheral heavy-ion collisions at RHIC—specifically the $Z^4$ enhancement in two-photon luminosity and low photon detection thresholds—to search for axion-like particles (ALPs) in a mass range (2–5 GeV) that is challenging for other experimental setups. This approach targets the resonant production channel $γγ \to a \to γγ$, which offers a clean signature and access to previously unconstrained regions of the ALP-photon coupling parameter space.\n\n
该研究的动机是利用 RHIC 上超外围重离子碰撞的独特优势——特别是双光子光度的 $Z^4$ 增强效应和较低的光子探测阈值——来搜寻质量范围在 2–5 GeV 的类轴子粒子（ALP），这一区间对其他实验构型具有挑战性。该方法聚焦于共振产生道 $γγ \to a \to γγ$，该道具有干净的信号特征，并能探测此前未被约束的 ALP-光子耦合参数空间。

Method: The authors simulate both signal ($γγ \to a \to γγ$) and dominant background processes—including light-by-light scattering, hadronic resonance decays, and misidentified $e^+e^-$ pairs—in ultra-peripheral Au+Au collisions at RHIC. They use the known two-photon luminosity scaling as $Z^4$ and assume 1.9 nb⁻¹ of integrated luminosity from the PHENIX experiment. By modeling detector acceptance and energy thresholds, they estimate expected event yields and derive projected upper limits on the ALP-photon coupling $g_{aγγ}$ as a function of ALP mass $m_a$.\n\n
作者在 RHIC 的超外围 Au+Au 碰撞中模拟了信号过程（$γγ \to a \to γγ$）和主要本底过程——包括光子-光子散射（light-by-light scattering）、强子共振态衰变以及误辨认的 $e^+e^-$ 对。他们利用已知的双光子光度按 $Z^4$ 标度的特性，并假设 PHENIX 实验已积累 1.9 nb⁻¹ 的积分亮度。通过建模探测器接受度和能量阈值，他们估算预期事例产额，并推导出 ALP-光子耦合常数 $g_{aγγ}$ 随 ALP 质量 $m_a$ 变化的预期上限。

Result: The analysis demonstrates sensitivity to axion-like particles with masses in the range $2~\text{GeV} \lesssim m_a \lesssim 5~\text{GeV}$ and photon couplings $g_{aγγ} \gtrsim 4\times10^{-4}~\text{GeV}^{-1}$ using existing PHENIX data. This probes a region of the $(m_a, g_{aγγ})$ parameter space not previously accessible by other experiments, such as beam-dump setups, fixed-target experiments, or $e^+e^-$ colliders.\n\n
该分析表明，利用现有的 PHENIX 数据，可对质量范围为 $2~\text{GeV} \lesssim m_a \lesssim 5~\text{GeV}$、光子耦合强度 $g_{aγγ} \gtrsim 4\times10^{-4}~\text{GeV}^{-1}$ 的类轴子粒子具有探测灵敏度。这探索了 $(m_a, g_{aγγ})$ 参数空间中此前其他实验（如束流打靶实验、固定靶实验或 $e^+e^-$ 对撞机）无法覆盖的区域。

Conclusion: Ultra-peripheral collisions at RHIC provide a powerful and underutilized avenue to search for GeV-scale axion-like particles via photon fusion. The existing dataset already offers competitive sensitivity, and future analyses with larger luminosities—potentially from PHENIX or other RHIC experiments—could significantly extend the reach in both mass and coupling, making this a compelling opportunity for new physics searches in heavy-ion collision data.\n\n
RHIC 上的超外围碰撞为通过光子融合搜寻 GeV 量级类轴子粒子提供了一条强大但尚未充分利用的途径。现有数据集已具备有竞争力的灵敏度，而未来利用更大亮度的数据集（可能来自 PHENIX 或其他 RHIC 实验）的分析可显著拓展在质量和耦合强度上的探测范围，使得在重离子碰撞数据中寻找新物理成为一个极具吸引力的机会。

Abstract: We point out that ultra-peripheral Au+Au collision data collected at the Relativistic Heavy Ion Collider, operational during 2000-2026, can be used to search for axion-like particles coupled to photons via the resonant process $γγ\to a \to γγ$. Exploiting the $Z^4$ enhancement of the two-photon luminosity in heavy-ion collisions and the low photon energy thresholds achievable at RHIC, we simulate signal and background processes, the latter dominated by light-by-light scattering, hadronic resonance production, and misidentified $e^+e^-$ pairs, and estimate upper limits on the ALP-photon coupling $g_{aγγ}$ assuming $1.9~\text{nb}^{-1}$ of existing data collected by the PHENIX experiment. We find sensitivity to ALP masses in the range $2~\text{GeV} \lesssim m_a \lesssim 5~\text{GeV}$ with couplings $g_{aγγ} \gtrsim 4\times 10^{-4}~\text{GeV}^{-1}$, probing previously unexplored regions of parameter space. Access to larger luminosity datasets could substantially extend the sensitivity of this search, motivating a dedicated analysis of ultra-peripheral collision data collected at RHIC by PHENIX as well as other experiments.

</details>


### [2] [Core Composition Effects on the QCD Axion Mass Limit from Neutron Star Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07742)
*Fernando Arias-Aragón,Federico Nola*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper investigates how the presence of hyperons and $\\Delta$ resonances in neutron star cores—modeled via different equations of state—affects existing astrophysical limits on the QCD axion mass (KSVZ and DFSZ models). It finds that the resulting shift in the axion mass bound is generally mild, supporting the robustness of current constraints. In the DFSZ case, the revised limit may fall within IAXO’s sensitivity range, potentially enabling IAXO to probe neutron star core composition if an axion signal is detected.
\n\n
本文研究了中子星核心中可能存在超子（hyperons）和$\\Delta$共振态（通过不同状态方程建模）对现有QCD轴子质量限制（KSVZ和DFSZ模型）的影响。研究发现，轴子质量界限的变化总体上较温和，从而支持了当前约束的稳健性。在DFSZ情形下，修正后的界限可能落入IAXO实验的灵敏度窗口内，若在该窗口内探测到轴子信号，则IAXO有望揭示中子星核心的成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard limits on the QCD axion mass from neutron star cooling assume a minimal composition of the core (neutrons, protons, electrons, muons). However, under extreme densities in massive neutron stars, exotic baryonic degrees of freedom like hyperons and $\\Delta$ resonances may appear on-shell, potentially altering axion emissivity and thus the inferred mass bounds. The authors aim to quantify this dependence to assess the robustness of existing limits and explore implications for next-generation experiments like IAXO.
\n\n
标准中子星冷却对QCD轴子质量的限制通常假设星体核心仅包含中子、质子、电子和缪子。然而，在大质量中子星的极高密度下，超子和$\\Delta$共振态等奇异重子自由度可能以实壳（on-shell）形式出现，从而改变轴子发射率并影响推断出的质量界限。作者旨在量化这种依赖性，以评估现有界限的稳健性，并探讨其对IAXO等下一代实验的意义。

Method: The authors employ various realistic equations of state (EoS) that incorporate hyperons and/or $\\Delta$ resonances in neutron star matter. They compute the axion production rate via nucleon-nucleon bremsstrahlung and modified Urca processes, extended to include contributions from these additional baryonic species. Using observed neutron star surface temperatures and cooling models, they derive updated upper bounds on the axion mass for both KSVZ and DFSZ scenarios as a function of EoS.
\n\n
作者采用多种包含超子和/或$\\Delta$共振态的现实状态方程（EoS）来描述中子星物质。他们计算了通过核子-核子轫致辐射和修正Urca过程产生的轴子发射率，并将这些过程推广至包含额外重子组分的贡献。结合观测到的中子星表面温度和冷却模型，他们针对KSVZ和DFSZ两种情形，推导出依赖于状态方程的轴子质量新上限。

Result: The derived axion mass limits exhibit only mild dependence on the inclusion of hyperons and $\\Delta$ resonances across different EoSs, confirming the robustness of previous bounds. Specifically, for DFSZ axions, the upper limit tightens sufficiently in some EoSs to fall within the projected sensitivity range of the International Axion Observatory (IAXO). This creates a potential observational link between an IAXO detection and the internal composition of neutron stars.
\n\n
所得轴子质量界限在不同状态方程下对超子和$\\Delta$共振态的引入仅表现出微弱依赖性，证实了先前界限的稳健性。特别地，对于DFSZ轴子，在某些状态方程下，其上限收紧至足以落入国际轴子天文台（IAXO）的预期灵敏度范围内。这建立了IAXO探测与中子星内部成分之间的潜在观测联系。

Conclusion: The standard astrophysical bounds on QCD axion mass from neutron star cooling are largely insensitive to the uncertain high-density composition of the core, reinforcing their reliability. Moreover, in the DFSZ model, refined limits may intersect with IAXO’s reach, offering a unique opportunity: a future axion detection by IAXO within this window could indirectly reveal whether exotic baryons like hyperons or $\\Delta$ resonances exist in neutron star cores.
\n\n
来自中子星冷却的QCD轴子质量天体物理界限对核心高密成分的不确定性基本不敏感，从而增强了其可靠性。此外，在DFSZ模型中，修正后的界限可能与IAXO的探测能力相交，提供了一个独特机会：若IAXO在未来该窗口内探测到轴子，可间接揭示中子星核心中是否存在超子或$\\Delta$共振态等奇异重子。

Abstract: Neutron stars are very dense media in which axions may be produced. This has been used to set limit on the QCD axion mass, usually under the assumption that only neutrons, protons, electrons, and muons appear in the star core. Given the extreme conditions reached within neutron stars, it is reasonable to consider that other particles, such as hyperons and $Δ$ resonances, may exist on-shell. In this work, we study how the limit on the mass of QCD axions, namely KSVZ and DFSZ invisible axions, is altered when different equations of state are used, allowing for heavier particles to appear in the neutron star core. We find that this dependence is in general mild and thus reinforces the reliability of the known limit. Additionally, in the DFSZ scenario, it may drive the limit within the sensitivity window for IAXO. This would allow this experiment to discern the composition of neutron star cores if an axion were to be observed within that window.

</details>


### [3] [Detector performance at SHiP for cascade-produced long-lived particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07743)
*Matei Climescu,Yehor Kyselov,Maksym Ovchynnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper evaluates the impact of cascade production on detectable long-lived particle (LLP) signals at the SHiP experiment, finding that while cascade mechanisms can increase LLP yields, detector-level effects—especially soft kinematics and geometric acceptance—significantly suppress observable rates. For axion-like particles (ALPs), only light masses benefit modestly; for heavy neutral leptons (HNLs), cascade contributions are subdominant. Potential mitigations include relaxed selection cuts or an active-target subdetector.
\n\n
本文评估了级联产生机制对SHiP实验中可观测的长寿命粒子（LLP）信号的影响，发现尽管级联机制可增加LLP产额，但探测器层面的效应——尤其是软动量学和几何接受度——显著压低了可观测事例率。对于类轴子粒子（ALP），仅在最轻质量区间有适度增强；对于重中性轻子（HNL），级联贡献在考虑子粒子几何接受度后已属次主导。可能的缓解方案包括放宽事例选择条件或引入主动靶次级探测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous work suggested that cascade production in SHiP’s thick target could greatly boost the number of LLPs reaching the decay volume. However, these cascade-produced LLPs tend to be soft, raising concerns that daughter-particle reconstruction inefficiencies and geometric acceptance losses might negate the apparent gain. This paper aims to rigorously quantify this suppression and assess whether cascade channels remain phenomenologically relevant under realistic detector conditions.
\n\n
先前研究表明，SHiP厚靶中的级联产生机制可大幅增加到达衰变区的LLP数量。然而，这类级联产生的LLP通常动量较软，引发担忧：子粒子重建效率低下和几何接受度损失可能抵消表观增益。本文旨在严格量化此类压低效应，并评估在真实探测器条件下级联道是否仍具有现象学意义。

Method: The authors combine a semi-analytic calculation of the LLP production rate from cascades with a detailed detector-level simulation focused on reconstruction efficiency in the electromagnetic calorimeter. They study two benchmark scenarios: (1) photophilic axion-like particles (ALPs) from electromagnetic cascades, and (2) heavy neutral leptons (HNLs) from secondary kaon decays. Geometric acceptance and kinematic thresholds for daughter particles are explicitly modeled. They also explore modifications to the detector design and event selection to recover lost signal efficiency.
\n\n
作者将级联产生LLP事例率的半解析计算与针对电磁量能器中重建效率的详细探测器级模拟相结合。研究了两个基准情形：(1) 电磁级联中产生的亲光子类轴子粒子（ALP）；(2) 次级K介子衰变产生的重中性轻子（HNL）。显式建模了子粒子的几何接受度和运动学阈值。此外还探索了探测器设计和事例选择的改进方案以恢复丢失的信号效率。

Result: For ALPs, cascade production provides at most a moderate enhancement over primary production, and only for masses $m_a \\lesssim 100\\,\\text{MeV}$; at higher masses, the cascade contribution is negligible due to poor daughter reconstruction. For HNLs from secondary kaons, the cascade channel is already subdominant after applying daughter-level geometric acceptance cuts. The study identifies that relaxing transverse momentum ($p_T$) or vertex displacement requirements, or adding an active-target subdetector near the target, could partially recover the cascade signal.
\n\n
对于ALP，级联产生至多在质量 $m_a \\lesssim 100\\,\\text{MeV}$ 时比主产生道有适度增强；在更高质量下，由于子粒子重建困难，级联贡献可忽略。对于次级K介子产生的HNL，在施加子粒子几何接受度切割后，级联道已属次主导。研究指出，放宽横向动量（$p_T$）或顶点位移要求，或在靶附近增加主动靶次级探测器，可部分恢复级联信号。

Conclusion: Cascade production in SHiP’s target does not generically lead to large enhancements in observable LLP signals due to kinematic softness and detector acceptance effects. While it may contribute modestly for very light ALPs, it is largely irrelevant for heavier LLPs like HNLs. Future detector upgrades or analysis strategies that improve sensitivity to soft, displaced decays are necessary to fully exploit cascade channels.
\n\n
SHiP靶中的级联产生机制由于动量软化和探测器接受度效应，并不会普遍导致可观测LLP信号的大幅增强。尽管对极轻ALP可能有适度贡献，但对于HNL等较重LLP基本无关紧要。要充分利用级联道，未来需通过探测器升级或分析策略提升对软动量、大位移衰变的灵敏度。

Abstract: Previous studies have shown that cascade production in the thick target of the SHiP experiment may substantially enhance the number of light long-lived particles (LLPs) decaying in the fiducial volume. However, cascade-produced LLPs are typically soft, so daughter-level acceptance and reconstruction effects can strongly suppress the observable event rate. We quantify this suppression for two representative cases: photophilic axion-like particles produced in electromagnetic cascades, and heavy neutral leptons produced in decays of secondary kaons. We combine a semi-analytic event-rate calculation with a detector-level study of ALP reconstruction in the electromagnetic calorimeter. For the nominal SHiP detector design, cascade ALPs give at most a moderate enhancement over primary production, and only at the lightest masses; at higher masses, the cascade contribution becomes subdominant or negligible. For HNLs from secondary kaons, the cascade contribution is already subdominant after imposing daughter-level geometric acceptance. We also identify possible ways to recover part of the cascade event rate, including relaxed event-selection criteria and an active-target subdetector.

</details>


### [4] [Open quantum system approach to the transverse momentum broadening of a colour dipole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07744)
*François Arleo,Pietro Benzoni,Paul Caucal,Pol Bernard Gossiaux*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper employs the open quantum systems framework to analyze the evolution of a quark-antiquark ($q\\bar q$) pair traversing a dense QCD plasma, deriving its Lindblad equation and identifying a quasi-factorisation of the Wigner distribution in the correlation limit. This factorisation is modulated by a colour decoherence factor dependent on the ratio $θ_{q\\bar q}/θ_c$, where $θ_c \\sim (\\hat q L^3)^{-1/2}$ defines a critical angle tied to genuine quantum decoherence in colour space. The study shows that colour decoherence precedes full density matrix decoherence, signaling the emergence of classical dynamics, and finds that quantum diffusion corrections in transverse momentum are subleading.
\n\n
本文采用开放量子系统（open quantum systems）框架研究夸克-反夸克（$q\\bar q$）对在稠密QCD等离子体中的演化，推导了其Lindblad方程，并在关联极限下识别出Wigner分布的准因子化结构。该因子化受一个“色退相干”因子调控，依赖于角度比 $θ_{q\\bar q}/θ_c$，其中临界角 $θ_c \\sim (\\hat q L^3)^{-1/2}$ 与色空间中的真正量子退相干相关。研究表明，色退相干早于密度矩阵的完全退相干，标志着系统经典行为的出现；同时发现Lindblad方程中横向动量 $p_\\perp$ 的量子扩散项带来的修正较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to understand how quantum coherence and decoherence manifest in high-energy parton propagation through hot and dense QCD matter, such as the quark-gluon plasma created in heavy-ion collisions. Traditional approaches often assume factorisation between hard production and medium-induced effects, but a rigorous treatment of quantum correlations—especially in colour and kinematic spaces—is lacking. The open quantum systems formalism provides a natural language to describe the reduced dynamics of the $q\\bar q$ dipole interacting with a thermal environment, enabling a first-principles analysis of decoherence mechanisms.
\n\n
动机源于理解高能部分子在高温稠密QCD物质（如重离子碰撞中产生的夸克-胶子等离子体）中传播时量子相干性与退相干性的表现。传统方法通常假设硬产生过程与介质诱导效应之间可因子化，但缺乏对色空间和动量空间中量子关联的严格处理。开放量子系统形式体系为描述 $q\\bar q$ 偶极子与热环境相互作用的约化动力学提供了自然语言，使得能够从第一性原理出发分析退相干机制。

Method: The authors model the $q\\bar q$ pair as an open quantum system coupled to a QCD plasma bath, deriving a Lindblad master equation for its density matrix $\\rho$. They work in the boosted regime with small opening angle $θ_{q\\bar q} \\ll 1$ and focus on the correlation limit $p_\\perp \\gg q_\\perp, Q_s$, where $Q_s = \\sqrt{\\hat q L}$ is the saturation scale. By solving the Lindblad equation in phase space, they obtain the Wigner distribution and analyze its structure. Key tools include the identification of a critical angle $θ_c \\sim (\\hat q L^3)^{-1/2}$ and associated critical time $t_c \\sim 1/(\\hat q θ_c^2)$, which governs decoherence in colour space. They also examine the quantum diffusion term in $p_\\perp$ to assess corrections beyond quasi-factorisation.
\n\n
作者将 $q\\bar q$ 对建模为与QCD等离子体浴耦合的开放量子系统，为其密度矩阵 $\\rho$ 推导出Lindblad主方程。他们在小张角极限 $θ_{q\\bar q} \\ll 1$ 下工作，并聚焦于关联极限 $p_\\perp \\gg q_\\perp, Q_s$（其中 $Q_s = \\sqrt{\\hat q L}$ 为饱和动量标度）。通过在相空间中求解Lindblad方程，得到Wigner分布并分析其结构。关键工具包括识别临界角 $θ_c \\sim (\\hat q L^3)^{-1/2}$ 及其对应的临界时间 $t_c \\sim 1/(\\hat q θ_c^2)$，后者控制色空间中的退相干。作者还考察了 $p_\\perp$ 方向的量子扩散项，以评估准因子化图像之外的修正。

Result: In the correlation limit, the Wigner distribution exhibits quasi-factorisation into a hard splitting kernel and a medium-induced $q_\\perp$-broadening distribution. This factorisation is violated by a universal colour decoherence factor $\\mathcal{D}(θ_{q\\bar q}/θ_c)$, which suppresses off-diagonal elements of the density matrix in colour space when $θ_{q\\bar q} \\gtrsim θ_c$. The critical time $t_c$ marks the transition from coherent singlet-octet superpositions to incoherent mixtures, yet colour decoherence occurs before full kinematic decoherence. Corrections from the quantum diffusion term in $p_\\perp$ are found to be mild, justifying the quasi-factorised approximation for phenomenological applications.
\n\n
在关联极限下，Wigner分布呈现出准因子化结构：硬劈裂核与介质诱导的 $q_\\perp$ 展宽分布的乘积。该因子化被一个普适的“色退相干因子” $\\mathcal{D}(θ_{q\\bar q}/θ_c)$ 所破坏——当 $θ_{q\\bar q} \\gtrsim θ_c$ 时，该因子抑制密度矩阵在色空间中的非对角元。临界时间 $t_c$ 标志着从相干的单态-八重态叠加到非相干混合态的转变，但色退相干发生在完全动量空间退相干之前。研究发现 $p_\\perp$ 方向的量子扩散项修正较弱，为准因子化近似在唯象应用中的有效性提供了支撑。

Conclusion: The open quantum systems approach provides a consistent quantum-mechanical framework to describe parton energy loss and decoherence in QCD media. The identification of $θ_c$ and $t_c$ clarifies the hierarchy of decoherence scales: colour decoherence sets in at shorter times than full quantum decoherence, leading to an early onset of classical stochastic dynamics for the dipole. The robustness of quasi-factorisation—with controlled, mild corrections—supports its use in jet quenching phenomenology, while highlighting the essential role of quantum information concepts like entanglement and decoherence in high-energy QCD.
\n\n
开放量子系统方法为描述QCD介质中部分子的能量损失与退相干提供了自洽的量子力学框架。临界角 $θ_c$ 和临界时间 $t_c$ 的引入澄清了退相干尺度的层级结构：色退相干发生在比完全量子退相干更早的时间，导致偶极子动力学较早进入经典随机行为阶段。准因子化结构的稳健性（其修正可控且微弱）支持其在喷注淬火（jet quenching）唯象学中的应用，同时凸显了量子信息概念（如纠缠与退相干）在高能QCD中的核心作用。

Abstract: Using the open quantum systems formalism, we study the propagation of a quark-antiquark pair propagating through a dense QCD plasma of size $L$ and transverse momentum broadening transport coefficient $\hat q$, and we derive the Lindblad evolution equation for the density matrix of the system. We focus on the boosted regime where the opening angle $θ_{q\bar q}$ of this effective colour dipole satisfies $θ_{q\bar q}\ll 1$. In the correlation limit where the quark-antiquark relative transverse momentum $p_\perp$ is much larger than the imbalance $q_\perp$ as well as the medium typical transverse momentum scale $Q_s=\sqrt{\hat q L}$, we demonstrate that the resulting Wigner distribution displays quasi factorisation between a hard factor describing the hard splitting producing the $q\bar q$ pair and the transverse momentum imbalance $q_\perp$-distribution encoding the broadening induced by the medium. The factorisation is violated by a "colour decoherence" factor that controls the $θ_{q\bar q}$ dependence of the $q_\perp$-distribution through the ratio $θ_{q\bar q}/θ_c$, with $θ_c \sim (\hat q L^3)^{-1/2}$. The open quantum systems approach enables us to clarify the role of this critical angle $θ_c$ and its associated critical time $t_c$ in the genuine quantum decoherence of the density matrix in colour and kinematic space: in particular, $t_c$ controls both the suppression of the off-diagonal elements of the density matrix in colour space and the transition between singlet and octet states. We find, however, that colour decoherence sets in earlier than the full decoherence of the density matrix, thereby marking the onset of classical behaviour in the system. Finally, we investigate the corrections beyond the quasi-factorised picture due to the quantum diffusion term in $p_\perp$ of the Lindblad equation and we find that these corrections are mild.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: The authors simulate signal and background processes at muon collider benchmarks up to $\\sqrt{s} = 14$ TeV. By computing cross sections for both channels as functions of the kinetic mixing parameter $\\epsilon$ and the Higgs mixing angle $\\theta$, they construct likelihood-based sensitivity projections. The ratio of event rates in these channels breaks degeneracies between $\\epsilon$ and $\\theta$, allowing extraction of mixing parameters without beam polarization.
\n\n
作者在能量高达$\\sqrt{s} = 14$ TeV的muon对撞机基准点上模拟信号与本底过程，聚焦于两种产生机制：(1) radiative return（$\\mu^+ \\mu^- \\to Z' \\gamma$），伴随一个硬光子；(2) 通过$W$玻色子融合的vector boson fusion（VBF）过程（$\\mu^+ \\mu^- \\to Z' \\nu_\\mu \\bar{\\nu}_\\mu$）。通过计算这两个道的截面对kinetic mixing参数$\\epsilon$和Higgs mixing角$\\theta$的依赖关系，构建基于似然的灵敏度投影。两通道的事例率比值可打破$\\epsilon$与$\\theta$之间的简并，无需极化束流即可提取mixing参数。

Result: The muon collider achieves significant discovery reach for dark $Z'$ masses from 100 GeV up to the kinematic limit ($\\sim \\sqrt{s}$). For intermediate masses ($\\sim 1$–5 TeV), it surpasses the sensitivity of HL-LHC and future lepton colliders. Crucially, the ratio of radiative return to VBF event rates enables determination of the relative contributions of kinetic and Higgs mixing with precision $\\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$–$\\mathcal{O}(10^{-2})$, comparable to what is achievable using left-right asymmetry with fully polarized muon beams.

muon对撞机在dark $Z'$质量从100 GeV到运动学极限（$\\sim \\sqrt{s}$）范围内均具有显著的发现能力。对于中间质量区间（$\\sim 1$–5 TeV），其灵敏度超越HL-LHC及未来轻子对撞机。关键在于，radiative return与VBF事例率的比值可将kinetic mixing与Higgs mixing的相对贡献测定到$\\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$–$\\mathcal{O}(10^{-2})$精度，与使用全极化muon束流的left-right asymmetry方法相当。

Conclusion: A high-energy muon collider is a powerful probe of secluded dark $Z'$ scenarios with mixed couplings to the Standard Model. Its ability to simultaneously access radiative return and vector boson fusion channels provides a novel, polarization-independent method to disentangle kinetic and Higgs mass mixing, offering competitive or superior sensitivity compared to other future collider proposals.

Abstract: A secluded, massive Abelian gauge boson called a dark Z' may interact with the Standard Model through kinetic mixing and mass mixing in the Higgs sector. We determine the sensitivity of a future high-energy muon collider to discover such a particle and determine its mixing parameters. We examine a dark Z' with mass from 100 GeV up to the collider energy for a set of collider benchmarks up to 14 TeV. We show the discovery reach and compare to the current and proposed future colliders. A muon collider is sensitive to two complementary production modes: radiative return (muon fusion with an associated hard photon), and vector boson fusion of W bosons. The hard photon distinguishes these production modes and the relative rates of these processes allows one to determine the relative mixing. We show that these relative rates alone can determine the mixing to an accuracy comparable to that of a fully polarized muon beam using a left-right asymmetry.

Method: The analysis centers on effective field theory (EFT) with dimension-five operators coupling Standard Model fermions to light dark particles. 该分析基于有效场论（EFT），采用将标准模型费米子与轻暗粒子耦合的五维算符。作者研究标准模型粒子（如介子、轻子）的二体衰变过程（可见粒子 + 不可见ALP），计算衰变率与分支比。他们整合来自实验室味实验（如$K$、$B$、$\mu$衰变）、核心坍缩超新星冷却论证以及宇宙微波背景（CMB）能量注入限制的约束，并将这些映射到ALP耦合常数及紫外完备能标的上限。

Result: The paper presents a comprehensive set of current experimental and observational limits on flavor-violating couplings of sub-GeV ALPs, demonstrating that laboratory flavor experiments—despite their low-energy nature—can probe UV physics up to $10^{12} {\rm GeV}$ due to the dimension-five operator suppression. 本文给出了亚GeV轴子类粒子味破坏耦合的当前实验与观测限制的全面汇总，表明尽管实验室味实验属于低能范畴，但由于五维算符的压低效应，仍可探测高达$10^{12} {\rm GeV}$的紫外物理。研究揭示了基于加速器的味探针、来自超新星SN1987A的天体物理限制以及CMB谱形变或能量密度所给出的宇宙学约束之间的高度互补性。

Conclusion: Flavor physics provides a uniquely sensitive and complementary window into the parameter space of light dark matter, especially for axion-like particles with flavor-violating interactions. Abstract: We review the flavor phenomenology of light dark particles, focusing on axion-like particles with sub-GeV masses and generic flavor-violating couplings. Such states can naturally emerge from the spontaneous breaking of generic flavored symmetries, and are motivated by dark matter or the Strong CP Problem, with the QCD axion serving as a paradigmatic example. Light dark particles can be produced in two-body decays of Standard Model particles, giving rise to missing energy signals that can not only be observed in high-precision flavor experiments, but also be probed in core-collapse supernovae and the cosmic microwave background. These decays are controlled by dimension-five operators, which makes dedicated laboratory searches sensitive to very large UV scales up to $10^{12} {\rm GeV}$ and thus highly complementary to astrophysical and cosmological probes. We provide a comprehensive survey of the resulting limits and prospects across all relevant channels, highlighting the central role of flavor physics in exploring the landscape of light dark matter.

The results show that the branching ratios are sizable, especially for $B^0\to Λψ$ and $B_s^0\toΞ^0ψ$ in Type-I model, with values on the order of $\mathcal{O}(10^{-5})$ . Such processes are expected to facilitate the search for dark matter at hadron colliders and B factories.

### [8] [Maximal Abelian Flavor Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07799)
*Juanca Carrasco-Martinez,Lawrence J. Hall*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper introduces MAFS, a novel framework for describing fermion mass and mixing hierarchies using one small parameter $\\varepsilon_a$ per fermion multiplet, without assigning arbitrary charges. It successfully reproduces 15 observed flavor observables in $SU(5)$ with five parameters and in $SO(10)$ with only three. MAFS also links flavor structure to leptogenesis, explaining the baryon asymmetry in $SU(5)$ naturally, while requiring an extra small parameter in $SO(10)$.
\n\n
本文提出了一种名为 MAFS 的新框架，通过为每个费米子多重态分配一个小参数 $\\varepsilon_a$ 来描述夸克和轻子的质量与混合角层级结构，无需引入任意的荷。该框架在 $SU(5)$ 统一理论中用五个参数、在 $SO(10)$ 中仅用三个参数（取值为 $0.01, 0.02, 0.002$）成功复现了15个观测到的味物理观测量。MAFS 还将味结构与轻子生成机制（leptogenesis）联系起来：在 $SU(5)$ 中可自然解释宇宙重子不对称性，而在 $SO(10)$ 中则需额外引入一个约 0.2 的小参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to provide a more predictive and less ambiguous alternative to the Froggatt-Nielsen mechanism for explaining the observed hierarchies in quark and lepton masses and mixing angles. Unlike Froggatt-Nielsen, which relies on arbitrary charge assignments under a flavor symmetry, MAFS eliminates this freedom by associating a single small parameter $\\varepsilon_a$ with each fermion multiplet, thereby reducing model dependence and enhancing predictivity—especially in grand unified theories (GUTs) with fewer multiplets.
\n\n
动机是为了解释夸克和轻子质量及混合角的观测层级结构，提供一种比 Froggatt-Nielsen 机制更具预测性且更少任意性的替代方案。Froggatt-Nielsen 机制依赖于味对称性下任意的荷分配，而 MAFS 通过为每个费米子多重态关联一个独立的小参数 $\\varepsilon_a$，消除了这种自由度，从而减少了模型依赖性，并在多重态数量较少的大统一理论（GUTs）中显著提升了预测能力。

Method: MAFS assigns a single small expansion parameter $\\varepsilon_a$ to each fermion multiplet (e.g., $\\mathbf{10}$, $\\overline{\\mathbf{5}}$, $\\mathbf{1}$ in $SU(5)$), and constructs Yukawa couplings as products of these parameters raised to integer powers determined by group-theoretic considerations or minimality principles. The framework assumes that higher-dimensional operators suppressed by powers of $\\varepsilon_a$ generate hierarchical structures in the mass matrices. In GUTs like $SU(5)$ and $SO(10)$, the unification of quarks and leptons constrains the form of these matrices, linking quark and lepton sectors. Leptogenesis is then evaluated using the resulting neutrino mass and mixing parameters derived from the same $\\varepsilon_a$ set.
\n\n
MAFS 方法为每个费米子多重态（例如 $SU(5)$ 中的 $\\mathbf{10}$、$\\overline{\\mathbf{5}}$、$\\mathbf{1}$）分配一个独立的小展开参数 $\\varepsilon_a$，并通过群论考虑或极小性原理确定整数幂次，构造出由这些参数幂次乘积构成的 Yukawa 耦合。该框架假设由 $\\varepsilon_a$ 幂次压低的高维算符在质量矩阵中产生层级结构。在 $SU(5)$ 和 $SO(10)$ 等大统一理论中，夸克与轻子的统一约束了质量矩阵的形式，从而将夸克与轻子扇区关联起来。随后，利用由同一组 $\\varepsilon_a$ 导出的中微子质量和混合参数评估轻子生成机制（leptogenesis）。

Result: In $SU(5)$, MAFS describes 15 observed mass ratios and mixing angles at the factor-of-two level using five $\\varepsilon_a$ parameters. It predicts large neutrino mixing angles and small neutrino mass splittings as a consequence of the quark sector's hierarchy. In $SO(10)$, only three $\\varepsilon_a$ parameters ($0.01, 0.02, 0.002$) suffice to describe the same 15 observables. Furthermore, in $SU(5)$, the cosmological baryon asymmetry arises naturally from leptogenesis without introducing extra small parameters, whereas in $SO(10)$, an additional small parameter (~0.2) is required to match observations.
\n\n
在 $SU(5)$ 中，MAFS 利用五个 $\\varepsilon_a$ 参数在因子为二的精度内成功描述了15个观测到的质量比和混合角，并由此推断出：由于夸克扇区的层级结构，中微子必须具有大混合角和小质量分裂。在 $SO(10)$ 中，仅需三个 $\\varepsilon_a$ 参数（取值为 $0.01, 0.02, 0.002$）即可描述相同的15个观测量。此外，在 $SU(5)$ 中，宇宙学重子不对称性可自然地通过 leptogenesis 机制产生，无需引入额外的小参数；而在 $SO(10)$ 中，则需要一个额外的小参数（约 0.2）才能符合观测。

Conclusion: MAFS provides a minimal, predictive, and charge-free framework for flavor model building in grand unification. Its power increases with symmetry (e.g., in $SO(10)$), where fewer multiplets lead to fewer parameters. The framework not only explains fermion mass hierarchies but also connects them to leptogenesis, offering a unified origin for flavor structure and the baryon asymmetry of the universe—particularly elegantly in $SU(5)$.
\n\n
MAFS 为大统一理论中的味模型构建提供了一个极简、可预测且无需荷分配的框架。其预测能力随对称性增强而提升（例如在 $SO(10)$ 中），因为更少的多重态意味着更少的参数。该框架不仅解释了费米子质量层级，还将之与 leptogenesis 联系起来，为味结构和宇宙重子不对称性提供了统一的起源——尤其在 $SU(5)$ 中表现得极为简洁优雅。

Abstract: A framework, MAFS, is introduced that provides an approximate description of the hierarchies of quark and lepton masses and mixing angles in terms of a set of small parameters, $ε_a$, one for each fermion multiplet. 矢量相互作用增强了平行（$\\sigma_\\parallel$）和垂直（$\\sigma_\\perp$）于磁场方向的表面张力。值得注意的是，当存在矢量排斥时，$\\sigma_\\perp$随磁场强度增加而增大——这与先前工作中其渐近趋于零的结果相反。研究发现，中等强度磁场（$eB \\sim \\mathcal{O}(m_\\pi^2)$）是稳定夸克物质气泡成核所必需的。然而，矢量耦合略微降低了夸克物质的整体热力学稳定性。

Abstract: In this article, the surface tension and stability of quark matter modified by the vector interaction in a strong magnetic field are investigated in the quasiparticle model with the multiple reflection expansion. The self-consistent thermodynamic treatment of the chemical-potential-dependent quark mass is maintained by the effective bag function, which depends on both the chemical potential and the magnetic field. It is found that the vector interaction could enlarge the surface tension in both the parallel and transverse directions with respect to the magnetic field. In a stronger magnetic field region, the presence of the vector repulsive interaction leads to an increase in transverse surface tension with the magnetic field strength, which is opposite to the vanishing value without repulsive interaction in the previous work. Consequently, it is concluded that a moderate-intensity magnetic field is required for the formation of a quark matter bubble with the vector interaction. Finally, it is demonstrated that the vector interaction slightly reduces the stability of quark matter.

Abstract: The four-body decay $Λ_b^0\to pK^-π^+π^-$ has led to the first observation of baryonic $CP$ violation.   <summary>Details</summary>

For $\sqrt{s} > 2M_W$, they calculate the tree-level and loop-induced cross sections for $e^- μ^+ \to W^+ W^-$ in both single-sterile-neutrino and minimal type-I seesaw scenarios, comparing sensitivities across energy regimes.

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that while low-energy cLFV processes like $e^- μ^+ \to e^+ μ^-$ are theoretically clean, they are practically unobservable due to strong suppression from small active-sterile mixing. Abstract: We study high-energy charged-lepton-flavor-violating (cLFV) channels in $e^- μ^+$ scattering to probe heavy sterile neutrinos, which arise naturally in minimal extensions of the Standard Model. For $\sqrt{s} \le 2M_W$, we consider the process $e^- μ^+ \to e^+ μ^-$, which is dominated by one-loop box diagrams. We find this latter process to be significantly more promising for revealing the presence of heavy sterile neutrinos at $e-μ$ colliders.

作者采用基于 $b\to s\nu\bar\nu$ 跃迁的低能有效哈密顿量的模型无关分析方法，其中包含由右手性中微子场构成的维度六半轻子矢量算符。他们利用实验输入——特别是 Belle II 对 $\mathcal{B}(B\to K\nu\bar\nu)$ 的中心值和 $\mathcal{B}(B\to K^{*}\nu\bar\nu)$ 的上限——来约束这些新算符的 Wilson 系数。随后计算其他相关观测量的预言：$\mathcal{B}(B_s\to\phi\nu\bar\nu)$、$\mathcal{B}(\Lambda_b\to\Lambda\nu\bar\nu)$ 以及纵向极化分数 $F_L^{K^*}$，并分析它们与已测得的 $B\to K\nu\bar\nu$ 衰变率之间的关联。

Conclusion: The observed excess in $\mathcal{B}(B^+\to K^+\nu\bar\nu)$ can be naturally explained by New Physics mediated by right-handed neutrinos through dimension-six vector operators. Abstract: The recent Belle II measurement of $\mathcal{B}(B^+\to K^+ν\barν)$, which deviates from the Standard Model prediction, provides a strong motivation to search for New Physics in $b\to sν\barν$ transitions. Method: The authors define a rescaled linear combination $a_{μ-e} = a_μ - (m_μ/m_e)^2 a_e$, motivated by the fact that in QED and many BSM scenarios with flavor-universal couplings, the short-distance contributions to $a_ℓ$ scale as $m_ℓ^2$. \n\n
作者定义了一个重标度的线性组合 $a_{μ-e} = a_μ - (m_μ/m_e)^2 a_e$，其动机在于：在QED及许多具有味普适耦合的超出标准模型（BSM）场景中，$a_ℓ$ 的短程贡献按 $m_ℓ^2$ 缩放。这一缩放保证了 $a_{μ-e}$ 中紫外主导项的精确抵消。他们利用现有数据计算强子真空极化贡献 $a^{\rm HVP}_{μ-e}$ 并传播不确定性，表明其相对于 $a^{\rm HVP}_μ$ 显著压低。

Abstract: We propose the linear combination of the anomalous magnetic moments of the muon and electron, $a_{μ-e} \equiv a_μ- (m_μ/m_e)^2 a_e$, as a natural low-energy window quantity, with enhanced sensitivity to the current discrepancy between the data-driven and lattice-QCD evaluations of hadronic contributions. The rescaling ensures an exact cancellations the short-range effects, thereby improving the UV behavior and bypassing a number of issues that arise in $a_μ$ or $a_e$ separately. Abstract: We investigate an empirical low-rank structure underlying the fermion mass spectrum.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The pion decay constant $f_\\pi$ is a fundamental low-energy parameter of QCD that enters phenomenological Lagrangians and Effective Field Theories (EFTs). Existing determinations often lack precision in the low-energy constants (LECs) of ChPT due to overfitting or insufficient constraints. This work addresses this by employing the LASSO regression technique to robustly identify the most relevant LECs from LQCD data, enabling reliable extrapolation toward the SU(3) flavor limit.

Method: The authors perform a global fit of recent three-flavor Lattice QCD (LQCD) data for $f_\\pi$, $f_K$, and $f_\\eta$ using three-flavor Chiral Perturbation Theory (ChPT) expanded to next-to-next-to-leading order (N$^2$LO). To overcome the challenge of fitting many low-energy constants (LECs) with limited data, they employ the LASSO (Least Absolute Shrinkage and Selection Operator) method—a sparse regression technique from machine learning that penalizes the $\\ell_1$-norm of the coefficient vector. This promotes sparsity and selects only the most statistically significant LECs, improving predictive power and reducing overfitting. The resulting constrained ChPT expressions are then used as input in covariant Baryon ChPT to compute octet baryon masses in the SU(3) symmetric limit.

Result: Using LASSO-regularized fits of LQCD data, the authors determine the quark mass dependence of $f_\\pi$, $f_K$, and $f_\\eta$ with high precision up to pion masses of approximately 780 MeV, close to the SU(3) flavor-symmetric point. The method successfully identifies a minimal set of dominant low-energy constants (LECs) in the N$^2$LO ChPT expansion. As an application, they compute the masses of the baryon octet ($p$, $n$, $\\Sigma$, $\\Xi$, etc.) in the exact SU(3) limit using covariant Baryon ChPT, providing predictions grounded in a data-driven determination of mesonic LECs.

Conclusion: The integration of machine learning (LASSO) with Chiral Perturbation Theory enables a robust, high-precision extraction of low-energy constants from lattice QCD data, even in regimes approaching the SU(3) flavor limit. This approach not only improves the reliability of chiral extrapolations but also provides a solid foundation for predicting other hadronic observables—such as baryon masses—within consistent EFT frameworks. The results demonstrate the synergy between data science and theoretical EFT methods in non-perturbative QCD.

将机器学习（LASSO）与Chiral Perturbation Theory 相结合，即使在接近 SU(3) 味极限的区域，也能从格点QCD 数据中稳健且高精度地提取低能常数。该方法不仅提升了手征外推的可靠性，还为在自洽的有效场论（EFT）框架内预测其他强子观测量（如重子质量）提供了坚实基础。结果展示了数据科学与理论 EFT 方法在非微扰 QCD 研究中的协同效应。

作者进行了一个10维贝叶斯推断：其中两个物理参数描述畴壁引力波信号，八个干扰参数（nuisance parameters）用于建模仪器噪声和天体物理前景。他们利用模拟信号注入开展了LISA单独、Taiji单独以及PTA–LISA/PTA–Taiji联合分析——单探测器研究使用49个注入信号，联合分析则在PTA支持区域内使用65个注入信号。后验分布通过Clough–Tocher插值生成热图进行可视化。

While LISA and Taiji alone cannot break degeneracies in the underlying model, their synergy with PTA measurements enables precise reconstruction of key parameters and validates the domain-wall origin of the observed GW background.

Abstract: Domain walls provide an excellent fit to Pulsar Timing Array (PTA) data. In this work, we explore the PTA-compatible parameter space of domain-wall models and study both the reconstruction prospects at LISA and Taiji and the information gain achievable through joint PTA--LISA and PTA--Taiji analyses. In the space-based-detector-only analysis, the posterior is strongly degenerate in the underlying model parameters, although one principal parameter combination can be reconstructed with high precision where the signal is sufficiently strong. Our analysis is based on a 10-dimensional Bayesian inference with two domain-wall signal parameters and eight nuisance parameters describing instrumental noise and astrophysical foregrounds. TL;DR: This paper computes matrix elements of four-quark operators relevant for heavy-flavor hadron lifetimes using both full QCD sum rules and HQET sum rules, demonstrating their consistency at leading order and resolving previously reported discrepancies by carefully analyzing the nontrivial heavy-quark limit. \n\n 本文使用全QCD求和规则和HQET求和规则计算了与重味强子寿命相关的四夸克算符的矩阵元，在领头阶展示了二者的一致性，并通过细致分析非平凡的重夸克极限，澄清了文献中报道的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The lifetime differences among hadrons containing the same heavy quark (e.g., $B^+$ vs. $B_d^0$) cannot be explained at the parton level and require nonperturbative input from matrix elements of four-quark operators within the Heavy Quark Expansion (HQE). While both full QCD sum rules and HQET sum rules have been used to compute these matrix elements, significant numerical discrepancies between the two approaches have been reported in the literature, casting doubt on their reliability. This work aims to reconcile these approaches by rigorously deriving HQET sum rules from full QCD sum rules in the heavy-quark limit and identifying the source of prior inconsistencies. \n\n 含有相同重夸克的强子（例如 $B^+$ 与 $B_d^0$）之间的寿命差异无法在部分子层次上解释，而需依赖重夸克展开（Heavy Quark Expansion, HQE）框架中四夸克算符的非微扰矩阵元。尽管全QCD求和规则和HQET求和规则均被用于计算这些矩阵元，但文献中报道了二者之间存在显著数值差异，引发对其可靠性的质疑。本文旨在通过在重夸克极限下严格从全QCD求和规则导出HQET求和规则，并识别先前不一致性的根源，从而调和这两种方法。

Method: The authors compute the matrix elements of dimension-six four-quark operators using the method of full QCD sum rules at leading order in $\\alpha_s$. They then take the heavy-quark limit ($m_Q \\to \\infty$) of the full QCD correlator and spectral function to derive the corresponding HQET sum rules. Special attention is paid to contributions that do not commute with the heavy-quark limit—specifically, terms involving gluon condensates or higher-dimensional operators whose operator product expansion (OPE) structure changes nontrivially in the limit. The Borel transform is applied in both frameworks to suppress higher-state contributions and enhance ground-state dominance. \n\n 作者在 $\\alpha_s$ 领头阶使用全QCD求和规则方法计算六维四夸克算符的矩阵元。随后，他们对全QCD关联函数和谱函数取重夸克极限（$m_Q \\to \\infty$），以导出相应的HQET求和规则。特别关注那些与重夸克极限不可交换的贡献——尤其是涉及胶子凝聚或高维算符的项，其算符乘积展开（OPE）结构在该极限下发生非平凡变化。在两种框架中均应用了Borel变换，以压高激发态贡献并增强基态主导性。

Result: Numerical evaluation shows that when the heavy-quark limit is correctly implemented—including proper treatment of non-commuting contributions—the matrix elements obtained from full QCD sum rules and HQET sum rules agree within uncertainties. The previously reported large discrepancies are traced to an inconsistent application of the heavy-quark limit in earlier HQET sum rule calculations, particularly the mishandling of certain condensate contributions that require matching beyond naive dimensional analysis. \n\n 数值计算表明，当正确实施重夸克极限（包括对不可交换贡献的恰当处理）时，全QCD求和规则与HQET求和规则所得矩阵元在误差范围内一致。此前报道的巨大差异被追溯至早期HQET求和规则计算中对重夸克极限的不一致应用，尤其是对某些凝聚项的误处理——这些项需要超越朴素量纲分析的匹配。

Conclusion: The study establishes a consistent bridge between full QCD sum rules and HQET sum rules for four-quark operator matrix elements, confirming their equivalence at leading order when the heavy-quark limit is handled rigorously. This resolves a long-standing tension in the literature and provides a robust foundation for future precision calculations of heavy-flavor hadron lifetimes within the Heavy Quark Expansion framework. \n\n 本研究在四夸克算符矩阵元的计算中建立了全QCD求和规则与HQET求和规则之间的一致性桥梁，证实了在严格处理重夸克极限时二者在领头阶的等价性。这解决了文献中长期存在的张力，为未来在重夸克展开（HQE）框架内对重味强子寿命进行高精度计算提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Heavy quark expansion theory provides the standard theoretical framework for understanding the lifetimes of weakly decaying heavy-flavor hadrons. Within this framework, the matrix elements of four-quark operators play a crucial role in explaining the lifetime differences among hadrons containing the same heavy quark. In this work, we calculate these matrix elements at leading order using full QCD sum rules, with particular emphasis on deriving the corresponding HQET sum rules by taking the heavy-quark limit. While this limit is straightforward for most contributions, it turns out to be rather nontrivial for certain ones. Numerical analyses show that the results obtained from the two approaches are consistent with each other. We further clarify the origin of the large discrepancies reported in the literature between full QCD sum rule and HQET sum rule results. This work contributes to a deeper understanding of the relationship between the two sum-rule approaches and is expected to facilitate future applications of QCD and HQET sum rules in the study of heavy-flavor hadrons. The findings presented here may be of interest to both practitioners of QCD sum rules and researchers working on effective field theories.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The lattice QCD calculation of generalized form factors are exploited to determine the subtraction constants through all order dispersion relations of Deeply Virtual Compton Scattering (DVCS). The leading order relation is found to constrain significantly the real part of the Compton Form Factors (CFFs), and the higher order one reduces considerably both the real and imaginary part of CFFs in a global analysis of proton data. This is realized by a synthesis of the DVCS data and LQCD calculations within a neural network framework, whose architecture is specifically designed for a reliable extrapolation to unmeasured kinematic regime. 作者在B-LSSM框架下解析计算了带电轻子电偶极矩的主要两圈贡献（包括Barr–Zee型图及其他费曼图），纳入了来自传统超对称$\\mu$项及B-LSSM特有参数（如与$U(1)_{B-L}$规范扇区和额外Higgs场相关的复相位）的CP破坏相位。在推导出一般解析表达式后，他们在满足当前实验限制的B-LSSM参数空间内进行了数值扫描。

Result: Numerical evaluation shows that the conventional $\\mu$-term remains the dominant source for $d_\\mu$ and $d_\\tau$, but B-LSSM-specific CP-violating phases provide non-negligible enhancements. The predicted $d_\\mu$ lies within the projected sensitivity of Phase II of upcoming muon EDM experiments, while $|d_\\tau|$ can reach $\\mathcal{O}(10^{-21})\\,e\\cdot\\text{cm}$, potentially observable in future tau EDM searches.

Conclusion: The B-LSSM provides testable predictions for the EDMs of second- and third-generation leptons through both universal SUSY mechanisms and model-specific CP-violating phases. Upcoming high-precision EDM experiments will be able to probe substantial portions of the B-LSSM parameter space, offering a powerful avenue to test this extension of supersymmetry.

B-LSSM通过普适的超对称机制与模型特有的CP破坏相位，对第二、三代轻子的电偶极矩给出了可检验的预言。即将开展的高精度EDM实验将能够探测B-LSSM参数空间的相当大区域，为检验这一超对称扩展模型提供强有力的新途径。

Abstract: Recently proposed experiments are expected to significantly improve the measurement sensitivities of the electric dipole moments (EDMs) of muon ($d_μ$) and tau ($d_τ$). Given that theoretical predictions for $d_μ$ and $d_τ$ typically surpass those for the electron EDM, this work focuses on studying the contributions from the CP-violating (CPV) effects in the B-L supersymmetric (SUSY) standard model (B-LSSM) to $d_μ$ and $d_τ$. After considering the corrections from some two-loop diagrams, the contributions in the B-LSSM to the EDMs of charged leptons are presented analytically in general forms. The numerical results show that the traditional $μ$-term in most SUSY models makes dominant contributions to $d_μ$ and $d_τ$, while the B-LSSM specific CPV parameters also induce significant effects. It is found that across a substantial region of the B-LSSM parameter space, $d_μ$ falls well within the projected sensitivity at Phase II of the proposed experiment, and $|d_τ|$ can reach about $10^{-21}e\cdot\text{cm}$.

Result: The analysis yields ten narrow isoscalar poles with quantum numbers $J^P = 1/2^-, 3/2^-, 5/2^-$ in the mass range 7.72–7.96 GeV, organized into heavy-quark spin multiplets exhibiting near-degeneracies as predicted by HQSS. 分析得到十个窄的同位旋标量极点，量子数为 $J^P = 1/2^-, 3/2^-, 5/2^-$，质量范围在 7.72–7.96 GeV，按重夸克自旋对称性（HQSS）组织成近简并的多重态。其中四个极点与文献中先前的双道计算结果在约 2 MeV 内一致，验证了统一框架的自洽性。此外，由于强道间耦合，还出现了两个与 $B_sΛ_c$ 和 $B_s^{*}Λ_c$ 道相关的新深度束缚极点，其位置远离主导阈值。

Abstract: We study a family of genuinely exotic five-flavor molecular pentaquark states containing the five quark flavors $u,d,s,c,b$ that form experimentally accessible hadrons. We construct the meson-baryon interaction from the local hidden gauge symmetry combined with heavy-quark spin symmetry, following the chiral unitary description that reproduces the LHCb hidden-charm strange pentaquarks. Heavy-quark flavor symmetry allows us to obtain the $udsc\bar{b}$ sector by replacing the anti-charm quark in the meson with an anti-bottom quark while keeping the charm quark in the baryon. There are four states that overlap with the earlier two-sector study in the literature to about $2$ MeV, which shows that the separate-sector treatment is recovered as a limit of this work. Moreover, we also identify two additional, more deeply bound $B_sΛ_c$ and $B_s^{*}Λ_c$ poles generated by strong inter-channel coupling because these poles are farther from their dominated thresholds. ### [28] [Meson-Nucleus Bound States with Neural-Network Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09254)

Main category: hep-ph

A neural-network variational Monte Carlo method generalized for mesonic degrees of freedom solves the $(A{+}1)$-body Schrödinger equation. Results show that $φ$ binds starting at $A\ge2$ with tens of MeV binding energy, while $J/ψ$ and $η_c$ bind weakly from $A\ge4$ and $A\ge6$, respectively.   <summary>Details</summary>

This work addresses this gap by leveraging modern computational methods and high-fidelity potentials from HAL QCD to make testable predictions for upcoming experiments.

Method: The authors solve the $(A{+}1)$-body non-relativistic Schrödinger equation for a system of $A$ nucleons plus one meson ($φ$, $J/ψ$, or $η_c$) using a neural-network variational Monte Carlo (NN-VMC) framework. This method is generalized to include explicit mesonic degrees of freedom alongside nucleons. The two-body meson-nucleon ($MN$) and nucleon-nucleon ($NN$) interactions are taken from HAL QCD collaboration’s lattice QCD calculations at near-physical pion mass. The trial wave function is represented by a deep neural network, optimized via stochastic reconfiguration to minimize the energy expectation value. Benchmarking is performed on light nuclei ($^2$H–$^{12}$C) to validate the nuclear Hamiltonian before introducing the meson.

作者使用神经网络变分蒙特卡洛（NN-VMC）框架求解包含$A$个核子加一个介子（$φ$、$J/ψ$或$η_c$）的$(A{+}1)$体非相对论薛定谔方程。该方法被推广以显式包含介子自由度。两体介子-核子（$MN$）和核子-核子（$NN$）相互作用取自HAL QCD合作组在近物理π介子质量下的格点QCD计算结果。试探波函数由深度神经网络表示，并通过随机重构（stochastic reconfiguration）优化以最小化能量期望值。在引入介子前，先对轻核（$^2$H–$^{12}$C$）进行基准测试，以验证核哈密顿量的可靠性。

The binding energy per nucleon for charmonium ($J/ψ$, $η_c$) systems increases nearly linearly with $A$, while the $φ$-nucleus system shows non-monotonic behavior, peaking at $^4$He—indicative of a short-range, strongly attractive $φN$ potential. Additionally, the presence of the meson compresses the nucleon density distribution compared to the bare nucleus, and the meson’s spatial configuration evolves from a diffuse halo (at small $A$) to a compact, embedded state (at larger $A$).

计算预言：$φ$-原子核束缚态在$A \ge 2$时存在，结合能达$\sim$10–30 MeV；$J/ψ$-原子核态在$A \ge 4$时出现，结合能为几MeV；而$η_c$-原子核态仅在$A \ge 6$时形成，结合能低于1 MeV。对于粲偶素系统（$J/ψ$、$η_c$），每个核子的结合能随$A$近似线性增加；而$φ$-原子核系统则呈现非单调行为，在$^4$He处达到峰值——这反映了短程强吸引的$φN$势特性。此外，介子的存在压缩了核子密度分布（相比裸核），且介子的空间构型随$A$增大从弥散的晕态演变为紧致的嵌入态。

Conclusion: This work establishes a quantitative connection between first-principles lattice QCD meson-nucleon potentials and emergent many-body bound states in meson-nucleus systems. It demonstrates that modern neural-network quantum Monte Carlo methods can reliably handle complex few- to many-body problems involving both baryonic and mesonic degrees of freedom. The distinct binding patterns—especially the peak at $^4$He for $φ$—serve as clear signatures of the underlying short-range interaction physics. These results provide concrete predictions for experimental programs aiming to detect exotic mesic nuclei, particularly at facilities like J-PARC and FAIR.

本工作建立了格点QCD第一性原理介子-核子势与介子-原子核系统中涌现的多体束缚态之间的定量联系。研究表明，现代神经网络量子蒙特卡洛方法能够可靠处理同时包含重子与介子自由度的复杂少体至多体问题。不同的结合模式——尤其是$φ$系统在$^4$He处的结合能峰值——清晰揭示了底层短程相互作用的物理本质。这些结果为旨在探测奇异介子原子核的实验项目（如J-PARC和FAIR装置）提供了具体预言。

Abstract: We present the first systematic calculations of $φ$-, $η_c$-, and $J/ψ$-nucleus ground states up to mass number $A{=}12$ based on the HAL QCD meson-nucleon potentials at near-physical point. The $(A{+}1)$-body Schrödinger equation is solved with a neural-network variational Monte Carlo framework, generalized to incorporate mesonic degrees of freedom. Benchmarking on light nuclei from $^2$H to $^{12}$C yields ground-state energies consistent with experiment. Meson-nucleus bound states emerge at $A\ge2$ for $φ$, $A\ge4$ for $J/ψ$, and $A\ge6$ for $η_c$. The $φ$-nucleus systems exhibit the strongest binding, with binding energies reaching tens of MeV. The $J/ψ$-nucleus and $η_c$-nucleus systems are weakly bound at the few-MeV and sub-MeV scale, respectively. The binding energy per nucleon deepens nearly linearly with $A$ for charmonium systems, whereas the $φ$-nucleus system exhibits a non-monotonic behavior peaking at $^4$He -- a distinctive hallmark of the short-range and strongly attractive $φN$ interaction. The meson compresses the nucleon distribution relative to the parent nucleus, and evolves from a halo configuration to one embedded inside the nucleus with increasing $A$. Our results provide predictions for future experimental searches, and establish a quantitative bridge between lattice QCD meson-nucleon interactions and the emergent many-body phenomena in meson-nucleus bound states.

### [29] [Fluctuations and correlations of conserved charges in the Polyakov chiral SU(3) quark mean field model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09310)
*Dhananjay Singh,Arvind Kumar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper computes high-order generalized susceptibilities of conserved charges (baryon number $B$, electric charge $Q$, strangeness $S$) in the Polyakov chiral SU(3) quark mean field (PCQMF) model, both at zero and finite baryon chemical potential $μ_B$, with and without the fermion vacuum term (vac=1 vs. vac=0). It analyzes chiral and deconfinement crossover features via these susceptibilities and their ratios, revealing differences between the two model variants in critical behavior and temperature scales.
\n\n
本文计算了Polyakov手征SU(3)夸克平均场（PCQMF）模型中守恒荷（重子数$B$、电荷$Q$、奇异数$S$）的高阶广义susceptibilities，在零和有限重子化学势$μ_B$下分别考虑包含和不包含费米真空项（vac=1 与 vac=0）的情形。通过这些susceptibilities及其比值分析手征和退禁闭交叉特征，揭示了两种模型变体在临界行为和温度标度上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding the QCD phase structure near the chiral crossover and its relation to deconfinement requires precise knowledge of fluctuations of conserved charges, encoded in generalized susceptibilities. The PCQMF model provides a framework incorporating both chiral symmetry breaking and Polyakov-loop dynamics. Including or excluding the fermion vacuum term (Dirac sea) leads to distinct thermodynamic behaviors, and comparing them helps assess the role of quantum fluctuations in modeling the QCD transition region.
\n\n
理解QCD相结构在手征交叉附近及其与退禁闭的关系，需要精确掌握守恒荷涨落的信息，这些信息编码在广义susceptibilities中。PCQMF模型提供了一个同时包含手征对称性破缺和Polyakov圈动力学的框架。包含或排除费米真空项（Dirac海）会导致不同的热力学行为，比较二者有助于评估量子涨落在建模QCD相变区域中的作用。

Method: The authors perform thermodynamic calculations within the Polyakov chiral SU(3) quark mean field (PCQMF) model, including the fermion vacuum contribution (vac=1) and an independently refitted no-sea version (vac=0). They compute diagonal and off-diagonal generalized susceptibilities $χ_n^{B,Q,S}$ up to eighth order at $μ_B = 0$, and extend to finite $μ_B$ (with $μ_Q = μ_S = 0$) for various orders. Susceptibilities are obtained as derivatives of the pressure with respect to chemical potentials: $χ_{ijk}^{BQS} = \partial^{i+j+k} (P/T^4) / \partial(μ_B/T)^i \partial(μ_Q/T)^j \partial(μ_S/T)^k$. They analyze pseudocritical temperatures from chiral condensates and Polyakov loop, and study higher-order derivative structures and kurtosis-like ratios such as $R_{42}^B = χ_4^B/χ_2^B$.
\n\n
作者在Polyakov手征SU(3)夸克平均场（PCQMF）模型内进行热力学计算，包括费米真空贡献（vac=1）和一个独立重拟合的无海版本（vac=0）。他们在$μ_B = 0$时计算至八阶的对角和非对角广义susceptibilities $χ_n^{B,Q,S}$，并在有限$μ_B$（设$μ_Q = μ_S = 0$）下扩展至多种阶数。Susceptibilities通过压强对化学势的导数获得：$χ_{ijk}^{BQS} = \\partial^{i+j+k} (P/T^4) / \\partial(μ_B/T)^i \\partial(μ_Q/T)^j \\partial(μ_S/T)^k$。他们从手征凝聚和Polyakov圈分析赝临界温度，并研究高阶导数结构及类似峰度的比值如$R_{42}^B = χ_4^B/χ_2^B$。

Result: At $μ_B = 0$, the chiral pseudocritical temperature $T_{\\mathrm{pc}}$ is higher in vac=1 (170.5 MeV) than vac=0 (166.4 MeV), while the deconfinement temperature $T_{\\mathrm{dec}}$ is slightly lower in vac=1 (144.4 MeV vs. 146.6 MeV). In vac=1, the subtracted chiral condensate derivative shows an inflection near $T_{\\mathrm{dec}}$, suggesting interplay between chiral and deconfinement transitions. Higher-order susceptibilities exhibit twin peaks/minima (e.g., in $χ_4^B$, $χ_6^Q$, $χ_8^B$) resolving the splitting between $T_{\\mathrm{pc}}$ and $T_{\\mathrm{dec}}$. Off-diagonal correlators show vac=1 dominance in BQ channel across crossover, while BS/QS peak near strange-melting temperature with vac=0 larger. Along the crossover line $T_{\\mathrm{pc}}(μ_B)$, $R_{42}^B$ crosses zero at $μ_B/T_{\\mathrm{pc}} \\approx 2.15$ only in vac=1; vac=0 remains positive. Higher ratios $R_{51}^B$ and $R_{62}^B$ are negative and decrease further with $μ_B$ in vac=1.

在PCQMF模型中包含费米真空项显著改变了QCD交叉区域附近的热力学响应，通过高阶susceptibilities增强了对手征与退禁闭转变分离的敏感性。vac=1中$R_{42}^B$的符号变化暗示可能接近临界点或存在无海变体所缺乏的非平凡涨落结构。这些结果强调了在旨在定量描述QCD热力学的有效模型中，量子真空贡献的重要性。

Abstract: We compute generalized susceptibilities of conserved charges in the Polyakov chiral SU(3) quark mean field (PCQMF) model with the fermion vacuum term. Extending to finite $μ_B$ at $μ_Q = μ_S = 0$, we compute $χ_n^B$ through eighth order, $χ_n^{Q,S}$ through fourth order, the second-order off-diagonals, all twelve fourth-order off-diagonal correlators, and the odd-order baryon susceptibilities $χ_1^B$, $χ_3^B$, $χ_5^B$. Higher derivative orders resolve the chiral-deconfinement splitting as twin maxima in $χ_4^B$ and $χ_6^Q$, twin minima in $χ_8^B$ and $χ_8^Q$, and multiple zero crossings in $χ_6^B$. Among the fourth-order off-diagonal correlators, vac=1 amplitudes exceed vac=0 in the BQ channel across the chiral crossover. The BS, QS, and mixed BQS components peak near the strange-melting temperature, where vac=0 dominates. Conclusion: The study demonstrates that a symmetry-preserving DSE–Faddeev approach with a simple contact interaction can successfully describe the complex electromagnetic structure of the $Ω$ baryon. Abstract: We present predictions for the elastic electromagnetic form factors of the $Ω$ baryon in both space-like and time-like regions, computed within a confining, symmetry-preserving framework based on a vector$\,\otimes\,$vector contact interaction. The calculation is performed in the rainbow-ladder truncation of QCD's Dyson-Schwinger equations, combined with a Poincaré-covariant Faddeev equation for the three-quark bound state. The $Ω$ baryon, composed solely of strange quarks, provides a particularly clean environment in which to investigate the role of quark mass and SU(3)-flavor symmetry in shaping baryon structure. Within this approach, the electromagnetic current is constructed consistently with the Ward-Takahashi identity, yielding four independent form factors associated with the electric monopole, magnetic dipole, electric quadrupole, and magnetic octupole moments. We compute these form factors over a broad kinematic domain and analyze their behavior in both space-like and time-like regions. The resulting static electromagnetic moments and multipole form factors of the $Ω$ baryon exhibit a visible sensitivity to the dressed-quark anomalous magnetic moment, particularly in the magnetic and higher-order multipole sectors. The resonance line shape is then evaluated as a function of laser intensity and polarization, incorporating the modified kinematics and spinor structure into the matrix element squared and differential cross section.

强经典电磁场可通过修改对撞运动学并开启新的光子介导路径，从根本上改变电弱共振动力学。这使得对 $Z$ 玻色子产生截面大小及其手征结构的动态调控成为可能，从而在强场QED与高能对撞机唯象学之间建立起新颖联系，对未来激光辅助对撞机或极端场环境下的精密检验具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Resonant $Z$-boson production is a cornerstone of precision electroweak physics, with its vacuum line shape set by the $Z$ mass, width, and collision kinematics. By treating the field nonperturbatively, we find that laser dressing of the incoming fermions alters the effective collision kinematics and opens laser-photon exchange channels, including multiphoton processes, in $e^{+}e^{-}$ collisions. Additionally, the polarization composition of the produced $Z$ bosons is redistributed. In particular, at high intensities the laser-induced contribution can compensate the intrinsic chiral asymmetry of the electroweak interaction, leading to nearly parity-balanced $Z$-boson production. Abstract: Inspired by the $P$-wave molecular interpretation of the recently observed vector state $G(3900)$, we analyze the production of its possible pseudoscalar partner, denoted here as $G_0(3900)$, via the radiative decay $Y(4230) \to γG_0(3900)$. Although not yet experimentally established, such a structure is expected to appear near the $D\bar{D}^*$ threshold and to exhibit characteristic production patterns. The decay is assumed to proceed through a triangle mechanism. Depending on the model parameters and the binding energy of $G_0(3900)$, the resulting branching fraction lies in the range $\mathcal{B}(Y(4230) \to γG_0(3900)) = 3.8 \times 10^{-5} - 3.3 \times 10^{-4}$. Our results offer a pathway to search for signatures of $G_0(3900)$ in radiative channels and also provide a test of the consistency of loop-mediated radiative decays with a molecular description of the $Y(4230)$.

研究发现PBH平均自旋参数为$\\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$，表明形成时近乎无自转。出现了一个依赖环境的坍缩阈值$\\delta_c$，其值随由相变和再热决定的状态方程历史而变化。关键的是，PBH坍缩分数$\\beta$对再热效率$\\epsilon$极度敏感：$\\epsilon$发生量级为1的变化即可导致$\\beta$变化多个数量级。作者在$(T_*, \\epsilon)$参数空间中（$T_*$为逾渗温度）识别出PBH可解释全部观测暗物质密度的可行区域，并利用微引力透镜、CMB及引力波未探测等当前观测限制对这些区域进行了检验。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that slow first-order phase transitions with delayed reheating provide a viable and highly tunable mechanism for PBH production. The strong dependence on reheating efficiency offers a new handle for model discrimination, while the predicted low initial spins and non-Gaussian statistics distinguish this scenario from inflationary PBH formation mechanisms. Abstract: We study the possibility of significant PBH production from a slow first-order phase transition with delayed reheating. Since delayed reheating results in an early matter-dominated phase between percolation and reheating, we developed a peak-theoretic approach to PBH formation during this phase based on the non-Gaussian distribution of overdensity arising from the transition. To obtain the collapse probability, we performed large-scale Monte Carlo simulations and employed the hoop-conjecture criterion. We include tidal-torque terms to investigate the initial spin of the PBHs and find that the average spin parameter is $\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$. Furthermore, we obtain an emergent overdensity threshold for collapse that depends on the phase transition properties and reheating efficiency. We find that the resulting PBH abundance is extremely sensitive to the reheating efficiency, with order-unity changes in efficiency leading to variations of many orders of magnitude in the collapse fraction. We identify regions of parameter space where the resulting PBHs can account for the entirety of the dark matter abundance. We present NNLO QCD predictions based, for the first time, on a direct computation of the required two-loop amplitudes in the generalised leading-colour limit.

计算得到的 $ω_{\mathrm{EdH}}$ 随固有时 $τ$ 单调增加（源于自旋取向的累积效应），但随温度 $T$ 升高而减小（因热扰动破坏自旋有序）。在QCD交叉温度 $T_c \\sim 150–160\\,\\mathrm{MeV}$ 附近，$ω_{\mathrm{EdH}}$ 达到具有现象学意义的量级。关键的是，作者发现了一个非平凡的交叉温度 $T^*$：低于 $T^*$ 时，转动由自旋驱动（强场区）；高于 $T^*$ 时，转动受惯性限制（弱场区）。这反映了自旋取向带来的磁能增益与整体转动所需动能代价之间的竞争。

Abstract: Using the finite-temperature heat kernel method, we compute the gauge-invariant effective Lagrangian up to dimension-six for massive hot scalar QED. We propose two complementary methods: integrating out heavy modes at finite temperature, and deriving the finite-temperature heat kernel coefficients from the zero-temperature ones. We show that in the static limit, both lead to the same three-dimensional effective operators. We also compute the gauge-invariant Coleman-Weinberg effective potential for a constant background at finite temperature. We further examine how the Polyakov loop modifies the matching coefficients and assess its impact together with the higher-dimensional operators on the thermodynamics of cosmological first-order phase transitions, which in turn can affect an associated gravitational-wave spectrum.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lattice QCD simulations can compute bulk thermodynamic quantities like pressure and its derivatives (susceptibilities) as functions of temperature and baryonic chemical potential, but they cannot directly decompose these observables into contributions from individual hadronic species. This motivates the search for observables that project onto specific hadronic content.

Method: The authors exploit the structure of the QCD partition function expanded in terms of conserved charges—baryon number $B$, electric charge $Q$, and strangeness $S$—and their associated chemical potentials. By considering the Hadron Resonance Gas (HRG) model as a sum over non-interacting hadron and resonance contributions, each weighted by fugacities $e^{(\mu_B B + \mu_Q Q + \mu_S S)/T}$, they derive linear combinations of second- and fourth-order diagonal and off-diagonal susceptibilities that selectively cancel all contributions except those from hadrons with a given $(B, Q, S)$. These combinations are shown to exactly reproduce the expected single-species contributions within the ideal HRG model. When applied to continuum-extrapolated lattice QCD susceptibility data, the results are consistent with HRG predictions at low temperatures, validating both the method and the HRG description in that regime.

本文明确构造了能分离特定量子数 $(B, Q, S)$ 强子贡献的susceptibility线性组合。这些组合在理想HRG模型中被证明能精确复现单粒子物种的预期贡献。当应用于连续极限下的格点QCD susceptibility数据时，结果在低温下与HRG预言一致，从而验证了该方法及HRG模型在此区域的有效性。

Conclusion: The proposed linear combinations of QCD susceptibilities provide a powerful tool to disentangle the hadronic content of the QCD equation of state from lattice data. Their agreement with HRG expectations at low temperatures confirms the dominance of non-interacting hadronic degrees of freedom in this regime and offers a systematic way to test deviations from the HRG picture as temperature increases toward the crossover region.

Abstract: Lattice simulations provide the thermodynamics of quantum chromodynamics (QCD) as a function of the temperature, at zero-to-moderate values of the baryonic chemical potential. However, the contribution of single hadronic species cannot be directly isolated from lattice calculations. In this work, we find linear combinations of up to fourth order susceptibilities which isolate the contribution of hadrons to the QCD pressure according to their baryon number $B$, electric charge $Q$ and strangeness $S$ content. These combinations are valid, provided that the thermodynamics of a strongly-interacting gas in the low-temperature regime can be modeled as a gas of non-interacting hadrons and their resonances. Finally, we test the validity of these linear combinations in the Hadron Resonance Gas (HRG) model and compare them to available lattice QCD results, using continuum-estimated susceptibilities.

  <summary>Details</summary>

动机在于严格纳入强相互作用动力学——特别是宽共振态$f_0(500)$和窄共振态$f_0(980)$——以描述辐射型$J/ψ$衰变中低能$π^0π^0$的产生过程，同时满足解析性、幺正性和交叉对称性。这使得能够模型无关地提取振幅，作为计算引力形状因子等基本量中强子贡献的输入。

Method: The authors use a dispersive framework based on the Muskhelishvili-Omnès (MO) equations: a coupled-channel $ππ/K\bar K$ MO representation for the isoscalar $S$-wave ($0^{++}$) to account for $f_0(500)$ and $f_0(980)$ dynamics, and a single-channel $ππ$ MO representation for the $D$-wave ($2^{++}$). Helicity amplitudes are constructed with all kinematic constraints enforced before conversion to experimental multipoles ($E1$, $M2$, $E3$). Short-distance production is parameterized by subtraction polynomials, and left-hand-cut effects are estimated perturbatively. The $E1$ phase ambiguity is resolved using the modulo-$π$ freedom inherent in production amplitudes, and the overall normalization is fixed by the measured branching fraction.
\n\n
作者采用基于Muskhelishvili-Omnès（MO）方程的色散框架：对同位标量$S$波（$0^{++}$）使用耦合道$ππ/K\bar K$ MO表示以描述$f_0(500)$和$f_0(980)$的动力学，对$D$波（$2^{++}$）则采用单道$ππ$ MO表示。在转换为实验多极矩（$E1$、$M2$、$E3$）前，严格施加螺旋度振幅的所有运动学约束。短程产生过程用减法多项式参数化，左割线效应通过微扰估计。利用产生振幅固有的模-$π$自由度解决$E1$相位模糊问题，并通过测量的分支比固定整体归一化。

Result: The analysis identifies the negative solution of the BESIII $0^{++}-2^{++}$ $E1$ phase difference as the one compatible with unitarity, demonstrating that the observed phase motion can be explained by the Omnès phase without invoking large additional production phases. The absolute scale of the amplitudes is fixed using the extracted branching fraction $\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\toγπ^0π^0)$, yielding fully normalized $S$- and $D$-wave amplitudes that respect analyticity and unitarity.

分析确定BESIII测得的$0^{++}-2^{++}$ $E1$相位差的负解与幺正性兼容，表明观测到的相位演化可由Omnès相位解释，无需引入大的额外产生相位。利用提取的分支比$\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\toγπ^0π^0)$固定振幅的绝对尺度，得到完全归一化的$S$波和$D$波振幅，这些振幅满足解析性和幺正性。

Conclusion: The dispersive amplitudes derived are consistent with fundamental principles of S-matrix theory and provide a robust, model-independent foundation for future calculations of two-pion intermediate states in hadronic matrix elements, particularly for gravitational form factors where precise low-energy ππ dynamics are essential.

Abstract: We present a dispersive amplitude analysis of the low-energy $π^0π^0$ system in the radiative decay $J/ψ\toγπ^0π^0$, using the mass-independent BESIII $0^{++}$ and $2^{++}$ intensities, the total spectrum, and the measured $0^{++}-2^{++}$ $E1$ phase difference. The isoscalar $S$-wave is described by a coupled-channel $ππ/K\bar K$ Muskhelishvili-Omnès representation, which implements the strong final-state interactions associated with the $f_0(500)$ and $f_0(980)$. The $D$-wave is treated with a single-channel $ππ$ Muskhelishvili-Omnès representation, where we identify all kinematic constraints of helicity amplitudes before transforming them to the experimental $E1$, $M2$, and $E3$ multipoles. Smooth short-distance production of a pseudoscalar-meson pair is encoded in subtraction polynomials, while left-hand-cut effects are estimated and found to be numerically subleading. Abstract: We provide a complete classification of twisting supercharges for type IIA supergravity in a flat background by determining the orbits of square-zero elements in the (1,1) super Poincaré algebra in ten dimensions under the action of the Spin group. Abstract: Wilson lines are fundamental probes of gauge theories. We show that, in sufficiently rich representations, they support a large new class of operator insertions. By analyzing the associated beta functions, and relating them to specific OPE coefficients, we show that the deformations are marginally relevant. We support our finding with a weak-coupling computation of the four-point function of these operators for any gauge group and representation.

*Astrid Eichhorn,Zois Gyftopoulos,Aaron Held*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper reinterprets Renormalization Group (RG) flow as a boundary-value problem rather than the standard initial-value problem, revealing that such formulations can lead to non-unique solutions when the Jacobian of beta functions has complex eigenvalues. A diagnostic criterion for non-uniqueness in multi-coupling systems is proposed, with applications to the Standard Model and asymptotically safe quantum gravity.
\n\n
本文将重整化群（Renormalization Group, RG）流重新解释为边值问题而非标准的初值问题，揭示了当 beta 函数的 Jacobian 矩阵具有复本征值时，此类表述可能导致解的非唯一性。作者提出了一个多耦合系统中非唯一性的诊断判据，并将其应用于 Standard Model 和渐近安全量子引力的 Einstein-Hilbert 截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard treatments of the Renormalization Group formulate the flow as an initial-value problem, where all couplings are specified at a single scale. However, physical situations—such as matching conditions across scales or constraints from different observational regimes—naturally impose conditions at multiple scales, motivating a boundary-value formulation of RG flow.

重整化群的标准处理方法将流方程表述为初值问题，即所有耦合常数在单一能标上给定。然而，物理情境——例如跨能标的匹配条件或来自不同观测区域的约束——自然地在多个能标上施加条件，这促使作者将 RG 流重新表述为边值问题。

Method: The authors analyze the structure of Renormalization Group equations as boundary-value problems by examining the properties of the Jacobian matrix $J_{ij} = \\partial \\beta_i / \\partial g_j$, where $\\beta_i$ are beta functions and $g_j$ are couplings. They show that if $J$ possesses complex eigenvalues along the flow trajectory, the boundary-value problem may admit multiple solutions or none. A diagnostic based on the spectrum of $J$ is developed to detect such non-uniqueness in systems with many couplings.
\n\n
作者通过分析 Jacobian 矩阵 $J_{ij} = \\partial \\beta_i / \\partial g_j$（其中 $\\beta_i$ 为 beta 函数，$g_j$ 为耦合常数）的性质，研究了将重整化群方程作为边值问题的结构。他们证明，若 $J$ 沿流轨迹具有复本征值，则边值问题可能具有多重解或无解。基于 $J$ 的谱，作者发展了一种诊断工具，用于探测多耦合系统中的此类非唯一性。

Result: The main result is that boundary-value problems in RG flows are not guaranteed to have unique solutions, unlike initial-value problems. Specifically, non-uniqueness arises when the linearized flow (governed by the Jacobian of beta functions) exhibits oscillatory behavior due to complex eigenvalues. The paper provides a concrete diagnostic: if the Jacobian has complex eigenvalues between boundary scales, the solution may be non-unique. Two physics examples—the Higgs self-coupling and top Yukawa coupling in the Standard Model, and the Newton and cosmological constants in the Einstein-Hilbert truncation of asymptotically safe gravity—are discussed as potential cases where this phenomenon could occur.

主要结果是：与初值问题不同，RG 流中的边值问题无法保证解的唯一性。具体而言，当线性化流（由 beta 函数的 Jacobian 控制）因复本征值而表现出振荡行为时，就会出现非唯一性。本文提供了一个明确的诊断标准：若 Jacobian 在边界能标之间具有复本征值，则解可能非唯一。文章讨论了两个物理实例——Standard Model 中的 Higgs 自耦合与顶夸克 Yukawa 耦合，以及渐近安全引力 Einstein-Hilbert 截断中的 Newton 常数与宇宙学常数——作为该现象可能出现的潜在案例。

Conclusion: Reformulating RG flow as a boundary-value problem reveals new mathematical structures and potential ambiguities in physical predictions. The presence of complex eigenvalues in the stability matrix signals possible non-uniqueness, which must be accounted for in scenarios where boundary conditions are imposed at disparate scales. This insight impacts both particle physics and quantum gravity, particularly in contexts like asymptotic safety or high-scale matching in the Standard Model.
\n\n
将 RG 流重新表述为边值问题揭示了新的数学结构以及物理预言中潜在的模糊性。稳定性矩阵中复本征值的存在预示着可能的非唯一性，在边界条件被施加于不同能标的情形下必须加以考虑。这一见解对粒子物理和量子引力均有影响，特别是在渐近安全（asymptotic safety）或 Standard Model 中高能标匹配等情境中。

Abstract: The Renormalization Group flow connects microscopic to macroscopic descriptions of a system and is therefore typically considered as an initial-value problem. Motivated by situations in which different couplings within a system of Renormalization Group equations are constrained at different scales, we instead consider boundary-value problems in Renormalization Group flows. We find that, unlike initial-value problems which provide $n$ conditions for $n$ couplings, boundary-value problems which provide $n$ conditions for $n$ couplings do not always have a unique solution. When the Jacobian matrix, i.e., the matrix of first derivatives of beta functions, has complex eigenvalues, boundary-value problems may be non-unique. We provide a diagnostic tool for non-uniqueness in systems with many couplings. We also provide two examples with potential relevance for physics, namely within the Standard Model as well as within the Einstein-Hilbert truncation of asymptotically safe quantum gravity.

The geometric embedding into $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ and the proposed inverse Pohlmeyer reduction suggest deep ties to integrable systems and holography, opening new avenues for understanding strongly correlated fermions through geometric and topological methods.
\n\n
本工作提供了一个统一的非微扰框架，将 $(1+1)$ 维量子场论中的 spin-charge separation、规范 dressing 和几何结构联系起来。通过识别 emergent $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})$ 规范对称性，并将相变解释为禁闭/退禁闭现象，架起了凝聚态物理与高能理论之间的桥梁。向 $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ 的几何嵌入及所提出的逆 Pohlmeyer 约化，暗示了与可积系统和全息原理的深刻联系，为通过几何与拓扑方法理解强关联费米子开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Building on a recent derivation of spin-charge separation in $(1+1)$d paired Dirac fermions~\cite{Haddad2024}, we develop a self-consistent dressing $ψ(x) = U(x)χ(x)$ for the full Fierz-complete four-fermion model, extending that result and providing a more detailed resolution of the chiral-difermion phase structure. Our approach shows that the three regimes of our model (chiral, difermion, intermediate), are then tied together by an emergent $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})$ gauge field that binds the spin and charge degrees of freedom. In particular, the chiral-difermion transition is a deconfinement transition for these degrees of freedom, diagnosed by closed boost-sector Wilson loops that develop an area law in the chiral phase for which we compute the associated string tension. This provides a concrete realization of the conjectured Faddeev--Niemi link between spin-charge separation and confinement. We close with a unifying geometric picture in which the order-parameter manifold takes hyperbolic form $ρ^2 - |Δ|^2 = σ^2$, promoted to $\mathrm{AdS}_3 \cong \mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ on inclusion of the charge and difermion phases. The structural matching to the kinematic stage of $\mathrm{AdS}_3/\mathrm{CFT}_2$ is identified explicitly, with the conjecture that the dressed model realizes the inverse Pohlmeyer reduction of the $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ sigma model.

Abstract: We study conserved worldsheet charges associated with spacetime supersymmetry in heterotic pure-spinor superstrings on curved ten-dimensional superspace backgrounds. Using the Doob transform, it derives a universal inward drift for histories conditioned to stay within the EFT regime near hard boundaries, revealing universal boundary layers tied to quantum gravity constraints like species/tower scales or weak-coupling limits—without invoking microscopic forces. 本文将宇宙学中维持有效场论（EFT）控制的问题表述为模场在Swampland边界附近的一个随机生存问题。文章引入由损失面、退化轮廓、有限视界和随机动力学共同决定的生存概率，其对数定义了生存作用量。通过Doob变换，推导出在硬边界附近、被约束保持在EFT区域内的历史所具有的普适向内漂移，揭示了与量子引力约束（如species/tower截断或弱耦合极限）相关的普适边界层——而无需引入微观力。文中还提出一种逆映射方法，用于检验观测到的漂移是否与标量操作性损失面相容，并重构其边界法向Doob等价类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the tension between effective field theory (EFT) descriptions of cosmology and quantum gravity constraints encoded in the Swampland program and compactification data. The paper seeks to understand which cosmological histories can remain within the regime of EFT validity over long times despite stochastic fluctuations of moduli, thereby bridging quantum-gravity-motivated boundaries with observable cosmological dynamics.

动机源于宇宙学中的有效场论（EFT）描述与Swampland纲领及紧致化数据所编码的量子引力约束之间的张力。这些约束指出了场空间中EFT失效的区域（例如，在无限距离极限、弱耦合或轻塔阈值附近）。本文旨在理解：尽管模场存在随机涨落，哪些宇宙学历史仍能在长时间尺度上保持在EFT有效区域内，从而在量子引力驱动的边界与可观测宇宙学动力学之间建立桥梁。

Method: The authors model moduli evolution as a stochastic process governed by a diffusion generator.
