The survival action is $S_{\\text{surv}} = -\\log \\mathcal{P}_{\\text{surv}}$. Applying the Doob $h$-transform, they condition the stochastic ensemble on survival, yielding an effective drift $\\vec{b}_{\\text{cond}} = \\vec{b} + D \\nabla \\log h$, where $h$ solves the backward Kolmogorov equation with absorbing boundary conditions on the loss surface. Near a regular hard boundary with nonzero normal diffusion coefficient $D_\\perp$, they show the conditioned drift develops a universal inward component $\\sim -D_\\perp / \\delta$, where $\\delta$ is the proper distance to the boundary. They also construct an inverse map: given a drift, determine if it arises from conditioning on a scalar loss surface and recover its Doob-normal equivalence class.
\n\n
作者将模场演化建模为由扩散生成元支配的随机过程。他们定义生存概率 $\\mathcal{P}_{\\text{surv}}$，即轨迹在有限宇宙学视界内不穿越“硬损失面”（代表EFT失效）的概率。生存作用量定义为 $S_{\\text{surv}} = -\\log \\mathcal{P}_{\\text{surv}}$。通过应用Doob $h$-变换，他们对随机系综进行生存条件化，得到有效漂移 $\\vec{b}_{\\text{cond}} = \\vec{b} + D \\nabla \\log h$，其中 $h$ 满足带有吸收边界条件的后向Kolmogorov方程。在具有非零法向扩散系数 $D_\\perp$ 的正则硬边界附近，他们证明条件化漂移会产生一个普适的向内分量 $\\sim -D_\\perp / \\delta$，其中 $\\delta$ 是到边界的固有距离。此外，他们构建了一个逆映射：给定一个漂移，判断它是否源于对标量损失面的条件化，并重构其Doob-法向等价类。

Result: The key result is the emergence of a universal stochastic boundary layer near hard EFT breakdown surfaces: surviving cosmological histories exhibit a deterministic inward drift proportional to the normal diffusion coefficient and inversely proportional to the proper distance from the boundary, independent of microscopic details. This mechanism naturally incorporates swampland constraints—such as species scale, Kaluza-Klein/string thresholds, and weak-coupling limits—as effective stochastic boundaries without requiring new fundamental forces. The inverse Doob map provides a diagnostic tool to test whether an observed effective drift in moduli space originates from conditioning on a scalar operational boundary and to reconstruct its geometric class.

核心结果是在硬EFT失效面附近出现了一个普适的随机边界层：能够存活的宇宙学历史表现出一个确定性的向内漂移，该漂移正比于法向扩散系数，反比于到边界的固有距离，且与微观细节无关。这一机制自然地将Swampland约束（如species尺度、Kaluza-Klein/弦阈值和弱耦合极限）纳入为有效的随机边界，而无需引入新的基本力。逆Doob映射提供了一种诊断工具，可用于检验模空间中观测到的有效漂移是否源于对标量操作性边界的条件化，并重构其几何等价类。

Conclusion: The paper establishes a rigorous stochastic framework—grounded in survival analysis and the Doob transform—that connects quantum gravity constraints (from the Swampland and compactification) to the statistical properties of cosmological histories that remain within EFT control. Abstract: Swampland and compactification data tell us where EFT control can fail; stochastic cosmology asks which histories survive near that edge. The Doob transform then converts this logarithmic survival cost into the drift of the ensemble conditioned to remain on the controlled side. Near a regular hard boundary with nonzero normal diffusion, the answer is universal: surviving histories develop an inward wall response fixed only by proper distance and normal diffusion. In this way, tower/species cutoffs, weak-coupling limits, string and Kaluza-Klein thresholds, and potential-based diagnostics acquire stochastic boundary layers without becoming microscopic forces. The construction therefore gives a stochastic survival interface between quantum-gravity control data and the histories that remain on the landscape side of control.

Abstract: In this note we describe a systematic procedure for computing partition functions of noninvertible Rep ($G$) quantum symmetry actions on two-dimensional nonabelian $G$ orbifolds. Abstract: It is a long-standing problem to prove that the number of distinct topological types of Calabi-Yau threefolds is finite. A related proposition, Reid's fantasy, conjectures that all Calabi-Yau threefolds are connected in a single moduli space through extremal transitions. Finiteness of topological types has been proven for the class of elliptic and genus one fibered Calabi-Yau threefolds, which recently have been shown to constitute the vast majority of known Calabi-Yau threefolds; the moduli space of elliptic CY3's is connected.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from a recent conjecture that thermal partition functions of exotic CFTs exhibit universal singular behavior in the semi-universal limit $|ω_i|\rightarrow 1$, characterized by poles in $(1 - |ω_i|)$. Understanding this structure provides insight into non-standard features of these theories—particularly the existence of negative-twist states, which are linked to violations of Averaged Null Energy Condition (ANEC)-type bounds. Traditional high-temperature expansions fail to detect such pathologies, necessitating a refined analysis in this specific kinematic regime.

动机源于一个近期猜想：奇异共形场论（CFT）的热配分函数在半普适极限 $|ω_i|\rightarrow 1$ 下展现出普适的奇异性，表现为 $(1 - |ω_i|)$ 中的极点。理解这一结构有助于揭示这些理论中的非标准特征——尤其是负扭度态的存在，这类态与平均零能量条件（ANEC）型界的破坏相关。传统的高温展开无法探测此类病态行为，因此需要在这一特定运动学区域进行更精细的分析。

Method: The authors compute thermal partition functions on $S^1_β \times S^3$ for various free exotic CFTs: conformal higher-derivative scalars, fermions, vectors, Weyl gravitons, Weyl gravitinos, and general conformal higher-spin (CHS) fields. They employ two complementary techniques: (1) spectral mode-sum methods, which involve summing over eigenvalues of kinetic operators on the sphere, and (2) operator-counting methods based on character formulas of the conformal group. Additionally, they cross-check results for 4D CHS fields by computing the one-loop partition function of massless higher-spin fields in thermal AdS$_5$, leveraging the AdS/CFT correspondence.
\n\n
作者计算了多种自由奇异共形场论在 $S^1_β \times S^3$ 上的热配分函数，包括共形高阶导数标量、费米子、矢量、Weyl 引力子、Weyl 引力微子以及一般的共形高自旋（CHS）场。他们采用两种互补方法：(1) 谱模式求和法（spectral mode-sum），即对球面上动力学算符的本征值求和；(2) 基于共形群特征标的算符计数法（operator-counting）。此外，他们还通过计算热 AdS$_5$ 中无质量高自旋场的单圈配分函数，利用 AdS/CFT 对偶性对四维 CHS 场的结果进行交叉验证。

For four-dimensional CHS fields, the same pole-residue structure is reproduced from the one-loop partition function in thermal AdS$_5$. Crucially, the semi-universal limit is shown to be sensitive to negative-twist states—unlike the standard high-temperature expansion, which yields identical leading behavior regardless of their presence.

在所有考察的情形中，热配分函数均在 $|ω_i| = 1$ 处展现出普适极点（即在 $(1 - |ω_i|)$ 中），与猜想一致。该极点的留数依赖于具体理论，并编码了谱的信息：在存在与不存在负扭度态的理论之间，留数表现出定性差异。对于四维共形高自旋（CHS）场，相同的极点-留数结构也可从热 AdS$_5$ 中的单圈配分函数中复现。关键的是，半普适极限被证明对负扭度态敏感——而标准高温展开无论是否存在此类态，都给出相同的主导行为。

Conclusion: The semi-universal limit $|ω_i|\rightarrow 1$ serves as a powerful diagnostic tool for identifying pathological features—specifically negative-twist states—in exotic CFTs. Abstract: In this paper, we study thermal partition functions of free exotic conformal field theories, focusing on conformal higher-derivative and conformal higher-spin fields, in the semi-universal limit $|ω_i|\rightarrow 1$. It was recently conjectured in \cite{Anand:2025mfh} that, in this limit, the thermal partition function develops universal poles in $(1-|ω_i|)$, while the corresponding residue functions are theory-dependent. We analyze conformal higher-derivative scalar, fermionic, and vector fields in the semi-universal limit. We then extend the study to the Weyl graviton, the Weyl gravitino, and conformal higher-spin fields (CHS) on $S^1_β\times S^3$, using both spectral mode-sum and operator-counting methods. In all cases, we find the expected pole structure, with residue functions whose behavior depends on the presence or absence of negative-twist states. Finally, we show that the semi-universal limit provides a useful diagnostic of negative-twist states, which indicate violations of ANEC-type bounds in these theories, whereas the traditional high-temperature expansion is insensitive to them.

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper establishes an all-orders factorization of scattering amplitudes in planar ${\cal N}=4$ SYM into IR-divergent soft and IR-finite hard parts, showing that the hard part satisfies an uncorrected tree-level soft theorem and realizes the undeformed tree-level $\cal S$-algebra of soft gluons.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In nonabelian gauge theories, scattering amplitudes factorize into soft (IR divergent) and hard (IR finite) components. 在非阿贝尔规范理论中，散射振幅可因子化为软（红外发散）与硬（红外有限）两部分。本文旨在在平面${\cal N}=4$超杨-米尔斯理论（一种极大超对称且可积的理论）中精确地定义这种全阶因子化形式，并研究所得硬振幅的代数与对称性质，特别是其与软定理及由$\cal S$-代数编码的渐近对称性的关系。

Using these, they construct an all-orders definition of the factorization $\mathcal{A}_n = \mathcal{A}^{\rm soft}_n \times \mathcal{A}^{\rm hard}_n$, isolating the IR-finite hard component. They then analyze the behavior of $\mathcal{A}^{\rm hard}_n$ under soft limits and its realization of the $\cal S$-algebra generated by soft gluon currents.

Conclusion: By leveraging the integrability and dualities of planar ${\cal N}=4$ SYM, the work clarifies how infrared factorization can preserve tree-level soft theorems and asymptotic symmetries at the quantum level. Abstract: Scattering amplitudes of $n$ particles in nonabelian gauge theories admit factorizations of the general form $\mathcal{A}_n \;=\; \mathcal{A}^{\rm soft}_n \times \mathcal{A}^{\rm hard}_n$, where $\mathcal{A}^{\rm soft}_n$ is IR divergent, while $\mathcal{A}^{\rm hard}_n$ is IR finite and encodes the higher loop corrections to scattering. We specify a particular all-orders definition of this factorization for planar ${\cal N}=4$ super Yang-Mills (SYM) and argue that the resulting $\mathcal{A}_n^{\rm hard}$ obeys an uncorrected tree-level soft theorem. Moreover it furnishes a representation of the undeformed tree-level $\cal S$-algebra generated by a tower of soft gluons. The results follow from several commonly invoked assumptions for ${\cal N}=4$ SYM, including BDS one-loop exponentiation of the splitting function and amplitude/Wilson-loop duality.

该构造得到了首个具有独立 dilaton 荷 $\mathfrak{q}$ 与 $H$-flux 的解析旋转 NS--NS 真空解，由四个参数 $\{\mathfrak{m},j,\mathfrak{q},ζ\}$ 表征。其静态极限并非球对称，而是轴向 Zipoy–Voorhees 型形变，展现出广义相对论和 Einstein–Maxwell–dilaton–axion 理论中所没有的“几何记忆”。尽管该静态分支与球对称分支共享相同的 $\ell=0$ 阶 PPN 数据 $\{MG, β_{\mathrm{PPN}}, γ_{\mathrm{PPN}}, h\}$，但二者在 $\ell=2$ 阶出现差异，从而解除简并。外视界壳层通常存在奇点；然而当 $|\mathfrak{q}| > \sqrt{\mathfrak{m}^2 - j^2}$ 时，极向测地线被排斥，旋转轴变为曲率正则——尽管此处 $g_{μν}$ 发散而 $g^{μν}$ 保持有限。轴外区域 $g^{μν}$ 本身发散。这种仅在轴上局部的正则性为嵌入 DFT 的非黎曼几何提供了自然场景。

Conclusion: This work provides the first explicit rotating vacuum solution in the pure NS--NS sector with full analytic control over all three fields. The solution’s structure aligns naturally with Double Field Theory, where the fundamental variables are $\mathbf{O}(D,D)$-covariant and do not require a non-degenerate metric $g_{μν}$. This supports the idea that DFT can describe physical spacetimes beyond the reach of conventional differential geometry, especially in regimes where the metric degenerates but T-duality-covariant structures remain well-defined.

本工作提供了首个在纯 NS--NS 扇区中具有完整解析控制的旋转真空解，涵盖全部三个场。它揭示了新颖的几何现象——特别是“几何记忆”以及在奇异时空中的轴向局部正则性——这对黎曼几何范式提出了挑战。该解的结构天然契合 Double Field Theory，其中基本变量是 $\mathbf{O}(D,D)$ 协变的，无需非退化度规 $g_{μν}$。这支持了如下观点：DFT 能够描述超出传统微分几何范畴的物理时空，尤其是在度规退化但 T-对偶协变结构仍良定义的情形下。

Abstract: We construct an exact rotating vacuum of the NS--NS sector, equivalently a Double Field Theory vacuum. To our knowledge, this is the first explicit rotating solution of the pure NS--NS vacuum equations with all three NS--NS fields $\{g,B,φ\}$ determined analytically, independent dilaton and $H$-flux charges, and no Maxwell sector. The static limit is obtained by taking the rotation parameter $j\to 0$. In this limit the geometry is not the spherical Burgess--Myers--Quevedo solution. Instead, it is an axial Zipoy--Voorhees branch carrying $H$-flux, so an oblate deformation remains after rotation is switched off. This geometric memory is absent in pure general relativity and in Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton--axion. The two static branches nevertheless share the same $\ell=0$ parametrized post-Newtonian data $\{MG,β_{\mathrm{PPN}},γ_{\mathrm{PPN}},h\}$. They give two inequivalent NS--NS geometries at identical monopole charges, with the degeneracy lifted at $\ell=2$. At the Kerr horizon locus the outer shell is generically singular in curvature. Above the threshold $|\mathfrak{q}|>\sqrt{\mathfrak{m}^{2}-j^{2}}$, polar geodesics are repelled outward and the rotation axis becomes regular in curvature at the shell. On that axis the inverse metric $g^{μν}$ stays finite while the lower-index Riemannian metric components diverge, whereas off the axis the inverse metric itself diverges. Conclusion: The work successfully unifies Geometric Thermodynamics and Information Geometry through a common Kähler–contact framework and extends Souriau thermodynamics to curved Kähler phase spaces encoded by Calabi–Vesentini manifolds. The explicit computation of partition functions across a broad universality class and the introduction of symmetry-breaking Casimir terms open new avenues for geometric statistical mechanics and its application to structured machine learning models like Cartan Neural Networks.

Abstract: In this paper we clarify the relation between Geometric Thermodynamics and Information Geometry based on the Fisher matrix. On the macroscopic odd-dimensional contact manifold of thermodynamic variables, we introduce for the first time a metric, whose pull-back on the isoentropic symplectic submanifolds transverse to the Reeb field is Kählerian. The pull-back of such metric on equilibrium states, that are lagrangian submanifolds, is the Fisher Hessian. Then we consider the Souriau-like Thermodynamics that uses Calabi-Vesentini (CV) manifolds as Kaehlerian microscopic event manifolds and the Killing moment maps as observable functions. A systematic use of the theory of compact abelian structures and the setup of Special Kähler Geometry in which CV manifolds are encoded allows us to perform the explicit integration defining the partition function for any entry in the CV Tits Satake universality class. The additional actions completing the abelian structure are non linear Casimir functions of the Killing moment-maps and suggest a generalization of Souriau thermodynamics that partially breaks the isometry group symmetry by means of the non vanishing mean values of the Casimir functions in a manner similar to the spontaneous magnetization in ferromagnetism. Our new exact Gibbs distributions provide the analogue for Cartan Neural Networks of the Gaussian probability distributions in flat space used in conventional Machine Learning.

本文通过指出必须一致地包含量子效应（特别是由 Jordan 与 Einstein frame 变换引起的 trace anomaly），解决了文献中关于 $f(R)$ 引力中 scalaron 与光子耦合的分歧。当同时考虑 anomaly-induced 项和微扰圈图贡献时，在 scalaron 质量远小于圈内粒子质量的极限下，二者相互抵消，导致 scalaron 到光子的衰变率被强烈压低。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Discrepancies exist in previous studies of the scalaron--photon coupling in $f(R)$ gravity, with different results depending on whether only the classical trace of the energy--momentum tensor or quantum anomalies are considered. Method: The authors treat the scalaron as part of the Jordan-frame metric and compute its coupling to gauge fields via its interaction with the energy--momentum tensor. Using Fujikawa's method, they derive the anomaly-induced effective interaction due to the non-invariance of the fermionic measure under conformal transformations when changing frames. They first perform the calculation in QED and then extend it to the full Standard Model. They also compare this with direct diagrammatic (perturbative) computations that include the trace anomaly.

作者将 scalaron 视为 Jordan-frame 度规的内禀分量，并通过其与能量–动量张量的耦合计算其与规范场的相互作用。利用 Fujikawa 方法，他们推导出由于在 frame 变换下费米子测度在共形变换下非不变性所导致的 anomaly-induced 有效相互作用。计算首先在 QED 中进行，随后推广至完整 Standard Model。他们还将此结果与包含 trace anomaly 的直接圈图（微扰）计算进行对比。

Result: The anomaly-induced scalaron--photon coupling derived via Fujikawa’s method matches the result from perturbative calculations that include the trace anomaly, but differs from approaches using only the classical trace. Crucially, in the limit $m_{\\text{scalaron}} \\ll m_{\\text{loop particles}}$, the anomaly contribution is exactly canceled by the perturbative loop diagram, yielding a vanishing effective coupling and thus a highly suppressed decay rate $\\Gamma(\\phi \\to \\gamma\\gamma)$.
\n\n
通过 Fujikawa 方法导出的 anomaly-induced scalaron–光子耦合与包含 trace anomaly 的微扰计算结果一致，但不同于仅使用经典迹的方法。关键的是，在 $m_{\\text{scalaron}} \\ll m_{\\text{loop particles}}$ 极限下，anomaly 贡献被微扰圈图精确抵消，导致有效耦合为零，从而使得衰变率 $\\Gamma(\\phi \\to \\gamma\\gamma)$ 被强烈压低。

Conclusion: The apparent contradictions in the literature stem from inconsistent treatments of quantum effects under frame transformations in $f(R)$ gravity. Abstract: We revisit the effective coupling of the scalaron to gauge fields in $f(R)$ gravity minimally coupled to the Standard Model, focusing on the scalaron decay into two photons. Treating the scalaron as an intrinsic component of the Jordan-frame metric, we analyse quantum effects arising from its coupling to the energy--momentum tensor. In this framework, the trace anomaly contributes to the scalaron--gauge boson interaction.
  Using Fujikawa's method, we derive the anomaly-induced contribution, associated with the transformation of matter fields between the Jordan and Einstein frames, first in QED and then in the full Standard Model. The resulting effective scalaron--photon interaction agrees with direct perturbative calculations that include the trace anomaly and differs from the result obtained when only the classical trace of the energy--momentum tensor is taken into account. In the limit where the scalaron mass is much smaller than the masses of particles circulating in the loop, the diagrammatic contribution cancels the anomaly-induced term, leading to a vanishing effective coupling and a strong suppression of the scalaron decay rate into photons.
  These results clarify the origin of discrepancies in the literature concerning the effective scalaron coupling to massless gauge fields. The discrepancies arise from inequivalent prescriptions for implementing quantum loop effects in $f(R)$ gravity, leading to different scalaron--gauge field interactions with direct implications for scalaron dark-matter phenomenology.

### [65] [Negative heat capacities in spherically symmetric sectors of $d$-matrix quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09521)
*Denjoe O'Connor,Sanjaye Ramgoolam*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper studies the $SO(d)$ and $O(d)$ invariant sectors of a bosonic $d$-matrix harmonic oscillator with $U(N)$ gauge symmetry. It derives an exact pairing formula for the micro-canonical degeneracy $\mathcal{Z}(N,d,k)$ using representation theory and harmonic analysis, obtains large-$N$ asymptotics via group integrals, identifies a "caloric fold" in the energy–temperature curve due to finite-$N$ effects (with $k_{\rm crit} \sim N^2/4$), and proposes this matrix model as a solvable framework capturing features of black hole thermodynamics in AdS.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to construct and analyze tractable quantum mechanical matrix models that exhibit thermodynamic behaviors analogous to those of black holes in anti-de Sitter (AdS) space—particularly the non-monotonic caloric curve featuring a negative-to-positive heat capacity transition. The authors focus on $SO(d)$/$O(d)$-invariant sectors of multi-matrix models to leverage symmetry and representation-theoretic tools while maintaining connections to gravitational physics via holography.
\n\n
动机是构建并分析可处理的量子力学矩阵模型，使其展现出类似于反德西特（AdS）空间中黑洞的热力学行为——特别是具有负到正热容转变的非单调热容曲线（caloric curve）。作者聚焦于多矩阵模型的 $SO(d)$/ $O(d)$ 不变扇区，以利用对称性和表示论工具，同时通过全息原理保持与引力物理的联系。

Method: The authors use representation theory of the symmetric group $S_k$ and unitary groups, Schur-Weyl duality, and harmonic analysis on the homogeneous space $U(d)/SO(d)$ to count $SO(d)$/$O(d)$-invariant words built from matrix variables $X^i_{j,a}$. This yields a pairing formula for $\mathcal{Z}(N,d,k)$ between $N$-dependent and $d$-dependent vectors indexed by integer partitions of $k$. For large $N$ and $k \leq N$, they employ $U(N)$ and $SO(d)$ (or $O(d)$) group integrals to derive asymptotic expressions. A matrix model approximation with semi-classical eigenvalue density analysis is used to explain the scaling $k_{\rm crit} \sim N^2/4$. Ribbon graph combinatorics emerges in the joint large $N,d$ limit.
\n\n
作者使用对称群 $S_k$ 与酉群的表示论、Schur-Weyl 对偶性以及齐性空间 $U(d)/SO(d)$ 上的调和分析，来计数由矩阵变量 $X^i_{j,a}$ 构成的 $SO(d)$/ $O(d)$ 不变字（words）。这导出了微正则简并度 $\mathcal{Z}(N,d,k)$ 的一个配对公式：该公式将依赖于 $N$ 和依赖于 $d$ 的向量在整数 $k$ 的分拆空间中配对。对于大 $N$ 且 $k \leq N$ 的情形，他们采用 $U(N)$ 与 $SO(d)$（或 $O(d)$）群积分推导渐近表达式。通过矩阵模型近似结合本征值密度的半经典分析，解释了标度律 $k_{\rm crit} \sim N^2/4$。在联合大 $N,d$ 极限下，带状图（ribbon graph）组合结构显现出来。

Result: The key results include: (1) an exact combinatorial pairing formula for $\mathcal{Z}(N,d,k)$ based on Kronecker coefficients and partition data; (2) analytic large-$N$ expressions showing negative micro-canonical heat capacity that becomes positive beyond a critical excitation level $k_{\rm crit}$; (3) numerical and analytical evidence that $k_{\rm crit} \sim N^2/4$ for small $d$; (4) emergence of ribbon graph combinatorics in the double-scaling limit; and (5) identification of a “caloric fold” structurally similar to that in AdS black hole thermodynamics.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 基于 Kronecker 系数与整数分拆数据的 $\mathcal{Z}(N,d,k)$ 精确组合配对公式；(2) 大 $N$ 解析表达式显示微正则热容为负，并在临界激发能级 $k_{\rm crit}$ 之后变为正；(3) 数值与解析证据表明对小 $d$ 有 $k_{\rm crit} \sim N^2/4$；(4) 在双重缩放极限下出现带状图（ribbon graph）组合结构；(5) 发现一种“热容折叠”（caloric fold），其结构与 AdS 黑洞热力学中的现象相似。

Conclusion: The $SO(d)$/$O(d)$-invariant sectors of $d$-matrix quantum mechanics provide a solvable, symmetry-reduced matrix model that reproduces the hallmark caloric fold of black hole thermodynamics. This suggests these models are promising laboratories for studying quantum aspects of gravity and holography, especially through the interplay of group representation theory, large-$N$ matrix techniques, and combinatorial structures like ribbon graphs.
\n\n
$d$-矩阵量子力学的 $SO(d)$/ $O(d)$ 不变扇区提供了一个可解的、对称性约化的矩阵模型，能够复现黑洞热力学的标志性“热容折叠”。这表明此类模型是研究引力的量子性质及全息原理的有力平台，尤其可通过群表示论、大 $N$ 矩阵方法与带状图等组合结构之间的相互作用进行深入探索。

Abstract: We consider the $SO(d)$ and $O(d)$ invariant sectors of the bosonic $d$-matrix harmonic oscillator with $U(N)$ gauge symmetry. The micro-canonical degeneracy $\mathcal{Z}( N , d , k )$ for fixed energy $k$ is expressed as a pairing between an $N$-dependent vector and a $d$-dependent vector in the space of partitions of the integer $k$. This pairing formula is derived by counting invariant words in multi-matrix variables $X^i_{j,a}$, using properties of Clebsch-Gordan multiplicities (Kronecker coefficients) for the symmetric group $S_k$, Schur-Weyl duality and harmonic analysis on the homogeneous space $U(d)/SO(d)$. Analytic formulae for large $N$ and $k$ with $ k \le N $ are obtained using group integrals over $U(N)$ and $SO(d)$ (or $ O(d)$). The micro-canonical heat capacity in this regime is negative and turns positive, at a critical value $k_{\rm crit}$, due to finite $N$ modifications to the counting, thus forming what we denote as a characteristic caloric fold in the $ E $ versus $T$ curve. Data from the pairing formula is well fitted by $k_{\rm crit} \sim { N^2 \over 4 }$ for small values of $d$. A derivation of this large $N$ formula is given using a matrix model approximation and semi-classical analysis of the eigenvalue density. The large $N,d$ limit of the degeneracies reveals a key role for ribbon graph combinatorics. The caloric fold is also notably a property of black hole thermodynamics in anti-de-Sitter spaces. We propose the spherically symmetric \(SO(d)\) and \(O(d)\) invariant sectors of \(d\)-matrix quantum mechanics as tractable matrix systems for capturing key features of dual descriptions of black-hole thermodynamics.

</details>


### [66] [Double-Current Deformations of Two-Dimensional QFTs with Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09752)
*Zhengyuan Du*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper constructs double-current deformations of 2D quantum field theories (QFTs) with background gauge-field anomalies by coupling to dynamical gauge fields and compact Stueckelberg fields, preserving the original anomaly. For flat backgrounds, the deformation reduces to a finite-dimensional holonomy integral; on the torus, this yields a Gaussian transform of the partition function for the compact boson/$U(1)$ WZW model, mapping momentum $k \to K_λ$ while preserving anomaly-controlled data. Non-Abelian and homogeneous Yang-Baxter generalizations are also proposed.
\n\n
本文构建了具有背景规范场反常的二维量子场论（QFT）的双流形变。通过将原始理论耦合到动力学规范场和紧致Stueckelberg场，并在反常线丛中插入平行输运，所构造的形变配分函数保持与未形变情形相同的反常。对于平坦背景规范场，Stueckelberg场的非零模使动力学规范场局域化为平坦联络，从而将形变简化为有限维的和乐积分；在环面上，该积分核对紧致玻色子/$U(1)$ WZW模型给出配分函数的高斯变换：在零背景时能谱由$k \to K_λ$给出，而接触项和谱流数据仍由原始反常控制。文中还提出了与反兼容的非阿贝尔及齐次Yang-Baxter推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to generalize solvable irrelevant deformations—like $T\bar{T}$—to theories with background gauge-field anomalies, ensuring that the deformed theory retains the same anomaly structure as the seed theory. This addresses the challenge of defining consistent deformations in the presence of global symmetries with 't Hooft anomalies, which constrain renormalization group flows and symmetry realization.
\n\n
动机在于将可解的无关形变（如$T\bar{T}$形变）推广至具有背景规范场反常的理论，并确保形变后的理论保留原始理论的反常结构。这解决了在存在具有’t Hooft反常的整体对称性情形下定义一致形变的难题，因为此类反常会约束重整化群流和对称性的实现方式。

Method: The authors extend the path integral approach of [1] by introducing dynamical gauge fields $A$ and compact Stueckelberg scalar fields $\phi$, coupled to the seed QFT. They insert a parallel transport operator along paths in the space of background gauge fields within the anomaly line bundle. For flat background connections, non-zero modes of $\phi$ localize $A$ to flat connections, reducing the path integral to a finite-dimensional integral over holonomies. The kernel of this integral is explicitly computed on the torus and generalized to higher genus. The compact boson case is solved exactly, yielding a Gaussian integral kernel implementing $k \to K_λ$. The framework is extended to non-Abelian and homogeneous Yang-Baxter deformations compatible with the anomaly.
\n\n
作者通过引入动力学规范场$A$和紧致Stueckelberg标量场$\phi$，并将其与原始QFT耦合，从而扩展了文献[1]中的路径积分方法。他们在反常线丛中沿背景规范场空间中的路径插入平行输运算符。对于平坦背景联络，$\phi$的非零模使$A$局域化为平坦联络，从而将路径积分简化为关于和乐的有限维积分。该积分核在环面上被显式计算，并推广至更高亏格情形。紧致玻色子情形被精确求解，得到一个高斯积分核，实现$k \to K_λ$映射。该框架进一步被推广至与反常兼容的非阿贝尔及齐次Yang-Baxter形变。

Result: For the compact boson (or $U(1)$ WZW model), the double-current deformation yields a torus partition function given by a Gaussian transform: the momentum modes are shifted as $k \to K_λ$, while contact terms and spectral flow data remain governed by the original background gauge anomaly. The deformation preserves the anomaly exactly. The method produces an explicit integral kernel on the torus and its higher-genus analogues. The construction admits consistent non-Abelian and homogeneous Yang-Baxter generalizations that respect the anomaly constraint.
\n\n
对于紧致玻色子（或$U(1)$ WZW模型），双流形变给出的环面配分函数表现为高斯变换：动量模被移动为$k \to K_λ$，而接触项和谱流数据仍由原始背景规范反常控制。该形变精确保持反常不变。该方法给出了环面上显式的积分核及其高亏格推广。该构造允许与反常约束相容的非阿贝尔及齐次Yang-Baxter推广。

Conclusion: The paper establishes a systematic path-integral framework for double-current deformations of 2D QFTs with background gauge anomalies, preserving the anomaly while modifying the spectrum via a controlled integral transform. This extends solvable deformation techniques beyond anomaly-free settings and opens avenues for exploring integrable structures in anomalous theories, including non-Abelian generalizations rooted in Yang-Baxter equations.
\n\n
本文建立了一个系统的路径积分框架，用于处理具有背景规范反常的二维QFT的双流形变，在通过受控积分变换修改能谱的同时精确保持反常。这将可解形变技术从无反常情形拓展至存在反常的情形，并为探索反常理论中的可积结构（包括基于Yang-Baxter方程的非阿贝尔推广）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We construct the double-current deformations of two-dimensional quantum field theories whose partition functions have background gauge-field anomalies. Extending the path integral construction of [1], we couple the seed theory to dynamical gauge fields and compact Stueckelberg fields and insert parallel transport in the anomaly line bundle. The deformed partition function then has the same anomaly as the undeformed one.
  For flat background gauge fields the Stueckelberg non-zero modes localize the dynamical gauge fields to flat connections, reducing the deformation to a finite-dimensional holonomy integral. We derive the integral kernel on the torus and its higher-genus generalization. For the compact boson, or equivalently the Abelian $U(1)$ WZW model, the kernel gives a Gaussian transform of the torus partition function: at zero background the spectrum is obtained by $k\to K_λ$, while contact terms and spectral-flow data remain controlled by the original anomaly. We also formulate the anomaly-compatible non-Abelian and homogeneous Yang-Baxter generalization.

</details>

该方法通过用多变量多重多对数函数（MPLs）及其单值版本修饰平直空间的圆盘（开弦）和球面（闭弦）积分，将其提升到AdS。作者通过对世界面积分进行解析延拓，推导出开弦构建模块的单值性关系——这是对平直空间BCJ关系的推广。对于闭弦，他们建立了KLT型因子化公式，将AdS闭弦振幅表示为开弦模块的双线性组合，类似于平直空间中的KLT关系。

Result: The paper successfully constructs well-defined all-$n$ building blocks for tree-level string amplitudes in AdS, proves monodromy relations for open-string integrals, and derives a KLT factorization for closed-string amplitudes. These results demonstrate that the analytic and algebraic structures of flat-space string amplitudes—such as color-kinematics duality and double-copy—admit natural generalizations to AdS via appropriate transcendental dressing.

本文成功构建了AdS中树图级弦振幅的良定义全$n$点构建模块，证明了开弦积分的单值性关系，并推导出闭弦振幅的KLT因子化。这些结果表明，平直空间弦振幅的解析与代数结构（如色-动量对偶和双拷贝）可通过适当的超越函数修饰，在AdS中得到自然推广。

Conclusion: This work provides a foundational step toward a complete Cosmological Bootstrap or AdS/CFT-based understanding of string scattering in curved spacetime by extending flat-space amplitude techniques to general multiplicity in AdS. The use of multivariable MPLs and single-valued analogues offers a promising pathway to incorporate higher-loop and quantum corrections in future studies.

Abstract: We propose and study all-multiplicity building blocks for tree-level string amplitudes in AdS. These are worldsheet integrals obtained by dressing the corresponding flat-space disc and sphere integrals with multivariable multiple polylogarithms and their single-valued analogues, respectively. We derive monodromy relations for the open-string building blocks and a KLT factorisation for their closed-string counterparts. This extends the non-commutative AdS uplift of lower-point flat-space structures to general $n$-point kinematics.

TL;DR: This paper constructs a unified formalism to analyze how topological defects—such as cosmic strings and global monopoles—affect the local quantum behavior of a scalar field in warped spacetime geometries.   <summary>Details</summary>

动机源于需要理解由拓扑缺陷（如宇宙弦或全局单极子）引起的非平凡时空拓扑结构与弯曲几何共同如何影响量子场涨落。这类构型出现在宇宙学和高能物理中（例如膜世界场景），但此前缺乏对具有任意曲率耦合参数 $\\xi$ 的量子场在此类背景下的系统性处理。

They then separate the Klein-Gordon equation for a scalar field with general curvature coupling $\\xi R \\phi^2$ into radial, angular, and warp-coordinate ordinary differential equations. Explicit expressions for the Hadamard two-point function are obtained for a global monopole in AdS space, revealing how both warping and the monopole’s solid angle deficit modify vacuum fluctuations.

本文成功推导出在包含拓扑缺陷的弯曲时空中量子标量场的一组完备归一化模函数，适用于任意曲率耦合参数 $\\xi$ 和一般角亏参数 $\\alpha$。针对AdS空间中的全局单极子，获得了Hadamard两点函数的显式表达式，揭示了时空弯曲和单极子立体角亏如何共同修正真空涨落。

Conclusion: The developed framework provides a powerful and general method to study quantum field theory in curved spacetimes with nontrivial topology and warping. It enables precise computation of local observables like the Hadamard function, which encodes information about vacuum polarization and particle production near topological defects in braneworld or AdS/CFT-inspired geometries.

所发展的框架为研究具有非平凡拓扑和弯曲结构的弯曲时空中的量子场论提供了一种强大而普适的方法。该方法能够精确计算Hadamard函数等局域可观测量，后者编码了膜世界或AdS/CFT启发几何中拓扑缺陷附近真空极化和粒子产生的信息。

Abstract: We develop a general framework for investigating the influence of topological defects on the local characteristics of a quantum scalar field in a warped geometry background. The Ricci tensor and curvature scalar are decomposed into contributions from the warp factor, the radial geometry and the angular defect structure. For an arbitrary curvature coupling parameter, the field equation is separated into independent radial, angular and warp-coordinate parts. A complete set of normalized mode functions is obtained for general values of the angular deficit parameters. The general formalism is applied to several specific cases, such as conformally flat warped spacetimes, generalized cosmic strings, global monopoles and anti-de Sitter (AdS)-type warped geometries. The Hadamard two-point function is then evaluated for a global monopole in AdS spacetime using the obtained mode functions.

### [69] [Post-Newtonian analysis of the quantum signatures of gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09119)
*Tuhin Chatterjee,Soham Sen,Sunandan Gangopadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper extends a previous quantum information–based proposal for detecting quantum gravity signatures by incorporating leading-order post-Newtonian corrections. Using a Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) in a harmonic trap as a detector, the authors show that non-Gaussianity in the system’s state arises exclusively from quantum gravitational interactions—enabled by non-quadratic terms in the Hamiltonian—which cannot be mimicked by classical gravity. Post-Newtonian corrections slightly suppress the signal-to-noise ratio but do not eliminate the signature.

本文在先前基于量子信息理论探测量子引力信号的工作基础上，引入了领头阶后牛顿（post-Newtonian）修正。作者使用谐振势阱中的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate, BEC）作为探测器，表明系统态中的非高斯性（non-Gaussianity）仅能由量子引力相互作用产生——这源于哈密顿量中的非二次项，而经典引力无法复现这一行为。后牛顿修正略微抑制了信噪比，但并未消除该信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to distinguish genuine quantum gravitational effects from classical or other quantum field theoretic backgrounds. Previous work showed that only a quantum model of gravity introduces non-Gaussian features via non-quadratic operators in the interaction Hamiltonian. The current study aims to assess the robustness of this signature under more realistic conditions by including leading-order post-Newtonian corrections, which account for relativistic effects beyond Newtonian gravity.
\n\n
动机源于需要将真实的量子引力效应与经典或其他量子场论背景区分开来。先前工作表明，只有引力的量子模型通过相互作用哈密顿量中的非二次算符引入非高斯特征。本研究旨在通过引入领头阶后牛顿修正（即超越牛顿引力的相对论效应），评估该信号在更现实条件下的鲁棒性。

Method: The authors employ a quantum information–theoretic framework where a Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) confined in a harmonic trap serves as a probe for gravitational non-Gaussianity. They model gravity as either classical or quantum and compute the resulting evolution of the BEC’s quantum state. Crucially, Feshbach resonances allow tuning electromagnetic interactions to zero, isolating gravitational effects. The analysis incorporates leading-order post-Newtonian corrections into the gravitational potential, modifying the interaction Hamiltonian to $H_{\text{int}} \sim \Phi_{\text{PN}}(\mathbf{x}_i, \mathbf{x}_j)$, where $\Phi_{\text{PN}}$ includes $1/c^2$ relativistic corrections. Non-Gaussianity is diagnosed via higher-order cumulants or Wigner function negativity.
\n\n
作者采用量子信息理论框架，将谐振势阱中的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（BEC）作为引力非高斯性的探针。他们将引力建模为经典或量子形式，并计算BEC量子态的演化。关键在于利用Feshbach共振将电磁相互作用调至零，从而隔离引力效应。分析中将领头阶后牛顿修正纳入引力势，修改相互作用哈密顿量为 $H_{\\text{int}} \\sim \\Phi_{\\text{PN}}(\\mathbf{x}_i, \\mathbf{x}_j)$，其中 $\\Phi_{\\text{PN}}$ 包含 $1/c^2$ 阶相对论修正。非高斯性通过高阶累积量或Wigner函数负值进行诊断。

Result: The inclusion of post-Newtonian corrections leads to a slight damping of the signal-to-noise ratio for detecting non-Gaussianity, but the qualitative signature remains intact: only quantum gravity generates non-Gaussian features due to non-quadratic terms in the Hamiltonian. 引入后牛顿修正导致探测非高斯性的信噪比略有衰减，但定性信号依然存在：只有量子引力因哈密顿量中的非二次项产生非高斯特征。即使包含后牛顿修正，经典引力最多只产生高斯动力学。由于可通过Feshbach共振独立抑制电磁相互作用，BEC仍是一个可行的探测器，确保观测到的任何非高斯性可唯一归因于量子引力。

Conclusion: Quantum gravity leaves a distinct non-Gaussian imprint on matter systems that cannot be replicated by classical gravity, even when relativistic (post-Newtonian) corrections are included. The proposed BEC-based detection scheme remains robust under these more realistic conditions, albeit with modestly reduced sensitivity. This strengthens the case for using quantum information–inspired tabletop experiments to probe quantum aspects of gravity.

量子引力在物质系统上留下独特的非高斯印记，即使包含相对论性（后牛顿）修正，经典引力也无法复现。所提出的基于BEC的探测方案在这些更现实条件下依然稳健，尽管灵敏度略有下降。这进一步支持了利用受量子信息启发的桌面实验来探测引力的量子性质。

Abstract: In a recent work \href{https://doi.org/10.1103/PRXQuantum.2.010325}{PRX QUANTUM 2 (2021) 010325}, a new way of investigating quantum gravity signatures using quantum information theoretic techniques, have been proposed. The primary result of this analysis revealed that non-Gaussianity can arise only through the consideration of a quantum model for the gravity part. Compared to classical gravity, only quantum gravity can result in non-quadratic operators in the Hamiltonian which leads to the non-Gaussian behavior. In our current analysis, we have considered a more realistic scenario taking into effect leading order post-Newtonian corrections in the analysis. We have stayed with the same model of a Bose-Einstein condensate placed inside a harmonic trap potential which indeed works as the detector of the non-Gaussianity generated due to quantum gravitational effects. Bose-Einstein condensates are experimentally well studied; apart from being a single quantum system, they include Feshbach resonances, which helps tuning the strength of the electromagnetic interactions which in principle can be set to zero. This is important since it can help distinguish quantum gravity from electromagnetic interactions without affecting gravitational interactions, and any non-Gaussianity can then be solely attributed to quantum gravity. We observe that the signal to noise ratio gets slightly damped due to the post-Newtonian effects taken under consideration.

</details>

### [70] [A Landscape of Cosmological Decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07663)
*S. Shajidul Haque,Bret Underwood*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper proposes a unified framework for early-universe quantum-to-classical transition by analyzing mixed states of primordial perturbations, constrained by purity and momentum variance. It shows that viable decoherence models must satisfy stringent bounds from gravitational non-linearities and CMB coherence, ruling out thermal states and limiting amplitude-diagonal models to <70 e-folds of inflation.
\n\n
本文提出一个统一框架，通过分析原初扰动的混合态（由纯度和动量方差参数化）来研究早期宇宙的量子-经典过渡。研究表明，可行的退相干模型必须满足来自引力非线性和CMB时间相干性的严格限制：热态被彻底排除，而振幅对角退相干模型被限制在少于70个e-fold的暴胀内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Observational constraints (adiabaticity, near-Gaussianity, scale invariance) do not fully fix the quantum state of primordial perturbations; generic mixed states consistent with data possess unconstrained parameters like purity and momentum variance. Understanding these degrees of freedom is essential to model the quantum-to-classical transition and assess physical viability of decoherence mechanisms in inflationary cosmology.
\n\n
观测约束（绝热性、近高斯性、近标度不变性）并未完全确定原初扰动的量子态；与数据一致的通用混合态仍具有未受约束的自由度，如纯度和动量方差。理解这些自由度对于建模量子-经典过渡以及评估暴胀宇宙学中退相干机制的物理可行性至关重要。

Method: The authors parameterize allowable mixed states of primordial curvature perturbations using quantum-optical tools—specifically purity $\\gamma$ and momentum variance $\\langle \\pi^2 \\rangle$. They analyze the geometry of this state space and impose two key physical criteria: (1) positivity of the Glauber-Sudarshan $P$-function (requiring active momentum injection by the environment), and (2) suppression of gravitational non-linearities sourced by the decaying mode of the Bardeen potential $\\Phi$, which is dynamically enhanced by large momentum variance during radiation domination. They derive theoretical bounds by demanding that non-linear corrections remain perturbative and that CMB temporal coherence is preserved.
\n\n
作者使用量子光学工具（特别是纯度$\\gamma$和动量方差$\\langle \\pi^2 \\rangle$）参数化原初曲率扰动的允许混合态。他们分析该态空间的几何结构，并施加两个关键物理判据：(1) Glauber-Sudarshan $P$-函数的正定性（要求环境主动向系统注入动量）；(2) 由Bardeen势$\\Phi$的衰减模所引发的引力非线性效应必须被抑制——该衰减模在辐射主导时期因大动量方差而被动力学增强。通过要求非线性修正保持微扰性且CMB时间相干性不被破坏，他们导出了理论限制。

Result: The analysis yields two major constraints: (i) Decohered thermal states are definitively excluded because they produce excessive gravitational non-linearities; (ii) Amplitude-diagonal decoherence models suffer from infrared divergences and are only viable if inflation lasts fewer than 70 $e$-folds. Additionally, the decaying mode induced by enhanced momentum variance vanishes sufficiently fast after horizon re-entry to preserve CMB coherence, but its initial amplitude tightly constrains early-universe decoherence scenarios.
\n\n
分析得出两个主要限制：(i) 退相干热态因产生过强的引力非线性而被彻底排除；(ii) 振幅对角退相干模型存在红外发散问题，仅在暴胀持续少于70个e-fold时才可行。此外，由增强动量方差诱导的衰减模在重新进入视界后衰减足够快以保持CMB相干性，但其初始振幅对早期宇宙退相干场景施加了严格约束。

Conclusion: The paper establishes a geometric and physically consistent framework for classifying mixed initial states of primordial perturbations, linking decoherence mechanisms to observable consequences via momentum variance and purity. Viable models of quantum-to-classical transition must avoid excessive momentum fluctuations to prevent non-linear gravitational backreaction, thereby significantly narrowing the landscape of allowed early-universe quantum states.
\n\n
本文建立了一个几何上清晰且物理自洽的框架，通过动量方差和纯度将退相干机制与可观测效应联系起来，用于分类原初扰动的混合初态。可行的量子-经典过渡模型必须避免过大的动量涨落，以防引发引力非线性反作用，从而大幅收窄了允许的早期宇宙量子态景观。

Abstract: Current observations constrain primordial perturbations to be adiabatic, approximately Gaussian, and nearly-scale invariant. However, a generic mixed state satisfying these constraints has additional unconstrained degrees of freedom, which can be parameterized by the purity of the state and its momentum variance. This allowable parameter space reveals a unified geometric landscape of mixed states, allowing us to map and relate distinct models of decoherence and their respective pointer bases. Crossing the threshold of a regular, positive-definite Glauber-Sudarshan $P$-function requires the environment to actively inject momentum into the system, rather than suppress it. This enhanced momentum variance dynamically sources the decaying mode of the gravitational potential in the radiation era. While the decaying mode vanishes fast enough to preserve the temporal coherence of the Cosmic Microwave Background, its initial amplitude places severe theoretical constraints on decoherence models to avoid gravitational non-linearities. These non-linearity bounds definitively rule out decohered thermal states, while long-wavelength divergences restrict amplitude-diagonal decoherence models to fewer than 70 $e$-folds of inflation. Altogether, we present a unifying framework for evaluating the quantum-to-classical transition of the early universe.

</details>

Method: The authors define a Bartnik-type quasilocal mass by minimizing the ADM mass over admissible extensions of a given initial data set. They analyze two classes of domains: (i) compact spacelike hypersurfaces with apparent horizon boundary, and (ii) noncompact asymptotically flat hypersurfaces containing an apparent horizon. Positivity is established via contradiction using the dominant energy condition and properties of apparent horizons. Monotonicity is derived by examining time evolution under appropriate geometric flows or Einstein evolution equations.

Conclusion: This work reinforces the physical relevance of Bartnik-type quasilocal masses by establishing fundamental properties—positivity and monotonicity—in black hole contexts, supporting their use as robust measures of localized gravitational energy.
\n\n
本工作通过在黑洞背景下确立Bartnik型准局域质量的基本性质（正能量性与单调性），强化了其物理相关性，支持将其作为局域引力能量的可靠度量。

Abstract: We define a quasilocal mass of Bartnik type, and establish its positivity and temporal monotonicity properties for two classes of domains associated with black holes. More precisely, we first show that the quasilocal mass is strictly positive for spacelike hypersurfaces that are: compact with apparent horizon boundary or noncompact with asymptotically flat ends and containing an apparent horizon in any admissible extension. Secondly, we show that the quasilocal mass is monotonically nondecreasing in time within evolutionary scenarios related to the two aforementioned settings.

</details>

TL;DR: The paper shows that in canonical single-field eternal inflation, stochastic upward fluctuations of the inflaton do not violate the smeared null energy condition (SNEC) because gravitational backreaction disrupts the background spacetime long before the SNEC bound is reached. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eternal inflation features rare stochastic upward fluctuations of the inflaton that locally increase the Hubble parameter $H$, seemingly conflicting with the smeared null energy condition (SNEC), which bounds negative energy along null geodesics. The authors aim to resolve whether such quantum-induced self-reproduction genuinely violates SNEC within the semiclassical regime.
\n\n
永恒暴胀中暴胀子的稀有随机向上涨落会局部提升哈勃参数 $H$，这似乎与沿类光测地线对负能量施加限制的 smeared null energy condition (SNEC) 相冲突。作者旨在澄清：这种量子诱导的自复制是否在半经典框架内真正违反 SNEC。

Method: The authors employ two complementary approaches: (1) an ensemble description via the Fokker-Planck equation to compute the drift of the Hubble parameter $H$, showing it is suppressed by slow-roll parameters and semiclassical factors; and (2) a single-trajectory analysis comparing the timescale to saturate the SNEC bound ($N_{\rm SNEC}$) with the backreaction timescale ($N_{\rm BR}$), revealing $N_{\rm SNEC} \gg N_{\rm BR}$.
\n\n
作者采用两种互补方法：(1) 利用 Fokker-Planck 方程描述系综行为，计算哈勃参数 $H$ 的漂移，表明其受慢滚参数和半经典压低因子抑制；(2) 单轨迹分析比较达到 SNEC 限制所需的时间尺度（$N_{\rm SNEC}$）与引力反作用时间尺度（$N_{\rm BR}$），发现 $N_{\rm SNEC} \gg N_{\rm BR}$。

Result: The ensemble drift of $H$ is parametrically small due to slow-roll suppression, and for individual trajectories, gravitational backreaction invalidates the fixed-background approximation well before the SNEC bound can be approached. Thus, no actual violation of SNEC occurs in the semiclassical slow-roll regime.
\n\n
系综中 $H$ 的漂移因慢滚压低而参数上极小；对于单条轨迹，引力反作用会在接近 SNEC 限制前就破坏固定背景近似。因此，在半经典慢滚机制下，SNEC 实际上并未被违反。

Conclusion: Standard stochastic eternal inflation does not lead to violations of the smeared null energy condition (SNEC) as long as one remains within the semiclassical slow-roll regime. Apparent tensions arise only when neglecting gravitational backreaction, which becomes dominant long before SNEC saturation.
\n\n
只要处于半经典慢滚机制内，标准的随机永恒暴胀并不会导致 smeared null energy condition (SNEC) 的违反。表面上的张力仅在忽略引力反作用时出现，而该效应在达到 SNEC 饱和前早已主导动力学。

Abstract: The smeared null energy condition (SNEC) imposes a semilocal bound on the negative energy accumulated along null geodesics. In eternal inflation, rare stochastic upward fluctuations of the inflaton locally increase the Hubble parameter, creating an apparent tension with the SNEC. Focusing on a canonical single-field model, we investigate whether this quantum-induced self-reproduction violates the SNEC. Using the Fokker-Planck equation, we demonstrate that the ensemble drift of the Hubble parameter is parametrically bounded by slow-roll parameters and semiclassical suppression. Furthermore, a complementary single-trajectory analysis reveals a strong timescale hierarchy, $N_{\rm SNEC} \gg N_{\rm BR}$. This indicates that even for rare upward stochastic excursions, gravitational backreaction invalidates the background spacetime assumption long before the SNEC bound can be mathematically approached. We conclude that while standard stochastic diffusion drives eternal inflation, it does not inherently lead to SNEC violations within the semiclassical slow-roll regime.

</details>

TL;DR: The paper investigates three degenerate spacetime geometries with wormhole topology—derived from Rindler, Minkowski, and Schwarzschild vacua via branching coordinate transformations—and shows they are matter-free solutions in the Einstein–Palatini–Cartan formalism, unlike conventional thin-shell wormholes that require exotic matter. These degenerate configurations cannot be obtained as smooth limits of non-degenerate wormholes, indicating they form a distinct sector in general relativity’s configuration space.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conventional traversable wormholes in general relativity typically require exotic matter violating energy conditions, as dictated by the thin-shell formalism. 广义相对论中传统的可穿越虫洞通常需要违反能量条件的奇异物质，这是由薄壳形式所决定的。作者旨在探索：若放松度规的非退化条件（即允许$\\det(g_{\\mu\\nu}) = 0$），是否存在无物质的类虫洞时空，从而探究退化几何在引力物理中的作用。

Method: The authors construct three explicit degenerate spacetime metrics with wormhole topology by applying branching coordinate transformations to the Rindler, Minkowski, and Schwarzschild vacuum solutions. They then analyze these configurations within the Einstein–Palatini–Cartan formulation, where the connection is treated as an independent variable. This framework allows consistent treatment of degenerate metrics (with vanishing determinant) and reveals that the resulting field equations are satisfied without any matter source ($T_{\\mu\\nu} = 0$).

Result: All three degenerate wormhole configurations—the planar-throat Rindler wormhole and the spherical-throat Klinkhamer and Schwarzschild Klinkhamer wormholes—are shown to be exact vacuum solutions ($R_{\\mu\
u} = 0$ in the Palatini sense) without exotic matter. Moreover, no continuous limiting procedure connects these degenerate spacetimes to their non-degenerate counterparts, implying a topological or geometric discontinuity in the space of solutions.

所有三种退化虫洞构型——平面喉部的Rindler虫洞以及球形喉部的Klinkhamer虫洞和Schwarzschild Klinkhamer虫洞——均被证明是精确的真空解（在Palatini意义下满足$R_{\\mu\
u} = 0$），且无需奇异物质。此外，不存在从非退化时空到这些退化构型的连续极限过程，表明解空间中存在拓扑或几何不连续性。

Conclusion: Degenerate spacetime geometries with wormhole topology constitute a physically distinct and mathematically consistent sector of general relativity when formulated in the Einstein–Palatini–Cartan framework. Their matter-free nature and lack of connection to standard wormhole solutions suggest that degeneracy introduces genuinely new gravitational phenomena beyond the scope of classical non-degenerate GR.
\n\n
在Einstein–Palatini–Cartan形式下，具有虫洞拓扑的退化时空几何构成了广义相对论中一个物理上独立且数学上自洽的扇区。其无物质特性及与标准虫洞解的不可连通性表明，退化性引入了超越经典非退化广义相对论范畴的全新引力现象。

Abstract: We examine three degenerate spacetime configurations with wormhole topology, obtained via branching coordinate transformations of the Rindler, Minkowski, and Schwarzschild vacuum metrics.These configurations are, respectively, a Rindler wormhole with a planar throat, and the Klinkhamer and Schwarzschild Klinkhamer wormholes with spherical throats. Within the framework of the Einstein Palatini Cartan formulation, we demonstrate that these degenerate configurations are matter free. In this regard, they differ fundamentally from their nondegenerate wormhole counterparts in the thin shell model, which require exotic matter. Nevertheless, the degenerate configurations considered here exhibit distinct physical manifestations, ranging from purely topological structures to genuine gravitational effects. Furthermore, in all three examples, we demonstrate the absence of a limiting transition from the non-degenerate spacetime to the matter free degenerate configuration. This suggests that degenerate geometries constitute an independent sector of the configuration space of general relativity.

### [74] [Warped Product Einstein Manifolds in Four Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08047)
*Jack C. M. Hughes,Joudy F. Jamal Beek,Fedor V. Kusmartsev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper explores the chiral $6 \\times 6$ matrix representation of the curvature tensor on 4D (pseudo-)Riemannian manifolds, focusing on warped product geometries. It constructs explicit block decompositions for $1+3$, $2+2$, and $3+1$ warped products, relates them via similarity transformations, and uses this framework to algebraically classify Einstein warped products by Petrov type: $3+1$ are generically type-I, $2+2$ are type-D, and $1+3$ are necessarily type-O. In the Riemannian case, topological constraints force half-conformally flat Einstein warped products to be flat.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to exploit the chiral decomposition of the Riemann curvature tensor in four dimensions—an endomorphism on $\\Lambda^2 T^*\\mathcal{M}$—to gain algebraic insight into the structure of Einstein warped product manifolds. By comparing the standard chiral representation with an alternative block structure adapted to the warped product fibration, the authors aim to derive Petrov classifications and topological constraints purely from linear-algebraic properties of the curvature operator.
\n\n
动机在于利用四维中黎曼曲率张量的手征分解（作为 $\\Lambda^2 T^*\\mathcal{M}$ 上的自同态），以获得对爱因斯坦翘曲积流形结构的代数理解。通过将标准手征表示与适应于翘曲积纤维化的替代块结构进行比较，作者旨在仅从曲率算子的线性代数性质出发，推导出 Petrov 分类和拓扑约束。

Method: The authors use the identification of the Riemann tensor as a symmetric linear operator on the 6-dimensional space of 2-forms, which splits into self-dual and anti-self-dual subspaces under Hodge duality in 4D. For a warped product $\\mathcal{M} = F \\times_f B$, they construct an alternative basis for $\\Lambda^2 T^*\\mathcal{M}$ induced by the exterior algebras on base $B$ and fiber $F$, yielding a different $6 \\times 6$ block structure. They then explicitly compute the similarity transformation linking this warped-product-adapted representation to the standard chiral one for three splittings: $1+3$, $2+2$, and $3+1$. The Einstein condition (vanishing off-diagonal blocks in the chiral frame) is imposed to constrain the possible algebraic types.
\n\n
作者将黎曼张量视为 2-形式 6 维空间上的对称线性算子，该空间在四维中通过霍奇对偶分解为自对偶与反自对偶子空间。对于翘曲积 $\\mathcal{M} = F \\times_f B$，他们基于底空间 $B$ 和纤维 $F$ 上的外代数构造 $\\Lambda^2 T^*\\mathcal{M}$ 的另一组基，从而得到不同的 $6 \\times 6$ 块结构。随后，他们显式计算了连接这种翘曲积适配表示与标准手征表示的相似变换，适用于三种分裂情形：$1+3$、$2+2$ 和 $3+1$。通过施加爱因斯坦条件（在手征框架中非对角块为零），约束可能的代数类型。

Result: The key results are: (i) explicit construction of curvature matrices and similarity transformations for all three warped product types; (ii) Petrov classification of Einstein warped products: $3+1$ are generically type-I, $2+2$ are type-D, and $1+3$ must be conformally flat (type-O); (iii) in the closed Riemannian setting, if such an Einstein warped product is half-conformally flat (i.e., either self-dual or anti-self-dual Weyl tensor vanishes), it must be globally flat due to topological obstructions (e.g., signature and Euler characteristic constraints).
\n\n
主要结果包括：(i) 对所有三种翘曲积类型显式构造了曲率矩阵及相似变换；(ii) 对爱因斯坦翘曲积进行 Petrov 分类：$3+1$ 型通常为 I 型，$2+2$ 型为 D 型，而 $1+3$ 型必为共形平坦（O 型）；(iii) 在闭黎曼情形下，若此类爱因斯坦翘曲积为半共形平坦（即自对偶或反自对偶 Weyl 张量之一为零），则由于拓扑障碍（如符号差和欧拉示性数约束），其必为整体平坦。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that the interplay between the chiral curvature decomposition and warped product geometry provides a powerful algebraic framework for classifying Einstein manifolds. The Petrov types are rigidly constrained by the dimensionality of the base and fiber, and in the Riemannian case, global topology further restricts possible geometries—forcing flatness in the half-conformally flat Einstein limit. This approach bridges differential geometry, representation theory, and global analysis.
\n\n
本文表明，手征曲率分解与翘曲积几何之间的相互作用为爱因斯坦流形的分类提供了强有力的代数框架。Petrov 类型被底空间与纤维的维数严格约束，而在黎曼情形下，整体拓扑进一步限制了可能的几何结构——在半共形平坦的爱因斯坦极限下强制其为平坦。该方法融合了微分几何、表示论与整体分析。

Abstract: On four-dimensional (pseudo-)Riemannian manifolds $\mathcal{M}$ the curvature tensor (viewed as an endomorphism on 2-forms) admits a chiral $6 \times 6$ matrix representation which decomposes into four $3 \times 3$ blocks. $\mathcal{M}$ is Einstein if and only if the off-diagonal blocks vanish. If the manifold is a warped product $\mathcal{M} = F \times_f B$, then there exists an alternative matrix representation relative to the decomposition of the 2-forms into spaces induced by the exterior algebra on both the base and the fiber. These two representations are not independent and a similarity transformation can be found between them. We construct these matrices and associated transformations for $1+3$, $2+2$ and $3+1$ warped products, giving classifications for the Einstein limits from this algebraic perspective. Using this, one can easily Petrov classify the Einstein warped products for each case considered: $3+1$ are generically type-\RNum{1}, $2+2$ are type-D while $1+3$ are constrained to be type-O. In the closed Riemannian case, there are a number of topological restrictions on these manifolds that we discuss: in the half-conformally flat limit, each of these Einstein warped products must be flat.

</details>

*Shudi Jiang,Jianhui Lin,Xiangdong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper extends the higher-dimensional quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder model to include a cosmological constant, focusing on anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how quantum gravitational effects modify black hole thermodynamics in the presence of a cosmological constant, particularly in AdS spacetime where holographic and thermodynamic dualities are well-established. Additionally, the heat capacity exhibits characteristic behavior indicative of an extra phase transition induced by quantum corrections, highlighting the profound impact of quantum effects on black hole thermodynamics.

In a Friedmann-Robertson-Walker (FRW) universe, Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) constraints restrict the coupling such that the dark sector interacts only with the trace of the energy-momentum tensor.   <summary>Details</summary>

Consequently, the dust energy density scales as $\rho_{\text{dust}} \propto a^{-3/(1+\tau)}$, where $\tau$ quantifies the coupling strength between dust and the dark fluid, deviating from standard $\Lambda$CDM ($\tau=0$).

在FRW背景下，CMB约束迫使conformal Killing张量的贡献消失，仅留下 $g^{\mu\nu}(R, T)$ 型项作为dark sector。总能量-动量张量 $T_{\text{tot}}^{\mu\nu} = T_{\text{dust}}^{\mu\nu} + T_{\text{dark}}^{\mu\nu}$ 是守恒的（$\nabla_\mu T_{\text{tot}}^{\mu\nu} = 0$），尽管单独的 $\nabla_\mu T_{\text{dust}}^{\mu\nu} \neq 0$ 和 $\nabla_\mu T_{\text{dark}}^{\mu\nu} \neq 0$。因此，dust物质的能量密度按 $\rho_{\text{dust}} \propto a^{-3/(1+\tau)}$ 演化，其中 $\tau$ 表征了dust与dark fluid之间的耦合强度，偏离了标准的 $\Lambda$CDM 模型（$\tau=0$）。

Conclusion: Harada gravity offers a consistent theoretical framework extending Rastall and Unimodular gravity, where the dark sector emerges naturally from geometric modifications. Observational constraints from the CMB select a specific coupling form—only through the trace $T$—leading to testable deviations in cosmic expansion and matter evolution. Abstract: The so called Harada gravity with non conserved energy-momentum tensor is here taken into account. It includes Rastall gravity as a special case. The field equations are written as Einstein equations where the source is supplemented by a divergence-free conformal Killing tensor and a tensor proportional to the metric, linear in the scalar curvature and the trace of the energy-momentum tensor. These terms can be natural candidates for dark sector and give rise to a coupling of the dark sector with the matter content. The field equations are the conformal Killing extension of Rastall gravity, and include Unimodular gravity. In a Friedmann-Robertson-Walker background, the Cosmic Microwave Background restricts parameters so that the dark sector only couples with the trace of the energy-momentum tensor. The explicit form of the tensor for the dark sector is found, and the Friedmann and continuity equations are presented, with a standard cosmological analysis. Abstract: Gravitational-waves from astrophysical sources are characterized by their extreme faintness, which remains a primary obstacle for both current and next generation detectors. By formalizing the interaction between the bosonic cloud and an ambient stochastic gravitational-wave background, we establish the rigorous selection rules and threshold conditions that govern this amplification. Our analysis reveals that the emission rate depends critically on the boson mass, potentially yielding an enhancement of several orders of magnitude over spontaneous processes. For representative mass ranges, these amplified signals bridge the sensitivity gap between ground-based interferometers and pulsar timing arrays. These findings suggest that superradiant clouds can effectively boost previously undetectable signals, offering a novel observational frontier for exploring ultralight fields and the Kerr spacetime environment.

TL;DR: This paper re-examines Hawking radiation for two black-hole models—the Simpson–Visser black-bounce and Casadio–Fabbri–Mazzacurati braneworld geometries—that can approach wormhole configurations. Replacing the WKB approximation for greybody factors with direct numerical scattering calculations for photons and massless Dirac fields, the authors find that while qualitative trends (cooling, flux suppression, fermion dominance) persist, quantitative discrepancies are significant: WKB overestimates luminosity by orders of magnitude near the cold wormhole endpoint, leading to an ~85× underestimation of black-hole lifetime in Simpson–Visser models.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous studies of Hawking radiation in exotic black-hole spacetimes approaching wormhole limits relied on the WKB approximation to compute greybody factors, which may be unreliable far from the Schwarzschild regime—especially near the cold, extremal-like endpoint where low-frequency transmission dominates. The authors aim to assess the accuracy of WKB by performing exact numerical computations of scattering amplitudes for photon and massless Dirac fields, thereby obtaining more reliable evaporation rates and lifetimes for such black holes.
\n\n
先前对趋近虫洞极限的奇异黑洞时空中的霍金辐射研究依赖于 WKB 近似计算灰体因子，但在远离 Schwarzschild 区域（尤其是在低温、类极端端点附近，低频透射起主导作用）时，该近似可能不可靠。作者旨在通过对光子和无质量 Dirac 场进行精确的数值散射计算，评估 WKB 方法的准确性，从而获得更可靠的蒸发率和黑洞寿命。

Method: The authors replace the WKB-based greybody factor approximation with direct numerical integration of the radial wave equation for both electromagnetic ($s=1$) and massless Dirac ($s=1/2$) fields in the two background geometries. They solve the time-independent Schrödinger-like scattering problem across the effective potential barrier to compute exact transmission coefficients $|T_{\\ell s}(\\omega)|^2$. These are then used to reconstruct the energy emission spectra via $$\\frac{d^2E}{d\\omega dt} = \\sum_{\\ell,s} N_{\\ell s} \\frac{\\omega}{\\exp(\\omega/T_H) - (-1)^{2s}} |T_{\\ell s}(\\omega)|^2,$$ and integrate to obtain total luminosities and half-decay times.
\n\n
作者在两种背景几何中，用对径向波动方程的直接数值积分取代基于 WKB 的灰体因子近似，分别处理电磁场（$s=1$）和无质量 Dirac 场（$s=1/2$）。他们求解类薛定谔的时间无关散射问题，跨越有效势垒以计算精确的透射系数 $|T_{\\ell s}(\\omega)|^2$。随后利用这些系数通过公式 $$\\frac{d^2E}{d\\omega dt} = \\sum_{\\ell,s} N_{\\ell s} \\frac{\\omega}{\\exp(\\omega/T_H) - (-1)^{2s}} |T_{\\ell s}(\\omega)|^2$$ 重构能量辐射谱，并积分得到总光度和半衰期。

Quantitatively, WKB greybody factors agree with numerical results only near the Schwarzschild limit but deviate severely elsewhere—overestimating luminosity by up to orders of magnitude near the cold endpoint. In the Simpson–Visser model, using direct greybody factors increases the half-decay time coefficient by a factor of ~85 compared to WKB estimates, implying drastically longer evaporation timescales.

定性上，当趋近虫洞极限时，黑洞温度降低（$T_H \\to 0$），总霍金通量被抑制，且由于费米子具有更低的离心势垒，辐射逐渐由费米子主导。定量上，WKB 灰体因子仅在接近 Schwarzschild 极限时与数值结果一致，而在其他区域严重偏离——在冷端点附近对光度的高估可达数个数量级。在 Simpson–Visser 模型中，使用直接计算的灰体因子使半衰期系数比 WKB 估计值增大了约 85 倍，表明蒸发时标显著延长。

Conclusion: Reliable predictions for the evaporation of black holes that evolve toward wormhole-like geometries cannot rely solely on WKB approximations for greybody factors. Abstract: We revisit Hawking radiation from two black-hole families that can approach macroscopic wormhole configurations: the Simpson--Visser black-bounce geometry and the Casadio--Fabbri--Mazzacurati braneworld geometry. The earlier analysis of these backgrounds relied on WKB greybody factors. Here we replace that approximation by direct numerical scattering for the photon and massless Dirac channels and then recompute the emission spectra and integrated luminosities. The qualitative picture remains the same: as the wormhole endpoint is approached the black holes cool, the total flux is strongly suppressed, and the residual emission becomes increasingly fermion dominated. The quantitative picture, however, changes substantially. Close to the Schwarzschild limit the WKB estimates are reasonably accurate, but far from that limit the error can be large, and near the cold endpoint it can reach orders of magnitude. In particular, the WKB calculation can substantially overestimate the remaining luminosity precisely in the regime where the evaporation rate is most sensitive to the low-frequency tail of the greybody factors. In a fixed-parameter Simpson--Visser half-decay estimate, the direct-greybody luminosities increase the WKB lifetime coefficient by a factor of about 85. These results show that reliable evaporation rates for black holes evolving toward wormhole-like endpoints require direct numerical greybody factors rather than barrier-top WKB estimates alone.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding the geometric structure of accelerated relativistic motion beyond uniform proper acceleration is essential for modeling realistic trajectories in flat spacetime, especially when higher-order kinematic effects (e.g., jerk and snap) and out-of-plane rotations (via torsion) become relevant. The standard treatment often assumes constant curvature or vanishing torsion; this work generalizes that framework.

理解超越均匀固有加速度的加速相对论运动的几何结构，对于在平直时空中建模真实轨迹至关重要，尤其是在高阶运动学效应（如急动度和跃度）以及由挠率引起的平面外旋转变得显著的情形下。标准处理通常假设曲率为常数或挠率为零；本文推广了这一框架。
