They construct an orthonormal tetrad via Gram-Schmidt orthogonalization applied to the four-velocity $u^\\mu$ and its successive proper-time derivatives ($\\dot{u}^\\mu$, $\\ddot{u}^\\mu$, etc.). This yields explicit relations between the Frenet-Serret curvature $\\kappa(\\tau)$, torsion $\\tau_1(\\tau)$, and higher-order invariants (e.g., $\\kappa^2 = a_\\mu a^\\mu$, where $a^\\mu = \\dot{u}^\\mu$), and they solve reduced Frenet-Serret equations for prescribed functional forms of jerk invariant and torsion.
\n\n
作者采用适用于闵可夫斯基时空中类时曲线的Frenet-Serret形式体系。他们通过对四速度 $u^\\mu$ 及其连续固有时导数（$\\dot{u}^\\mu$、$\\ddot{u}^\\mu$ 等）应用Gram-Schmidt正交化来构造一个正交归一四标架。由此得到Frenet-Serret曲率 $\\kappa(\\tau)$、挠率 $\\tau_1(\\tau)$ 与高阶不变量之间的显式关系（例如 $\\kappa^2 = a_\\mu a^\\mu$，其中 $a^\\mu = \\dot{u}^\\mu$），并针对给定的急动度不变量和挠率函数形式求解约化的Frenet-Serret方程。

Result: The paper derives explicit expressions connecting Frenet-Serret parameters to relativistic kinematic invariants: curvature $\\kappa = \\sqrt{a_\\mu a^\\mu}$, first torsion $\\tau_1 \\propto \\epsilon_{\\mu\\nu\\rho\\sigma} u^\\mu a^\\nu \\dot{a}^\\rho \\ddot{a}^\\sigma / \\kappa^4$, etc. For specific ansätze—such as constant jerk invariant or linear torsion—they obtain analytic curvature profiles $\\kappa(\\tau)$ and solve the reduced Frenet-Serret system, demonstrating how non-constant $\\kappa$ and non-vanishing $\\tau_1$ induce richer rotational dynamics of the comoving frame beyond planar motion.
\n\n
本文推导出Frenet-Serret参数与相对论运动学不变量之间的显式表达式：曲率 $\\kappa = \\sqrt{a_\\mu a^\\mu}$，第一挠率 $\\tau_1 \\propto \\epsilon_{\\mu\\nu\\rho\\sigma} u^\\mu a^\\nu \\dot{a}^\\rho \\ddot{a}^\\sigma / \\kappa^4$ 等。对于特定假设（如恒定急动度不变量或线性挠率），获得了解析的曲率轮廓 $\\kappa(\\tau)$ 并求解了约化的Frenet-Serret系统，展示了非常数 $\\kappa$ 和非零 $\\tau_1$ 如何导致共动标架产生超出平面运动的更丰富的旋转动力学。

Conclusion: The generalized Frenet-Serret framework provides a geometrically transparent description of arbitrary accelerated motion in special relativity. By incorporating proper-time-dependent curvature and torsion, it captures both in-plane non-uniform acceleration and out-of-plane rotational effects, offering a systematic tool to analyze high-order kinematics of relativistic trajectories.
\n\n
广义的Frenet-Serret框架为狭义相对论中任意加速运动提供了几何上清晰的描述。通过引入依赖于固有时的曲率和挠率，该框架同时刻画了平面内的非均匀加速度和平面外的旋转效应，为分析相对论轨迹的高阶运动学提供了一个系统性工具。

Abstract: We study Frenet-Serret equations for timelike worldlines in Minkowski spacetime with proper-time-dependent curvature and torsion. This corresponds to relativistic motion with non-uniform proper acceleration and, when torsion is included, to trajectories whose Frenet-Serret frame rotates beyond the acceleration plane. Using the Gram-Schmidt construction of the tetrad from the four-velocity and its derivatives, we relate the intrinsic Frenet-Serret parameters to kinematic quantities such as proper acceleration, four-jerk, and four-snap. We then consider simple analytic cases for the jerk invariant and torsion, obtaining explicit curvature profiles and reduced Frenet-Serret equations. These examples clarify how non-constant acceleration and torsion modify the geometry of accelerated relativistic motion.

</details>


### [80] [Curvature-induced scalarization of charged AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08507)
*Chao-Ming Zhang,Yun Soo Myung,Lina Zhang,De-Cheng Zou,Fu-Wen Shu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper studies Gauss-Bonnet (GB) scalarization of Reissner-Nordström-AdS (RN-AdS) black holes in Einstein-Maxwell-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet theory with a negative cosmological constant $Λ$. It shows that scalarization is governed not only by the effective tachyonic mass $μ^2_{\text{eff}} < 0$ but also constrained by the Breitenlohner-Freedman (BF) bound. For $0 < η < 2.25$, spontaneous GB$^+$ scalarization yields a single fundamental branch ($n=0$), while for $η < 0$, GB$^-$ scalarization produces a unique branch. When the BF bound is violated ($η > 2.25$ for $Λ = -0.5$), AdS-tachyonic instability occurs. Thermodynamic analysis via Gibbs free energy reveals a natural second-order phase transition from scalarized AdS black holes back to RN-AdS black holes.
\n\n
本文研究了在具有负宇宙学常数 $Λ$ 的 Einstein-Maxwell-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet 理论中，Reissner-Nordström-AdS（RN-AdS）黑洞的 Gauss-Bonnet（GB）标量化现象。研究表明，标量化不仅由有效快子质量 $μ^2_{\text{eff}} < 0$ 决定，还受到 Breitenlohner-Freedman（BF）界的约束。对于 $0 < η < 2.25$，自发 GB$^+$ 标量化仅产生单一基态分支（$n=0$），而 $η < 0$ 时 GB$^-$ 标量化也给出唯一分支。当 BF 界被破坏时（$Λ = -0.5$ 下 $η > 2.25$），会出现 AdS 快子不稳定性。通过 Gibbs 自由能的热力学分析发现，标量化的 AdS 黑洞会自然地发生二阶相变回到 RN-AdS 黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from understanding how asymptotically Anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetimes modify spontaneous scalarization mechanisms known in asymptotically flat space, particularly in Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet theories. In flat space, scalarization arises when an effective mass squared $μ^2_{\text{eff}}$ becomes negative due to coupling to the Gauss-Bonnet invariant, triggering a tachyonic instability. However, in AdS, the presence of a negative cosmological constant introduces a new stability criterion—the Breitenlohner-Freedman (BF) bound—which can prevent or alter scalarization even when $μ^2_{\text{eff}} < 0$. This work aims to clarify the interplay between GB-induced tachyonic instability and the BF bound, and to characterize the resulting scalarized black hole solutions and their thermodynamic stability.
\n\n
动机源于理解渐近 Anti-de Sitter（AdS）时空如何改变在渐近平直时空中已知的自发标量化机制，尤其是在 Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet 理论中。在平直时空中，当有效质量平方 $μ^2_{\text{eff}}$ 因与 Gauss-Bonnet 不变量耦合而变为负值时，会触发快子不稳定性，从而引发标量化。然而，在 AdS 时空中，负宇宙学常数引入了一个新的稳定性判据——Breitenlohner-Freedman（BF）界——即使 $μ^2_{\text{eff}} < 0$，该判据也可能抑制或改变标量化行为。本文旨在阐明 GB 诱导的快子不稳定性与 BF 界之间的相互作用，并刻画由此产生的标量化黑洞解及其热力学稳定性。

Method: The authors analyze linear scalar perturbations around Reissner-Nordström-AdS (RN-AdS) black hole backgrounds in the Einstein-Maxwell-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet theory with action containing a coupling $η φ \\mathcal{G}$, where $\\mathcal{G}$ is the Gauss-Bonnet scalar and $φ$ the scalar field. They derive the effective mass term $μ^2_{\text{eff}} = -η \\mathcal{G}$ governing the perturbation equation. Stability is assessed by comparing $μ^2_{\text{eff}}$ against the Breitenlohner-Freedman bound $μ^2_{\\text{BF}} = -\\frac{9}{4L^2}$ (with $L$ the AdS radius, related to $Λ = -3/L^2$). Numerical construction of non-trivial scalarized black hole solutions is performed for different signs and magnitudes of $η$, distinguishing GB$^+$ ($η>0$) and GB$^-$ ($η<0$) cases. Thermodynamic preference is evaluated using the Gibbs free energy $G = M - TS - ΦQ$, where $M$, $T$, $S$, $Φ$, and $Q$ are mass, temperature, entropy, electric potential, and charge, respectively.
\n\n
作者在 Einstein-Maxwell-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet 理论中，围绕 Reissner-Nordström-AdS（RN-AdS）黑洞背景分析标量场的线性微扰，该理论的作用量包含耦合项 $η φ \\mathcal{G}$，其中 $\\mathcal{G}$ 为 Gauss-Bonnet 标量，$φ$ 为标量场。他们推导出控制微扰方程的有效质量项 $μ^2_{\text{eff}} = -η \\mathcal{G}$。通过将 $μ^2_{\text{eff}}$ 与 Breitenlohner-Freedman 界 $μ^2_{\\text{BF}} = -\\frac{9}{4L^2}$（$L$ 为 AdS 半径，满足 $Λ = -3/L^2$）进行比较来评估稳定性。针对不同符号和大小的 $η$，数值构造非平凡的标量化黑洞解，区分 GB$^+$（$η>0$）和 GB$^-$（$η<0$）情形。热力学优劣通过 Gibbs 自由能 $G = M - TS - ΦQ$ 判断，其中 $M$、$T$、$S$、$Φ$ 和 $Q$ 分别为质量、温度、熵、电势和电荷。

Result: Key results include: (1) The onset condition for scalarization in AdS requires both $μ^2_{\text{eff}} < 0$ and violation of the BF bound, i.e., $μ^2_{\text{eff}} < μ^2_{\\text{BF}}$. For $Λ = -0.5$ ($L^2 = 6$), this implies $η > 2.25$ triggers AdS-tachyonic instability. (2) For $0 < η < 2.25$, spontaneous GB$^+$ scalarization occurs despite $μ^2_{\text{eff}} > μ^2_{\\text{BF}}$, yielding only the fundamental ($n=0$) branch of scalarized black holes—contrary to expectations of infinite branches seen in some flat-space models. (3) For $η < 0$, GB$^-$ scalarization always produces a single branch of scalarized solutions. (4) Thermodynamic analysis shows that the Gibbs free energy of scalarized black holes is higher than that of the RN-AdS branch, indicating that scalarized solutions are thermodynamically disfavored and undergo a second-order phase transition back to RN-AdS black holes.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) AdS 中标量化的发生条件不仅要求 $μ^2_{\text{eff}} < 0$，还需违反 BF 界，即 $μ^2_{\text{eff}} < μ^2_{\\text{BF}}$。对于 $Λ = -0.5$（$L^2 = 6$），这意味着 $η > 2.25$ 会触发 AdS 快子不稳定性。(2) 对于 $0 < η < 2.25$，尽管 $μ^2_{\text{eff}} > μ^2_{\\text{BF}}$，仍会发生自发 GB$^+$ 标量化，但仅产生基态（$n=0$）分支的标量化黑洞——这与某些平直时空模型中预期的无限分支相矛盾。(3) 对于 $η < 0$，GB$^-$ 标量化始终给出唯一分支的标化解。(4) 热力学分析表明，标量化黑洞的 Gibbs 自由能高于 RN-AdS 分支，说明标化解在热力学上不利，并会通过二阶相变回到 RN-AdS 黑洞。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that the negative cosmological constant fundamentally alters the scalarization mechanism in Gauss-Bonnet gravity. The Breitenlohner-Freedman bound acts as a critical threshold that suppresses scalarization unless strongly violated. Even when scalarization occurs within the BF-stable regime ($0 < η < 2.25$), it produces only a single fundamental branch, challenging previous assumptions about solution multiplicity. Moreover, the scalarized AdS black holes are thermodynamically unstable relative to the RN-AdS background, undergoing a second-order phase transition. This implies that in AdS, GB scalarization is either pathological (when BF-violating) or transient/metastable (when BF-respecting), and does not lead to stable, preferred black hole configurations.
\n\n
本研究表明，负宇宙学常数从根本上改变了 Gauss-Bonnet 引力中的标量化机制。Breitenlohner-Freedman 界作为一个关键阈值，除非被强烈破坏，否则会抑制标量化。即使在 BF 稳定区域内发生标量化（$0 < η < 2.25$），也仅产生单一基态分支，挑战了关于解多重性的先前假设。此外，标量化的 AdS 黑洞相对于 RN-AdS 背景在热力学上不稳定，会发生二阶相变。这意味着在 AdS 时空中，GB 标量化要么是病态的（违反 BF 界时），要么是瞬态或亚稳的（遵守 BF 界时），并不会导致稳定且热力学优先的黑洞构型。

Abstract: We investigate how a negative cosmological constant affects the Gauss-Bonnet (GB) scalarization in the Einstein-Maxwell-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet theory with a scalar coupling constant $η$ to GB term. We focus on the instability of Reissner-Nordström-AdS (RN-AdS) black holes under a scalar perturbation governed by an effective mass $μ^2_{\text{eff}}$ sourced by the GB term. Unlike the asymptotically flat spacetime case, the onset of scalarization is not merely determined by $μ^2_{\text{eff}} < 0$, but it is constrained by the Breitenlohner-Freedman (BF) bound. In case that the BF bound is violated ($η>2.25$ with $Λ=-0.5$), one may find AdS-tachyoinic instability. We find that for $0<η<2.25$, the GB$^+$ scalarization may be performed through spontaneous scalarization, while for $η<0$ the GB$^-$ scalarization is found to give the single branch of scalarized AdS black holes. For the GB$^+$ scalarization in $η_{th}\leη<2.25$ with $η_{th}$ threshold instability, we obtain the single branch ($n=0$ fundamental branch) of scalarized AdS black holes, contradicting to infinite branches. Finally, it is observed from Gibbs free energy that a phase transition from scalarized AdS black holes to RNAdS black hole occur natually and it is confirmed to be second-order.

</details>


### [81] [Shadow of Rotating Black Hole Immersed in Dark Matter Halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08616)
*Yong-You Liu,Tian-Yun Ye,Zhen Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper studies how a generalized double power law dark matter halo affects the spacetime geometry, photon orbits, and shadow of a rotating black hole. Using the Newman–Janis algorithm to generate a rotating metric from a static dark matter black hole solution, the authors show that the dark matter halo enlarges the event horizon, shifts the photon shell outward, and distorts the shadow—effects amplified by high spin. They use Sgr A* shadow data to constrain dark matter halo parameters and reveal degeneracies with black hole spin and inclination angle.
\n\n
本文研究了广义双幂律暗物质晕对旋转黑洞时空几何、光子轨道和阴影的影响。作者利用Newman–Janis算法从静态暗物质黑洞解构造出旋转度规，发现暗物质晕会扩大事件视界、使光子壳层外移并扭曲黑洞阴影——这些效应在高自旋情况下更为显著。他们利用Sgr A*的阴影观测数据约束了暗物质晕参数空间，并揭示了其与黑洞自旋及观测倾角之间的简并性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the Event Horizon Telescope’s (EHT) observations of black hole shadows, which offer a new observational window into strong-field gravity and the immediate environment of supermassive black holes. Since dark matter halos are theoretically expected to accumulate around galactic-center black holes, their gravitational influence could imprint detectable features on the shadow morphology. This work aims to systematically explore such imprints using a physically motivated dark matter profile—the generalized double power law—and connect theoretical predictions to actual EHT data.
\n\n
动机源于事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope, EHT）对黑洞阴影的观测，这为强引力场和超大质量黑洞近邻环境提供了新的观测窗口。理论上预期星系中心黑洞周围存在暗物质晕，其引力效应可能在阴影形态上留下可观测印记。本文旨在利用一个物理上合理的暗物质分布模型——广义双幂律轮廓（generalized double power law）——系统研究此类印记，并将理论预测与实际EHT数据联系起来。

Method: The authors begin with a static spherically symmetric black hole solution embedded in a generalized double power law dark matter halo. They apply the Newman–Janis algorithm to complexify the metric and generate a rotating (Kerr-like) spacetime, resulting in a modified mass function $M(r)$ that incorporates the dark matter density profile. They then compute key geometric features: event horizons from $\\Delta(r) = 0$, ergosphere boundaries from $g_{tt} = 0$, and unstable spherical photon orbits via an effective potential analysis. The black hole shadow is constructed by tracing null geodesics backward from an asymptotic observer, parameterized by celestial coordinates $(\\alpha, \\beta)$. Finally, they compare synthetic shadow images with the observed Sgr A* shadow size and shape to perform parameter estimation and identify degeneracies among dark matter parameters, black hole spin $a$, and inclination angle $i$.
\n\n
作者从嵌入广义双幂律暗物质晕中的静态球对称黑洞解出发，应用Newman–Janis算法对度规进行复化，生成旋转（类Kerr）时空，得到包含暗物质密度轮廓的修正质量函数 $M(r)$。随后计算关键几何结构：通过 $\\Delta(r) = 0$ 确定事件视界，由 $g_{tt} = 0$ 确定能层边界，并通过有效势分析求解不稳定球形光子轨道。黑洞阴影通过从渐近观测者反向追踪零测地线构建，用天球坐标 $(\\alpha, \\beta)$ 参数化。最后，将合成阴影图像与观测到的Sgr A*阴影大小和形状对比，进行参数估计，并识别暗物质参数、黑洞自旋 $a$ 和倾角 $i$ 之间的简并性。

Result: The presence of the dark matter halo increases the radius of the event horizon and pushes the unstable photon orbits outward, leading to a larger and more distorted black hole shadow. These effects are enhanced for higher black hole spin. Quantitatively, the shadow angular diameter grows with increasing dark matter density normalization and depends sensitively on the inner and outer power-law indices of the halo profile. Using EHT measurements of Sgr A*, the authors place constraints on the dark matter halo parameters, finding that certain combinations of the density scale, power-law slopes, spin, and inclination can reproduce the observed shadow within error bars. Strong correlations (degeneracies) exist between the halo parameters and $a$–$i$, implying that shadow observations alone cannot uniquely determine all parameters without additional priors.
\n\n
暗物质晕的存在增大了事件视界半径，并使不稳定光子轨道外移，导致更大且更扭曲的黑洞阴影。这些效应在黑洞自旋较高时更为显著。定量上，阴影的角直径随暗物质密度归一化参数增大而增大，并对晕轮廓的内外幂律指数敏感。利用EHT对Sgr A*的测量，作者对暗物质晕参数进行了约束，发现密度标度、幂律斜率、自旋和倾角的某些组合可在误差范围内重现观测阴影。暗物质晕参数与 $a$–$i$ 之间存在强相关性（简并性），表明仅靠阴影观测无法在无额外先验条件下唯一确定所有参数。

Conclusion: Black hole shadow observations are a viable probe of dark matter distributions in the vicinity of supermassive black holes. The generalized double power law halo model leaves distinct imprints on the shadow geometry, which can be constrained using current EHT data. However, degeneracies between dark matter parameters, black hole spin, and viewing angle necessitate complementary observations or theoretical priors for precise inference. This work establishes a framework linking dark matter astrophysics to strong-gravity phenomenology via black hole shadows.
\n\n
黑洞阴影观测是探测超大质量黑洞附近暗物质分布的有效手段。广义双幂律晕模型在阴影几何上留下独特印记，可利用当前EHT数据加以约束。然而，暗物质参数、黑洞自旋和观测视角之间的简并性要求结合其他观测或理论先验才能实现精确推断。本工作建立了通过黑洞阴影将暗物质天体物理与强引力现象学相联系的理论框架。

Abstract: The recent black hole shadow observations by the Event Horizon Telescope provide a unique opportunity to probe both the strong gravity regime and the environment surrounding supermassive black holes. Since dark matter halos are expected to exist around galactic-center black holes, they may leave observable signatures on black hole shadows. In this work, we investigate the spacetime structure, photon dynamics, and shadow properties of a rotating black hole immersed in a generalized double power law dark matter halo. Starting from a static dark matter black hole solution, we employ the Newman--Janis algorithm to construct its rotating counterpart and obtain a Kerr-like metric with a dark matter-modified mass function. We analyze the horizon structure, ergosphere, and unstable spherical photon orbits, and find that the dark matter halo generally enlarges the horizon, shifts the photon shell outward, and modifies the black hole shadow. The effects become more pronounced for rapidly rotating black holes. Using the shadow observation of Sgr A*, we further constrain the dark matter halo parameter space and identify correlations among the halo parameters, black hole spin, and observer inclination angle. Our results suggest that black hole shadow observations provide a promising probe of dark matter distributions near supermassive black holes.

</details>


### [82] [Hawking Radiation from the Dymnikova Regular Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08631)
*Milena Skvortsova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper investigates Hawking radiation from the Dymnikova regular black hole, which features a de Sitter core replacing the singularity and asymptotes to Schwarzschild geometry at large distances. The authors compute greybody factors for Standard Model fields and gravitons, finding that while transmission probabilities change modestly near the extremal remnant, the steep drop in Hawking temperature drastically reduces total luminosity. Evaporation slows dramatically as the black hole approaches a cold, near-extremal state asymptotically, implying finite lifetime estimates correspond to cutoff times rather than full evaporation.\n\n
本文研究了Dymnikova正则黑洞的Hawking辐射，该黑洞模型以de Sitter核心取代中心奇点，并在远处渐近于Schwarzschild几何。作者计算了Standard Model场和graviton的greybody因子，发现尽管在接近extremal remnant时传输概率仅有小幅变化，但Hawking温度的急剧下降显著抑制了总光度。黑洞蒸发过程在趋近于cold、near-extremal状态时急剧减缓，因此有限寿命估计应理解为截断时间，而非完全蒸发时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from addressing the physical inconsistencies of classical black hole singularities by studying regular black hole models like Dymnikova’s, which eliminate the singularity via a de Sitter core. Understanding how such modifications affect Hawking radiation—particularly the greybody factors and evaporation dynamics—is crucial for assessing their viability as alternatives to singular black holes and for exploring quantum gravity phenomenology without invoking full quantum gravity.\n\n
动机源于通过研究如Dymnikova黑洞这类正则黑洞模型来解决经典黑洞奇点带来的物理不自洽问题，该模型通过de Sitter核心消除奇点。理解此类几何修正如何影响Hawking辐射——特别是greybody因子和蒸发动力学——对于评估其作为奇点黑洞的替代方案的可行性，以及在不依赖完整量子引力理论的前提下探索量子引力现象学至关重要。

Method: The authors solve the radial wave equations for test fields (photons, light fermions, and gravitons) in the Dymnikova black hole background to compute exact greybody factors via numerical scattering theory. They compare these results with semi-analytic WKB approximations. Using the resulting emission spectra and the known Hawking temperature profile $T_H(M)$, they integrate the energy loss rate $dM/dt = -\\sum_i \\int d\\omega\\, \\omega\\, \\Gamma_i(\\omega, M)\\, n_i(\\omega, T_H)$ to reconstruct the adiabatic evaporation history.\n\n
作者在Dymnikova黑洞背景下求解测试场（光子、轻费米子和graviton）的径向波动方程，通过数值散射理论精确计算greybody因子，并与半解析的WKB近似结果进行比较。利用所得发射谱及已知的Hawking温度轮廓 $T_H(M)$，他们对能量损失率 $dM/dt = -\\sum_i \\int d\\omega\\, \\omega\\, \\Gamma_i(\\omega, M)\\, n_i(\\omega, T_H)$ 进行积分，重构出绝热蒸发历史。

Result: The greybody thresholds shift only slightly as the black hole approaches its cold extremal remnant, indicating modest changes in transmission probabilities. However, the Hawking temperature drops rapidly near extremality, causing a strong suppression of total luminosity. All massless channels (photons, fermions, gravitons) fade near the endpoint, with gravitons remaining subdominant. The photon channel is suppressed more than fermionic ones, leading to an increasingly fermion-dominated residual flux. The evaporation process asymptotically approaches the remnant state, so finite “lifetimes” represent times to reach near-extremality, not complete evaporation.\n\n
当黑洞趋近其cold extremal remnant时，greybody阈值仅发生微小移动，表明传输概率变化不大。然而，Hawking温度在接近extremality时迅速下降，导致总光度被强烈抑制。所有无质量通道（光子、费米子、graviton）在终点附近均衰减，其中graviton始终处于次主导地位。光子通道比费米子通道受到更强的抑制，使得残余通量逐渐由费米子主导。蒸发过程渐近地趋近于remnant态，因此有限的“寿命”代表达到near-extremality的时间，而非完全蒸发的时间。

Conclusion: The Dymnikova regular black hole does not evaporate completely but instead asymptotically approaches a stable cold extremal remnant due to the rapid decline of Hawking temperature. This behavior implies that lifetime estimates should be interpreted as cutoff times to near-extremal configurations. The dominance of fermionic radiation at late stages and the subleading role of gravitons suggest that observational signatures of such remnants would be faint and spectrally distinct from standard evaporating black holes.\n\n
Dymnikova正则黑洞不会完全蒸发，而是由于Hawking温度的快速下降而渐近地趋近于一个稳定的cold extremal remnant。这一行为意味着寿命估计应被解释为达到near-extremal构型的截断时间。晚期费米子辐射的主导地位以及graviton的次主导角色表明，此类remnant的观测信号将非常微弱，且其能谱特征与标准蒸发黑洞明显不同。

Abstract: We study Hawking radiation of the Dymnikova regular black hole. This model replaces the central singularity by a smooth de Sitter core while remaining Schwarzschild-like far from the hole, and its black-hole branch ends in a cold extremal remnant. We compute the greybody factors of the Standard Model test fields and gravitons, compare the precise numerical scattering results with WKB estimates, and use the resulting spectra to estimate an adiabatic evaporation history. The main effect is not a dramatic change in the transmission probabilities: the greybody thresholds move only slightly as the geometry approaches the remnant. Instead, the rapid decrease of the Hawking temperature strongly suppresses the total luminosity. The photon, light-fermion and graviton channels all fade near the endpoint, with the gravitational contribution remaining subdominant. The residual massless flux becomes increasingly fermion dominated because the photon channel is suppressed more efficiently. The black hole approaches the cold remnant only asymptotically, so the quoted lifetime estimates should be interpreted as cutoff times to near-extremal configurations rather than as complete evaporation times.

</details>


### [83] [Forward Construction of Vacuum Initial Data with Borderline Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08716)
*Xuantao Chen,Sergiu Klainerman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper constructs general solutions to the Einstein vacuum constraint equations with minimal or borderline decay at spacelike infinity using the free data formalism of \cite{CK25} and a new gauge condition based on effective uniformization. These solutions are relevant for studying the stability of Minkowski space and will be applied in future work to construct short-pulse Cauchy data leading to trapped surface formation.
\n\n
本文利用\cite{CK25}中发展的free data formalism和一种基于effective uniformization的新规范条件，构造了在spacelike infinity处具有minimal或borderline decay的Einstein真空约束方程的一般解。这些解与Minkowski空间的稳定性研究相关，并将在后续工作中用于构造导致trapped surface形成的short-pulse Cauchy data。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to understand the asymptotic behavior of gravitational fields near spacelike infinity, particularly in scenarios where decay is slower than standard assumptions—such as minimal or borderline decay cases studied in \cite{Shen23, Shen24}. Such configurations are critical for probing the limits of Minkowski stability and for constructing physically relevant initial data that can lead to black hole formation.
\n\n
动机源于理解引力场在spacelike infinity附近渐近行为的需求，特别是在衰减比标准假设更慢的情形下——例如\cite{Shen23, Shen24}中研究的minimal或borderline decay情形。这类构型对于探索Minkowski稳定性极限以及构造能导致黑洞形成的物理相关初始数据至关重要。

Method: The authors employ the free data formalism introduced in \cite{CK25}, which reformulates the constraint equations as an evolution system integrated forward from a finite sphere toward spacelike infinity. They introduce a novel gauge condition derived from effective uniformization theory to control geometric degrees of freedom. This combination enables the construction of solutions without imposing strong decay assumptions at infinity.
\n\n
作者采用\cite{CK25}中引入的free data formalism，将约束方程重新表述为从有限球面向spacelike infinity前向积分的演化系统。他们引入了一种源自effective uniformization理论的新规范条件以控制几何自由度。这种组合使得无需在无穷远处施加强衰减假设即可构造解。

Result: The paper successfully constructs general solutions to the Einstein vacuum constraints exhibiting minimal and borderline decay at spacelike infinity—decay rates previously considered only in specialized contexts. These solutions extend the class of admissible initial data beyond those used in classical stability proofs (e.g., Christodoulou–Klainerman).
\n\n
本文成功构造了在spacelike infinity处具有minimal和borderline decay的Einstein真空约束方程的一般解——此类衰减速率此前仅在特定情境中被考虑。这些解将容许初始数据的类别扩展到了经典稳定性证明（如Christodoulou–Klainerman）所用数据之外。

Conclusion: The developed framework provides a robust method to generate asymptotically weakly decaying initial data for the Einstein equations, bridging geometric analysis with physical questions about gravitational collapse. It sets the stage for constructing short-pulse data that induce trapped surface formation, generalizing results like those of \cite{LiYu}.
\n\n
所发展的框架为生成Einstein方程的渐近弱衰减初始数据提供了稳健方法，连接了几何分析与关于引力坍缩的物理问题。它为构造诱导trapped surface形成的short-pulse数据奠定了基础，推广了如\cite{LiYu}等人的结果。

Abstract: We make use of the free data formalism developed in \cite{CK25} to construct solutions of the Einstein vacuum constraint equations by integrating in the forward direction. This, together with a new gauge condition based on effective uniformization, allows us to construct general solutions with limited decay at spacelike infinity. In particular, we construct solutions with minimal and even borderline decay, as considered in \cite{Shen23}, \cite{Shen24} in connection with the stability of the Minkowski space. In a forthcoming paper, we make use of the techniques we develop here to identify and construct a general class of short-pulse Cauchy data that lead to the formation of trapped surfaces, extending the well-known result of \cite{LiYu}.

</details>


### [84] [Non-Perturbative Bounds on Cosmological Backreaction, the Non-Linear Scale, and Gauge-Invariant Mutual Information from the Matter Power Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08764)
*Bob Osano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper applies a mesoscopic coarse-graining framework to three key issues in Cosmological Perturbation Theory and the backreaction problem: (i) it establishes a non-perturbative lower bound on kinematic backreaction $\QD$ using the Gibbs–Bogoliubov inequality, showing $\QD$ cannot fall below its linear-theory value under stability and temperedness; (ii) it identifies the radius of convergence of the mesoscopic cumulant expansion as $O(\kNL^{-1})$, linking perturbative breakdown to KAM-theorem-like behavior; and (iii) for Gaussian initial conditions, it computes inter-cell mutual information $I(i,j) = -\tfrac{1}{2}\ln(1 - r_{ij}^2)$ directly from the observed power spectrum $P(k)$, yielding a gauge-invariant, data-based measure of backreaction’s impact on the FRW free energy.
\n\n
本文将介观粗粒化框架应用于宇宙学微扰理论和反作用（backreaction）争论中的三个关键问题：(i) 利用Gibbs–Bogoliubov不等式建立了运动学反作用量 $\QD$ 的非微扰下界，表明在满足稳定性与缓增性条件下，$\QD$ 无法低于其线性微扰理论值；(ii) 证明介观累积量展开的收敛半径为 $O(\kNL^{-1})$，将标准微扰理论在 $k > \kNL$ 处的失效归因于类似KAM定理的动力学机制；(iii) 对于高斯物质场，相邻单元间的互信息精确为 $I(i,j) = -\tfrac{1}{2}\ln(1 - r_{ij}^2)$，该量在线性阶规范不变，并可直接从观测到的功率谱 $P(k)$ 计算得出；在 $\Lambda$CDM 模型中，当尺度 $\ell = 50\,\mathrm{Mpc}\,h^{-1}$ 时，最近邻互信息 $I_{\rm NN} \approx 0.10$，总互信息提供了对FRW自由能中反作用修正项的一种可由数据计算的度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to address long-standing ambiguities in the cosmological backreaction debate—specifically whether inhomogeneities significantly affect the global expansion—by introducing rigorous statistical-mechanical tools from mesoscopic coarse-graining. The authors aim to provide non-perturbative bounds, clarify the domain of validity of perturbation theory via dynamical-systems insights (KAM theorem), and construct observable, gauge-invariant diagnostics (mutual information) tied directly to data like $P(k)$.
\n\n
动机在于通过引入来自介观粗粒化框架的严格统计力学工具，解决宇宙学反作用争论中长期存在的模糊性——特别是非均匀性是否显著影响全局膨胀。作者旨在提供非微扰下界，借助动力系统理论（KAM定理）阐明微扰理论的有效范围，并构建可直接关联观测数据（如 $P(k)$）的规范不变诊断量（互信息）。

Method: The method combines the mesoscopic coarse-graining formalism of Osano with techniques from statistical mechanics and information theory. Specifically: (i) the Gibbs–Bogoliubov inequality is applied to derive a lower bound on $\QD$; (ii) the radius of convergence of the cumulant expansion is analyzed and identified with the non-linear scale $\kNL^{-1}$, invoking analogies to the Kolmogorov–Arnold–Moser (KAM) theorem; (iii) for a Gaussian random field, the mutual information between coarse-grained cells is computed analytically using the correlation coefficient $r_{ij}$ derived from $P(k)$, yielding $I(i,j) = -\tfrac{1}{2}\ln(1 - r_{ij}^2)$.
\n\n
方法结合了Osano提出的介观粗粒化形式体系与统计力学及信息论技术：(i) 应用Gibbs–Bogoliubov不等式推导 $\QD$ 的下界；(ii) 分析累积量展开的收敛半径，并将其与非线性尺度 $\kNL^{-1}$ 关联，借助Kolmogorov–Arnold–Moser (KAM) 定理的类比；(iii) 对高斯随机场，利用从 $P(k)$ 导出的相关系数 $r_{ij}$ 解析计算粗粒化单元间的互信息，得到 $I(i,j) = -\tfrac{1}{2}\ln(1 - r_{ij}^2)$。

Result: Three main results are obtained: (i) a rigorous lower bound $\QD \geq \QD^{\text{(linear)}}$ under stability and temperedness, implying backreaction cannot be arbitrarily suppressed; (ii) the cumulant expansion converges only for scales larger than $\kNL^{-1}$, explaining the failure of standard perturbation theory beyond the non-linear scale via a KAM-type mechanism; (iii) mutual information between neighboring cells is computable from $P(k)$, gauge-invariant at linear order, and for $\Lambda$CDM at $\ell = 50\,\mathrm{Mpc}\,h^{-1}$ gives $I_{\rm NN} \approx 0.10$, offering a data-driven measure of backreaction’s thermodynamic effect.
\n\n
获得三项主要结果：(i) 在稳定性与缓增性条件下，严格下界 $\QD \geq \QD^{\text{(linear)}}$ 成立，表明反作用无法被任意压低；(ii) 累积量展开仅在大于 $\kNL^{-1}$ 的尺度上收敛，通过KAM型机制解释了标准微扰理论在非线性尺度以外的失效；(iii) 相邻单元间的互信息可由 $P(k)$ 计算，在线性阶规范不变，在 $\Lambda$CDM 模型中当 $\ell = 50\,\mathrm{Mpc}\,h^{-1}$ 时 $I_{\rm NN} \approx 0.10$，为反作用对热力学自由能的修正提供了数据驱动的度量。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that mesoscopic statistical mechanics provides powerful, non-perturbative tools to constrain and quantify cosmological backreaction. The Gibbs–Bogoliubov bound rules out strong suppression scenarios, the KAM-inspired convergence analysis clarifies the limits of perturbation theory, and mutual information offers a novel, observable link between inhomogeneities and effective cosmological dynamics. While the gauge invariance holds only at linear order and the bound relies on a conjecture, the framework opens a path toward data-constrained, theoretically robust backreaction estimates.
\n\n
本文表明，介观统计力学为约束和量化宇宙学反作用提供了强有力的非微扰工具：Gibbs–Bogoliubov下界排除了反作用被强烈压低的情形；受KAM启发的收敛性分析澄清了微扰理论的适用极限；而互信息则为非均匀性与有效宇宙学动力学之间建立了新颖的、可观测的联系。尽管规范不变性仅在线性阶成立，且下界依赖于一个明确的猜想，但该框架为实现数据约束下、理论自洽的反作用估计开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We apply the mesoscopic coarse-graining framework of~\cite{OsanoMeso,OsanoExtensivity,OsanoPerturbation} to three problems in Cosmological Perturbation Theory and the backreaction debate. \textbf{(i)}~A non-perturbative lower bound on the kinematic backreaction $\QD$ in the Buchert equations, derived from the Gibbs--Bogoliubov inequality: $\QD$ cannot be suppressed below its linear-perturbation-theoryvalue, regardless of the degree of non-linearity, provided the system satisfies stability and temperedness. \textbf{(ii)}~The radius of convergence of the mesoscopic cumulant expansion equals $O(\kNL^{-1})$, the non-linear scale of the matter power spectrum, providing a KAM-theorem explanation for why standard perturbation theory fails at $k>\kNL$. \textbf{(iii)}~For a Gaussian matter field, the inter-cell mutual information is exactly $I(i,j)=-\tfrac{1}{2}\ln(1-r_{ij}^2)$, gauge-invariant at linear order and computable directly from the observed $P(k)$; for $Λ$CDM at $\ell=50\,\mathrm{Mpc}\,h^{-1}$, $I_{\rm NN}\approx 0.10$.The total mutual information gives a data-computable measure of the backreaction correction to the FRW free energy. The gauge proof holds at linear order; the KAM identification is exact; the backreaction bound rests on a stated conjecture.

</details>


### [85] [Line-of-sight acceleration in compact binaries with higher harmonics and eccentricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08838)
*Soumen Roy,Justin Janquart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper re-derives and consistently implements line-of-sight acceleration effects—induced by dynamical or AGN environments—into both quasi-circular (with precession and higher-order modes) and eccentric gravitational-waveform models. Applying this to GWTC-3 events, the authors find no significant evidence for such acceleration but caution that inconsistent treatment across harmonics can bias inference. The work establishes a robust framework for future environmental studies of compact binary mergers.
\n\n
本文重新推导并一致地将视线方向加速度效应（由动力学或活动星系核环境引起）纳入准圆轨道（包含进动和高阶模）及偏心引力波波形模型中。将该模型应用于GWTC-3事件，作者未发现此类加速度的显著证据，但指出在各谐波间不一致处理会导致推断偏差。该工作为未来致密双星并合环境特征的研究建立了稳健框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The astrophysical origin of compact binary mergers is still uncertain, with possible formation channels including isolated binary evolution, dynamical interactions in dense stellar clusters, or formation in active galactic nuclei (AGN). Binaries in dense or accelerating environments experience line-of-sight acceleration ($a_{\\parallel}$), which modulates the observed gravitational-wave phase. To reliably probe these environmental signatures, it is essential to model $a_{\\parallel}$ consistently across all waveform components, especially higher-order modes and eccentric harmonics, which prior works often treated inconsistently or neglected.
\n\n
致密双星并合的天体物理起源仍不确定，可能的形成通道包括孤立双星演化、致密星团中的动力学相互作用，或在活动星系核（AGN）中形成。处于致密或加速环境中的双星会经历视线方向加速度（$a_{\\parallel}$），这会调制观测到的引力波相位。为了可靠地探测这些环境特征，必须在所有波形成分（尤其是高阶模和偏心谐波）中一致地建模 $a_{\\parallel}$，而以往工作常对此处理不一致或忽略。

Method: The authors re-derive the line-of-sight acceleration correction to the gravitational-wave phase, ensuring consistency across all contributing harmonics. They implement this correction in two classes of waveform models: (1) state-of-the-art quasi-circular models that include spin precession and higher-order spherical harmonic modes beyond the dominant $(\\ell, m) = (2,2)$ mode, and (2) eccentric waveform models where multiple orbital harmonics contribute. The implementation modifies the time-frequency mapping via an effective Doppler shift: $\\phi(f) \\to \\phi(f) + \\frac{2\\pi a_{\\parallel}}{c H_0} f t_c$, with consistent application to each harmonic frequency. They then perform Bayesian parameter estimation on selected GWTC-3 events using this augmented model.
\n\n
作者重新推导了视线方向加速度对引力波相位的修正，确保在所有贡献谐波中保持一致性。他们将该修正实现在两类波形模型中：(1) 包含自旋进动和高于主导 $(\\ell, m) = (2,2)$ 模的高阶球谐模的先进准圆轨道模型；(2) 多个轨道谐波均有贡献的偏心波形模型。该实现通过有效多普勒频移修改时频映射：$\\phi(f) \\to \\phi(f) + \\frac{2\\pi a_{\\parallel}}{c H_0} f t_c$，并对每个谐波频率一致应用。随后，他们使用此增强模型对选定的GWTC-3事件进行贝叶斯参数估计。

Result: Applying their consistent waveform model to several GWTC-3 events, the authors find no statistically significant evidence for non-zero line-of-sight acceleration ($a_{\\parallel} \\approx 0$ within uncertainties). Crucially, they demonstrate that if $a_{\\parallel}$ is applied only to the dominant $(2,2)$ mode while neglecting its effect on higher harmonics, the inferred acceleration can be biased, potentially leading to false detections or misinterpretations of environmental effects.
\n\n
将他们的一致性波形模型应用于多个GWTC-3事件，作者未发现非零视线方向加速度的统计显著证据（$a_{\\parallel} \\approx 0$，误差范围内）。关键的是，他们证明：若仅将 $a_{\\parallel}$ 应用于主导的 $(2,2)$ 模而忽略其对高阶谐波的影响，所推断的加速度会出现偏差，可能导致环境效应的虚假探测或误释。

Conclusion: A consistent treatment of line-of-sight acceleration across all waveform harmonics is essential for unbiased inference of environmental effects in compact binary mergers. While current LIGO-Virgo data show no compelling evidence for such acceleration, the developed framework provides a reliable tool for future analyses with more sensitive detectors (e.g., Cosmic Explorer, Einstein Telescope) or louder events, enabling stringent tests of formation channels involving dense or accelerating environments like AGN disks or globular clusters.
\n\n
在所有波形谐波中一致处理视线方向加速度对于无偏推断致密双星并合的环境效应至关重要。尽管当前LIGO-Virgo数据未显示此类加速度的有力证据，但所发展的框架为未来利用更高灵敏度探测器（如Cosmic Explorer、Einstein Telescope）或更强信号事件的分析提供了可靠工具，可对涉及活动星系核盘或球状星团等致密或加速环境的形成通道进行严格检验。

Abstract: Direct detections of gravitational waves provide a unique opportunity to probe the astrophysical origin of compact binary mergers. The formation channels of these systems remain highly debated, and a fraction may originate in dynamical environments or active galactic nuclei. Binaries formed in such environments are expected to experience line-of-sight acceleration from their surroundings, which can imprint characteristic signatures on the observed gravitational-wave signal. Here, we re-derive the line-of-sight acceleration effects and implement them in state-of-the-art quasi-circular waveform models with precession and higher-order modes. We also implement the corrections in eccentric waveform models, applying them consistently to all contributing harmonics. Using this model, we investigate the impact of these effects on the inference of line-of-sight acceleration and analyze a few interesting GWTC events observed during the third observing run of LIGO and Virgo. We find no substantial evidence for line-of-sight acceleration in these events. We also show that an inconsistent treatment of line-of-sight acceleration between higher harmonics can lead to biased conclusions. Our model provides a robust framework for uncovering line-of-sight acceleration in current and future gravitational-wave observations, enabling more accurate probes of environmental signatures in compact-binary formation.

</details>


### [86] [Electromagnetism from two matter spaces: mutual helicity and the nondegenerate completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08917)
*Hyeong-Chan Kim*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper demonstrates that a generic nondegenerate Maxwell field can be reconstructed by coupling two independent degenerate matter-space flows, each yielding a self-degenerate field $G^{(I)}$ with $G^{(I)} \wedge G^{(I)} = 0$. The full electromagnetic field strength is $F = G^{(1)} + G^{(2)}$, and the nondegeneracy condition $F \wedge F \neq 0$ arises from the mutual helicity term $2G^{(1)} \wedge G^{(2)}$, which vanishes in the single-flow formulation. A variational principle over both flows yields the sourced Maxwell equations when the sector kernels intersect trivially.

\n\n
本文表明，一般的非退化Maxwell场可以通过耦合两个独立的退化matter-space流来重构，每个流产生一个自退化场 $G^{(I)}$，满足 $G^{(I)} \wedge G^{(I)} = 0$。完整的电磁场强为 $F = G^{(1)} + G^{(2)}$，其非退化条件 $F \wedge F \neq 0$ 源于互螺旋度项 $2G^{(1)} \wedge G^{(2)}$，该量在单流表述中恒为零。当两个扇区的核（kernels）平凡相交时，对两个流的变分原理可导出含源Maxwell方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The one-flow matter-space formulation of electromagnetism inherently restricts the field strength to satisfy $F \wedge F = 0$, thereby excluding generic (nondegenerate) Maxwell fields that carry both electric and magnetic components in a non-null configuration. This limitation motivates the search for a minimal extension that retains the geometric elegance of the matter-space framework while recovering the full dynamics of classical electromagnetism.

\n\n
单流matter-space电磁理论本质上要求场强满足 $F \wedge F = 0$，从而排除了携带电场与磁场非零混合分量的一般（非退化）Maxwell场。这一局限性促使作者寻求一种最小扩展方案，在保留matter-space框架几何优雅性的同时，恢复经典电磁学的完整动力学。

Method: The authors introduce two independent matter-space flows, each defining a degenerate two-form field $G^{(I)}$ as the pull-back of a two-form from a three-dimensional matter space. The total electromagnetic field is constructed as $F = G^{(1)} + G^{(2)}$. They analyze the algebraic structure of $F \wedge F$, decompose helicity into self- and mutual contributions, and formulate a joint variational principle where independent variations with respect to each flow yield the full sourced Maxwell equations under the condition that the kernels of the two sector maps intersect only at zero.

\n\n
作者引入两个独立的matter-space流，每个流将三维matter-space上的二形式拉回为一个退化场 $G^{(I)}$。总电磁场构造为 $F = G^{(1)} + G^{(2)}$。他们分析了 $F \wedge F$ 的代数结构，将螺旋度分解为自螺旋度与互螺旋度，并建立联合变分原理：当两个扇区映射的核（kernels）仅在零处相交时，对每个流的独立变分共同导出完整的含源Maxwell方程。

Result: The decomposition $F = G^{(1)} + G^{(2)}$ successfully reconstructs generic Maxwell fields, with $F \wedge F = 2 G^{(1)} \wedge G^{(2)}$ encoding the previously missing invariant. The total helicity splits into two self-helicity terms (each closed) and a mutual helicity whose exterior derivative gives $F \wedge F$. The variational principle over both flows yields $\mathrm{d} \star F = J$ provided the sector kernels intersect trivially, thus recovering standard sourced Maxwell theory within the extended matter-space framework.

\n\n
分解 $F = G^{(1)} + G^{(2)}$ 成功重构了一般Maxwell场，其中 $F \wedge F = 2 G^{(1)} \wedge G^{(2)}$ 编码了原先缺失的不变量。总螺旋度分为两个闭的自螺旋度项和一个互螺旋度项，其外微分为 $F \wedge F$。当扇区核平凡相交时，双流变分原理导出 $\mathrm{d} \star F = J$，从而在扩展的matter-space框架内恢复了标准含源Maxwell理论。

Conclusion: Generic electromagnetism is not fundamentally incompatible with the matter-space paradigm; rather, it emerges as the minimal coupled theory of two degenerate sectors. The key physical insight is that nondegeneracy—and hence the full richness of Maxwell theory—arises from mutual helicity between two otherwise degenerate flows. This provides a geometrically natural and variational formulation of classical electrodynamics beyond the null-field restriction.

\n\n
一般电磁学并非与matter-space范式根本不相容；相反，它作为两个退化扇区的最小耦合理论自然涌现。核心物理洞见在于：非退化性（以及Maxwell理论的全部丰富结构）源于两个原本退化的流之间的互螺旋度。这为超越零场限制的经典电动力学提供了一个几何上自然且具有变分结构的表述。

Abstract: We show that generic Maxwell fields can be represented within the matter-space framework by introducing two independent matter-space flows. In the one-flow formulation the electromagnetic field strength is the pull-back of a two-form on a three-dimensional matter space and therefore satisfies $F\wedge F=0$, so that a single flow captures only a degenerate, helicity-carrying sector. The minimal completion is obtained by writing $$F=G^{(1)}+G^{(2)},$$ where each sector field $G^{(I)}$ is the pull-back of a matter-space two-form from an independent flow. Each sector is individually degenerate, $G^{(I)}\wedge G^{(I)}=0$, while the full field satisfies $F\wedge F=2G^{(1)}\wedge G^{(2)}$; the invariant excluded by the one-flow theory is thus recovered as an inter-flow quantity. We interpret this structure in terms of mutual helicity: the total helicity decomposes into two self-helicity contributions and a mutual term whose exterior derivative is $F\wedge F$. Hence a configuration with vanishing mutual helicity lies in the degenerate sector, whereas a nondegenerate Maxwell field necessarily carries a nontrivial mutual helicity. A variational principle for the total field recovers the sourced Maxwell equation, the two matter-space variations combining into the full equation whenever the sector kernels intersect trivially. Generic electromagnetism is thereby reconstructed as the minimal coupled completion of two individually degenerate matter-space sectors.

</details>

Main category: gr-qc

Method: The paper adopts a comparative theoretical analysis: it first outlines the structural limitations of QFTCS, then reviews key features and challenges of leading QG approaches, and finally introduces the teleparallel equivalent of general relativity (TEGR) formulated in terms of the coframe field $e^a_\mu$ and the Lorentz spin connection $\omega^{ab}_\mu$. In this framework, spacetime curvature vanishes ($R^{ab}{}_{\mu\nu} = 0$), and gravity is mediated by torsion $T^a{}_{\mu\nu} = \partial_\mu e^a_\nu - \partial_\nu e^a_\mu + \omega^a{}_{b\mu} e^b_\nu - \omega^a{}_{b\nu} e^b_\mu$. The authors emphasize the natural incorporation of local Lorentz symmetry and minimal coupling to fermions.

本文采用比较性理论分析方法：首先概述 QFTCS 的结构性局限，然后回顾主流量子引力方法的关键特征与挑战，最后引入以余标架场 $e^a_\mu$ 和洛伦兹旋量联络 $\omega^{ab}_\mu$ 表述的广义相对论的远平行等价形式（TEGR）。在此框架中，时空曲率为零（$R^{ab}{}_{\mu\nu} = 0$），引力由挠率 $T^a{}_{\mu\nu} = \\partial_\\mu e^a_\\nu - \\partial_\\nu e^a_\\mu + \\omega^a{}_{b\\mu} e^b_\\nu - \\omega^a{}_{b\\nu} e^b_\\mu$ 传递。作者强调该框架自然地包含了局域洛伦兹对称性，并可实现与费米子的最小耦合。

Result: The main result is a conceptual clarification: the coframe/spin-connection formulation provides a geometrically transparent and gauge-compatible description of gravity that avoids metric-based curvature and naturally couples to fermionic fields. Conclusion: While not presenting a full quantization, the paper concludes that teleparallel gravity—formulated via coframe and spin connection—offers a geometrically refined and physically motivated starting point for quantum gravity. Its torsion-based structure, compatibility with local Lorentz symmetry, and natural fermion coupling make it a compelling alternative to curvature-based approaches, potentially circumventing some foundational obstacles in current QG programs.

尽管未提出完整的量子化方案，本文得出结论：通过余标架和旋量联络表述的远平行引力为量子引力提供了一个几何上更精细且物理动机明确的起点。其基于挠率的结构、与局域洛伦兹对称性的兼容性以及对费米子的自然耦合，使其成为曲率方法之外的一个引人注目的替代方案，可能规避当前量子引力方案中的一些基础性障碍。

Abstract: We revisit quantum field theory in curved spacetime (QFTCS) as a semi-classical framework for quantum matter on classical geometries, emphasizing its limitations, including vacuum ambiguity and background dependence. We briefly review major approaches to quantum gravity (QG), including Loop Quantum Gravity (LQG), string theory, and asymptotic safety, highlighting their conceptual challenges. Motivated by these issues, we outline a teleparallel framework based on coframe and spin-connection variables, where gravity is encoded in torsion rather than curvature. This framework naturally incorporates local Lorentz symmetry and fermionic couplings while displaying a gauge-like structure. We argue that the coframe/spin-connection pair provides an alternative and geometrically refined description of gravitational variables, which may serve as a useful starting point for future investigations of QG. The purpose of this work is not to provide a complete quantization of teleparallel gravity but to identify the geometric and conceptual ingredients that such a formulation would require.

动机在于理解奇异引力扇区——特别是对 2+1 维引力的 Kalb-Ramond（KR）修正——如何影响旋转黑洞周围定态标量云的形成与结构，尤其是在存在非平凡边界条件（如 Robin 型）的情况下。超辐射阈值处的标量云可作为探测广义相对论之外微妙引力物理的探针。

Method: The authors solve the Klein-Gordon equation for a massive scalar field in the background of a rotating KR-BTZ black hole with Robin boundary conditions at asymptotic infinity. They impose the superradiant threshold condition $ω = mΩ_H$ to find bound-state solutions (scalar clouds). The dependence of cloud existence on the KR parameter and Robin coefficient is analyzed. 作者在旋转 KR-BTZ 黑洞背景下求解具有 Robin 边界条件的渐近无穷远处的 Klein-Gordon 方程，施加超辐射阈值条件 $ω = mΩ_H$ 以寻找束缚态解（标量云）。分析了云的存在性对 KR 参数和 Robin 系数的依赖关系，并计算了准正规模（QNMs）和穿过视界的能量流作为一致性检验。

The KR parameter shifts the critical Robin parameter required for cloud formation. At the superradiant threshold, QNMs are purely real (non-damping), and the horizon energy flux changes sign, confirming the physical relevance of these clouds.

Conclusion: Stationary scalar clouds around rotating KR-BTZ black holes are highly sensitive to both the KR modification of gravity and the choice of Robin boundary conditions. Abstract: We investigate the scalar clouds around a rotating Kalb-Ramond (KR) BTZ black hole under Robin boundary conditions. Quasinormal modes (QNMs) and horizon fluxes are further used as consistency checks, confirming that the cloud solutions correspond to non-damping modes at the superradiant threshold where the energy flux changes sign. The KR parameter also shifts the critical Robin parameter at which the clouds exist. These results establish stationary scalar clouds as sensitive probes of the interplay between the Robin boundary conditions and KR gravity.

### [89] [Linear Ricci-Trace Deformations and Operational Equivalence in Rastall-Type Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09819)
*José A. C. Nogales,Karen-Luz Burgoa Rosso,Marcelo H. Alavarenga*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper provides a structural analysis of linear Ricci–trace deformations of Einstein’s equations, clarifying parameter equivalences, Newtonian calibration constraints, and distinctions from Unimodular Gravity, resulting in a compact operational classification of Rastall-type models.
\n\n
本文对爱因斯坦场方程的一类线性Ricci–trace形变进行了结构性分析，澄清了参数等价性、牛顿校准约束以及与Unimodular Gravity的区别，从而给出了Rastall型模型的紧凑操作性分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to resolve ambiguities in the literature on Rastall gravity regarding parameter definitions, algebraic vs. operational equivalence of formulations, and the physical meaning of deformations that modify the relative weight between the Ricci tensor $R_{\mu\nu}$ and scalar curvature trace $R$, while keeping the metric as the sole gravitational field variable. The goal is theoretical clarity, not phenomenological fitting.
\n\n
动机在于解决Rastall引力文献中关于参数定义、不同表述的代数等价性与操作等价性之间的混淆，以及在仅以度规为引力场变量的前提下，改变Ricci张量$R_{\mu\nu}$与标量曲率迹$R$之间相对权重的形变所具有的物理含义。目标是理论澄清，而非唯象拟合。

Method: The authors perform an algebraic and operational analysis of two common parametrizations of Ricci–trace deformed field equations: $(1-\varepsilon)R_{\mu\nu}-\frac{1}{2}g_{\mu\nu}R=\kappa_\varepsilon T_{\mu\nu}$ and $R_{\mu\nu}-\frac{1-\lambda}{2}g_{\mu\nu}R=\kappa_\lambda T_{\mu\nu}$. They examine the conditions under which these are equivalent, impose Newtonian calibration (fixing the measured Newton constant $G_N$ and laboratory stress tensor $T_{\mu\nu}$), derive the effective conserved source, analyze the FLRW perfect-fluid cosmology, and compare the framework to Unimodular Gravity (UG) by contrasting their variational origins.
\n\n
作者对两类常见的Ricci–trace形变场方程进行了代数与操作性分析：$(1-\varepsilon)R_{\mu\nu}-\frac{1}{2}g_{\mu\nu}R=\kappa_\varepsilon T_{\mu\nu}$ 和 $R_{\mu\nu}-\frac{1-\lambda}{2}g_{\mu\nu}R=\kappa_\lambda T_{\mu\nu}$。他们考察了二者等价的条件，在固定测量所得的牛顿常数$G_N$和实验室应力张量$T_{\mu\nu}$（即牛顿校准）下研究参数映射，并推导有效守恒源、分析FLRW完美流体宇宙学，最后通过变分原理的差异将该框架与Unimodular Gravity (UG)进行对比。

Result: The two parametrizations are algebraically isomorphic only if both the deformation parameter ($\varepsilon \leftrightarrow \lambda$) and the bare coupling ($\kappa_\varepsilon \leftrightarrow \kappa_\lambda$) are transformed together. However, under fixed physical conditions (same $T_{\mu\nu}$ and $G_N$), this map reduces to a passive reparametrization only at the Einstein point ($\varepsilon = \lambda = 0$). The $\lambda$-formulation is identified as the standard Rastall equation. The effective source $T_{\mu\nu}^{\text{eff}}$ is conserved due to the contracted Bianchi identity. The FLRW equations for perfect fluids are derived, and degenerate cases (vacuum, trace-free matter, radiation, dust, and the singular traceless point $\lambda=1$) are classified. Crucially, Ricci–trace deformations are distinguished from Unimodular Gravity: UG arises from a restricted variational principle with fixed $\sqrt{-g}$, yielding $\Lambda$ as an integration constant, whereas Ricci–trace models are algebraic modifications without such a variational basis.
\n\n
两种参数化仅在同时变换形变参数（$\varepsilon \leftrightarrow \lambda$）和裸耦合常数（$\kappa_\varepsilon \leftrightarrow \kappa_\lambda$）时才代数同构。然而，在固定物理条件（相同的$T_{\mu\nu}$和$G_N$）下，该映射仅在爱因斯坦点（$\varepsilon = \lambda = 0$）退化为被动重参数化。$\lambda$-表述被确认为标准Rastall方程。由于缩并Bianchi恒等式，有效源$T_{\mu\nu}^{\text{eff}}$是守恒的。作者推导了完美流体的FLRW方程，并对退化情形（真空、无迹物质、辐射、尘埃及奇异无迹点$\lambda=1$）进行了分类。关键的是，Ricci–trace形变与Unimodular Gravity被明确区分：UG源于对$\sqrt{-g}$固定的受限变分原理，导致宇宙学常数$\Lambda$作为积分常数出现，而Ricci–trace模型则是无变分基础的代数修改。

Conclusion: The paper establishes a clear operational taxonomy for Rastall-type Ricci–trace gravity models, showing that apparent algebraic equivalences do not imply physical equivalence once empirical constraints (like Newtonian calibration) are imposed. It resolves parameter ambiguities in the literature and rigorously separates these models from Unimodular Gravity, emphasizing that Ricci–trace deformations are non-variational algebraic modifications of Einstein’s equations.
\n\n
本文建立了Rastall型Ricci–trace引力模型的清晰操作性分类，表明一旦施加经验约束（如牛顿校准），表面上的代数等价性并不意味着物理等价性。它解决了文献中的参数模糊性，并严格将此类模型与Unimodular Gravity区分开来，强调Ricci–trace形变是对爱因斯坦方程的非变分代数修改。

Abstract: We analyze a class of linear Ricci--trace deformations of Einstein's field equations in which the relative weight between the Ricci tensor and the scalar-curvature trace sector is modified while the metric remains the only gravitational field. The purpose of the analysis is structural rather than phenomenological: we classify the corresponding field-equation class, fix the parameter dictionaries commonly used in the Rastall-gravity literature, and identify which equivalence statements survive after Newtonian calibration. We show that two frequently used parametrizations, \[ (1-ε)R_{μν}-\frac12 g_{μν}R=κ_εT_{μν}, \qquad R_{μν}-\frac{1-λ}{2}g_{μν}R=κ_λT_{μν}, \] are algebraically isomorphic only if both the deformation parameter and the bare gravitational coupling are transformed simultaneously. This algebraic equivalence, however, is not automatically an operational equivalence. Once the same laboratory stress tensor and the same measured Newton constant are fixed, the parameter map is a passive reparametrization only at the Einstein point. We further identify the \(λ\)-representative with the standard Rastall equation, clarify the role of the conserved effective source, derive the corresponding FLRW perfect-fluid sector, and discuss degenerate cases including vacuum, trace-free matter, radiation, dust, and the singular traceless point. Finally, we distinguish the Ricci--trace class from Unimodular Gravity (UG): although both involve the trace sector, UG follows from a restricted variational principle and produces the cosmological constant as an integration constant, rather than from an algebraic Ricci--trace deformation. The result is a compact operational classification of Rastall-type Ricci--trace models.

</details>

*Chaoyi Yang,Neev Khera,Dongjun Li,Huan Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper develops a modified Teukolsky formalism to compute gravitational wave fluxes for a point particle inspiraling into a Schwarzschild black hole in higher-derivative gravity (e.g., cubic gravity), laying groundwork for extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) waveform modeling in modified gravity.

本文发展了一种修正的Teukolsky形式体系，用于计算在高阶导数引力理论（例如立方引力）中，点粒子旋进Schwarzschild黑洞所产生的引力波通量，为在修改引力理论中构建极端质量比旋进（EMRI）波形奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higher-derivative theories of gravity modify both the black hole background and gravitational wave dynamics compared to General Relativity. To enable tests of such theories with future gravitational wave observations—particularly from EMRIs—it is essential to develop tools to compute accurate waveforms in these modified theories.

高阶导数引力理论会同时改变黑洞背景时空和引力波的动力学行为，与广义相对论（General Relativity）不同。为了利用未来的引力波观测（尤其是来自极端质量比旋进系统EMRI的信号）检验这些修改引力理论，亟需发展能在这些理论中精确计算波形的工具。

Method: The authors generalize the Teukolsky formalism—a perturbative framework for computing gravitational radiation in black hole spacetimes—to accommodate higher-derivative corrections to the field equations. They apply this modified formalism to a non-rotating (Schwarzschild) black hole background in a specific cubic gravity theory, treating the inspiraling point particle as a distributional source, and compute energy fluxes at the horizon and null infinity.

作者将Teukolsky形式体系（一种用于计算黑洞时空中引力辐射的微扰框架）推广到包含场方程中的高阶导数修正的情形。他们将这一修正后的方法应用于特定立方引力理论中的非旋转（Schwarzschild）黑洞背景，将旋进的点粒子视为分布源，并计算了黑洞视界和类光无穷远（null infinity）处的能量通量。

Result: The paper successfully computes the gravitational wave energy fluxes to the horizon and null infinity for a point particle inspiraling into a Schwarzschild black hole in cubic gravity using the extended Teukolsky equation. The formalism is explicitly constructed to be generalizable to rotating (Kerr) black holes.
\n\n
本文利用扩展后的Teukolsky方程，成功计算了立方引力理论中点粒子旋进Schwarzschild黑洞所产生的引力波在视界和类光无穷远处的能量通量。该形式体系被明确设计为可自然推广至旋转（Kerr）黑洞情形。

Conclusion: This work provides a foundational framework for constructing EMRI waveforms in higher-derivative gravity theories. Such waveforms can not only probe strong-field deviations from General Relativity but may also be rescaled to approximate signals from comparable-mass binary black hole mergers, extending existing GR-based phenomenological strategies to modified gravity.

本工作为在高阶导数引力理论中构建EMRI波形提供了基础性框架。这类波形不仅能探测强场区域对广义相对论的偏离，还可通过重新标度近似描述等质量双黑洞并合信号，从而将现有基于广义相对论的现象学策略拓展至修改引力理论。

Abstract: In this work, we study a model problem involving a point particle inspiraling into a non-rotating black hole in higher-derivative theories of gravity. In such theories, both the background spacetime and the generation and propagation of gravitational waves differ from those in General Relativity. We develop a modified Teukolsky formalism to describe gravitational waves sourced by the point particle and, as an illustrative example, compute the resulting fluxes to the black hole horizon and null infinity for a cubic gravity theory. The formalism is constructed in a way that can be naturally extended to rotating black holes. These results represent essential steps to build extreme mass-ratio-inspiral waveforms in modified gravity theories, which may also be rescaled to approximate waveforms from comparable-mass binary black hole systems, analogous to existing approaches in General Relativity.

</details>


### [91] [Neutron stars in Poincaré gauge gravity with quadratic torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09786)
*Chaitanya Vashistha,Radouane Gannouji,Apratim Ganguly*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper investigates static neutron stars in a Poincaré gauge gravity theory with non-propagating torsion governed by parity-even and parity-odd quadratic torsion terms. The torsion is algebraically solved via the contorsion equation using the spin current of a Weyssenhoff fluid obeying the Frenkel condition. The resulting metric field equations resemble Einstein's equations with an effective fluid that includes spin-squared corrections. The authors derive modified Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff (TOV) equations incorporating these corrections and solve them numerically with the DD2 equation of state, finding that positive spin-spin coupling leads to more compact stars, reduced maximum mass, and lower binding energy compared to general relativity, while spin-anisotropy effects are negligible for smooth polarization profiles.
\n\n
本文研究了在包含宇称偶和宇称奇二次挠率不变量的Poincaré规范引力代数分支中的静态中子星。由于挠率是非传播的，其伴随方程是代数的，并可通过满足Frenkel条件的Weyssenhoff流体的自旋流求解。度规场方程约化为带有有效流体源的普通黎曼爱因斯坦方程，该有效流体包含自旋平方修正项。作者推导出有效能量密度、径向压强和切向压强，并考虑了各向同性和各向异性的自旋关联。与Einstein–Cartan理论不同，有效自旋-自旋相互作用的系数并非固定，而是依赖于无量纲的二次挠率耦合常数。在Einstein–Cartan极限下，使用度规定义的应力-能量张量，未极化的自旋贡献给出 $w_{\\mathrm{spin}}=-1/3$。作者进一步推导了相应的修正Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff（TOV）方程，并使用DD2物态方程进行数值求解。对于本文考虑的正有效自旋-自旋耦合分支，挠率修正使恒星结构更致密，最大质量降低，结合能相对于广义相对论序列减小；而对于平滑弱极化轮廓，自旋关联各向异性对质量-半径关系的影响可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from extending gravitational theories beyond general relativity by incorporating torsion within the framework of Poincaré gauge gravity (PGG), particularly focusing on its implications for compact objects like neutron stars. Unlike Einstein–Cartan theory where torsion is algebraic but yields fixed spin-spin couplings, this work explores a broader class of PGG models with quadratic torsion invariants—both parity-even and parity-odd—allowing variable effective spin interactions. Understanding how such torsion-induced corrections affect stellar structure provides a testbed for alternative gravity theories in strong-field regimes.
\n\n
动机源于在Poincaré规范引力（PGG）框架内通过引入挠率来拓展广义相对论之外的引力理论，特别关注其对中子星等致密天体的影响。与Einstein–Cartan理论中挠率为代数且导致固定自旋-自旋耦合不同，本工作探索了一类更广泛的PGG模型，其中包含宇称偶和宇称奇的二次挠率不变量，从而允许可变的有效自旋相互作用。理解此类由挠率引起的修正如何影响恒星结构，为强场区域中的替代理论提供了检验平台。

Method: The authors consider an algebraic sector of Poincaré gauge gravity where torsion does not propagate, so the contorsion tensor $K_{\mu\nu}{}^\rho$ is determined algebraically from the spin current $\sigma_{\mu\nu}{}^\rho$ of a Weyssenhoff fluid satisfying the Frenkel condition ($\sigma_{\mu\nu}{}^\rho u^\nu = 0$). Substituting this solution into the metric field equations yields effective Einstein equations: $$G_{\mu\nu} = 8\pi G \left( T_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{fluid}} + T_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{spin}} \right),$$ where $T_{\mu\nu}^{\mathrm{spin}}$ contains quadratic-in-spin contributions dependent on dimensionless torsion couplings $\beta_i$. They compute the effective energy density $\rho_{\mathrm{eff}}$, radial pressure $p_r^{\mathrm{eff}}$, and tangential pressure $p_t^{\mathrm{eff}}$, allowing for both isotropic and anisotropic spin correlations. The modified Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff (TOV) equations are then derived under spherical symmetry and solved numerically using the DD2 nuclear equation of state.
\n\n
作者考虑Poincaré规范引力的一个代数分支，其中挠率不传播，因此伴随张量 $K_{\\mu\\nu}{}^\\rho$ 可通过满足Frenkel条件（$\\sigma_{\\mu\\nu}{}^\\rho u^\\nu = 0$）的Weyssenhoff流体的自旋流 $\\sigma_{\\mu\\nu}{}^\\rho$ 代数地确定。将此解代入度规场方程后，得到有效爱因斯坦方程：$$G_{\\mu\\nu} = 8\\pi G \\left( T_{\\mu\\nu}^{\\mathrm{fluid}} + T_{\\mu\\nu}^{\\mathrm{spin}} \\right),$$ 其中 $T_{\\mu\\nu}^{\\mathrm{spin}}$ 包含依赖于无量纲挠率耦合常数 $\\beta_i$ 的自旋平方项。他们计算了有效能量密度 $\\rho_{\\mathrm{eff}}$、径向压强 $p_r^{\\mathrm{eff}}$ 和切向压强 $p_t^{\\mathrm{eff}}$，并允许各向同性和各向异性的自旋关联。随后在球对称假设下推导出修正的Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff（TOV）方程，并使用DD2核物态方程进行数值求解。

Result: For positive values of the effective spin-spin coupling (determined by the quadratic-torsion parameters), the torsion corrections lead to more compact neutron star configurations, a reduction in the maximum mass (compared to the general-relativistic case), and decreased binding energy. In the Einstein–Cartan limit (where only specific torsion couplings are retained), the unpolarized spin fluid contributes an equation-of-state parameter $w_{\mathrm{spin}} = -1/3$. Numerical solutions using the DD2 equation of state show that anisotropy in spin correlations has negligible impact on the mass–radius relation for smooth weak-polarization profiles.
\n\n
对于有效自旋-自旋耦合为正值的情况（由二次挠率参数决定），挠率修正导致中子星结构更加致密，最大质量降低（相较于广义相对论情形），且结合能减小。在Einstein–Cartan极限下（仅保留特定挠率耦合），未极化的自旋流体贡献的状态方程参数为 $w_{\\mathrm{spin}} = -1/3$。使用DD2物态方程的数值解表明，对于平滑的弱极化轮廓，自旋关联的各向异性对质量-半径关系的影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that Poincaré gauge gravity with quadratic torsion invariants introduces nontrivial modifications to neutron star structure through effective spin-squared stresses, with outcomes sensitive to the sign and magnitude of torsion couplings. Unlike Einstein–Cartan theory, the flexibility in torsion parameters allows for tunable spin interactions, offering richer phenomenology. However, for physically motivated weak and smooth polarization, anisotropic spin effects are subdominant, and the dominant impact arises from the isotropic spin-pressure correction, which stiffens or softens the effective equation of state depending on the coupling branch. This provides observational signatures potentially testable with future multimessenger data.
\n\n
本研究表明，包含二次挠率不变量的Poincaré规范引力通过有效的自旋平方应力对中子星结构引入非平凡修正，其结果对挠率耦合的符号和大小敏感。与Einstein–Cartan理论不同，挠率参数的灵活性允许可调的自旋相互作用，从而展现出更丰富的现象学。然而，对于物理上合理的弱且平滑的极化情形，各向异性自旋效应是次主导的，主要影响来自各向同性的自旋压强修正，该修正根据耦合分支的不同会使有效物态方程变硬或变软。这为未来多信使观测提供了潜在可检验的信号。

Abstract: We study static neutron stars in an algebraic sector of Poincaré gauge gravity with parity-even and parity-odd quadratic torsion invariants. Since torsion is non-propagating, the contorsion equation is algebraic and can be solved in terms of the spin current. For a Weyssenhoff fluid satisfying the Frenkel condition, the metric field equations reduce to ordinary Riemannian Einstein equations sourced by an effective fluid containing spin-squared corrections. We derive the effective energy density, radial pressure, and tangential pressure, allowing both isotropic and anisotropic spin correlations. In contrast with Einstein--Cartan theory, the coefficient of the effective spin-spin interaction is not fixed, but depends on the dimensionless quadratic-torsion couplings. In the Einstein--Cartan limit, using the metric definition of the stress-energy tensor, the unpolarized spin contribution gives $w_{\mathrm{spin}}=-1/3$. We then derive the corresponding modified Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equations and solve them numerically using the DD2 equation of state. For the positive effective spin-spin coupling branch considered here, the torsion correction makes the stellar configurations more compact, lowers the maximum mass, and reduces the binding energy relative to the general-relativistic sequence. For the smooth weak-polarization profiles considered, spin-correlation anisotropy has only a negligible effect on the mass--radius relation.

</details>

### [92] [CMBolic: Symbolic emulators for the Cosmic Microwave Background. I. Lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07745)
*David M. J. Vokrouhlicky,Constantinos Skordis,Deaglan J. Bartlett,Harry Desmond,Pedro G. Ferreira*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: CMBolic introduces the first symbolic emulator for the CMB lensing potential power spectrum $C_\ell^{φφ}$ in an extended $Λ$CDM model with massive neutrinos and CPL dark energy, achieving sub-percent accuracy (0.27–0.32%) and reducing parameter inference runtime from weeks to minutes while matching Boltzmann code CLASS results.
\n\n
CMBolic 提出了首个用于扩展 $Λ$CDM 模型（包含大质量中微子和采用 Chevallier-Polarski-Linder (CPL) 参数化的动力学暗能量）中 CMB 引力透镜势功率谱 $C_\ell^{φφ}$ 的符号化模拟器，在次百分比精度（0.27–0.32%）下运行，将参数推断时间从数周缩短至数分钟，并与 Boltzmann 代码 CLASS 结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing CMB emulators based on neural networks, while fast, lack interpretability and analytic dependence on cosmological parameters; CMBolic addresses this by providing fully analytic, symbolic emulators that are both accurate and easy to integrate into likelihood analyses, especially for high-precision CMB Stage 4 experiments where emulation error must be negligible compared to instrumental noise.
\n\n
现有的基于神经网络的 CMB 模拟器虽然快速，但缺乏可解释性且无法显式表达为宇宙学参数的解析函数；CMBolic 通过提供完全解析、符号化的模拟器来解决这一问题，这些模拟器不仅精确，而且易于集成到似然分析中，尤其适用于 CMB Stage 4 实验——其模拟误差必须远小于仪器噪声。

Method: The authors construct a symbolic regression-based emulator trained on high-fidelity $C_\ell^{φφ}$ spectra computed with the Boltzmann code CLASS across the extended $Λ$CDM parameter space (including $\\sum m_\\nu$ and CPL parameters $w_0$, $w_a$). The resulting emulator is an explicit analytic function of cosmological parameters and multipole $\ell$, validated over $2 \leq \ell \leq 5500$. They perform Bayesian parameter inference using ACT DR6 and Planck lensing-only likelihoods, comparing posteriors from CMBolic against those from direct CLASS evaluations.
\n\n
作者基于符号回归构建了一个模拟器，该模拟器在扩展的 $Λ$CDM 参数空间（包括 $\\sum m_\\nu$ 和 CPL 参数 $w_0$、$w_a$）中使用 Boltzmann 代码 CLASS 计算的高保真度 $C_\ell^{φφ}$ 谱进行训练。所得模拟器是宇宙学参数和多极矩 $\ell$ 的显式解析函数，并在 $2 \leq \ell \leq 5500$ 范围内进行了验证。他们使用 ACT DR6 和 Planck 的纯透镜似然进行贝叶斯参数推断，并将 CMBolic 得到的后验分布与直接使用 CLASS 计算的结果进行比较。

Result: CMBolic achieves mean absolute fractional errors of 0.27% in standard $Λ$CDM and 0.32% in the full extended model (massive neutrinos + CPL dark energy), well below CMB Stage 4 noise forecasts. Parameter posteriors from CMBolic match those from CLASS to high precision, while reducing computational cost from ~2 weeks to <3 minutes for lensing-only inference.
\n\n
CMBolic 在标准 $Λ$CDM 模型中达到 0.27% 的平均绝对相对误差，在完整扩展模型（大质量中微子 + CPL 暗能量）中为 0.32%，远低于 CMB Stage 4 的噪声预测。CMBolic 得到的参数后验分布与 CLASS 高度一致，同时将纯透镜推断的计算成本从约两周降至不到 3 分钟。

Conclusion: CMBolic demonstrates that symbolic, analytic emulators can achieve state-of-the-art accuracy for CMB observables while offering significant computational speedups and seamless integration into cosmological inference pipelines, paving the way for interpretable, differentiable, and efficient modeling of future CMB data.
\n\n
CMBolic 表明，符号化、解析型模拟器能够在 CMB 观测量上达到前沿精度，同时提供显著的计算加速并无缝集成到宇宙学推断流程中，为未来 CMB 数据的可解释、可微分且高效建模铺平了道路。

Abstract: We present the first installment of CMBolic: a suite of symbolic cosmic microwave background (CMB) emulators. In this instance, we emulate the CMB lensing potential power spectrum $C_\ell^{φφ}$ for the widely used extended $Λ$CDM model which simultaneously includes massive neutrinos and evolving dark energy modelled using the Chevallier-Polarski-Linder (CPL) parameterization. We achieve comparable precision to existing neural network emulators, with the added benefit of simpler handling as our emulators are analytic functions of the model parameters and multipole $\ell$. On independent validation spectra evaluated in the range $2\leq \ell \leq 5500$, CMBolic achieves mean absolute fractional errors of $0.27\%$ in the $Λ$CDM subspace and $0.32\%$ across the full extended parameter space. This emulation error is well below even the most optimistic noise forecasts from CMB Stage 4 experiments. We apply CMBolic to cosmological parameter estimation with Bayesian inference using the lensing-only likelihoods from ACT DR6 and Planck. We show excellent agreement between the posteriors obtained by CMBolic and the Boltzmann code CLASS. This demonstrates the practical use of CMBolic on cosmological parameter estimation, reducing the runtime from 2 weeks to under 3 minutes.

</details>


### [93] [Measurements of the Angular Homogeneity Scale from DESI DR1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07854)
*Mariana Lopes-Dias,Carlos A. P. Bengaly,Xiaoyun Shao,Rodrigo Gonçalves,Paula S. Ferreira,Gabriela Coutinho de Carvalho,Jailson Alcaniz*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper tests the Cosmological Principle by measuring the angular homogeneity scale $θ_H$ in DESI DR1 Luminous Red Galaxy (LRG) data across redshifts $0.4 < z < 1.1$, finding consistency with ΛCDM predictions and previous SDSS-IV eBOSS DR16 results, thereby supporting large-scale statistical isotropy and homogeneity.
\n\n
本文通过在红移范围 $0.4 < z < 1.1$ 内利用 DESI DR1 的 Luminous Red Galaxy (LRG) 数据测量角均匀性尺度 $θ_H$，检验 Cosmological Principle，结果与 ΛCDM 模型预测及先前的 SDSS-IV eBOSS DR16 测量一致，从而支持宇宙在大尺度上的统计各向同性和均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Cosmological Principle—asserting statistical homogeneity and isotropy on large scales—is a foundational assumption of the standard $Λ$CDM cosmological model, underpinning the use of the FLRW metric. However, this principle must be empirically validated. The motivation is to test this assumption directly using modern, high-precision galaxy survey data without strong dependence on an assumed cosmological model.
\n\n
Cosmological Principle——即宇宙在大尺度上具有统计均匀性和各向同性——是标准 $Λ$CDM 宇宙学模型的基础假设，也是采用 FLRW 度规的前提。然而，这一原理必须通过观测加以验证。本文旨在利用现代高精度星系巡天数据，在尽量减少对预设宇宙学模型依赖的前提下，直接检验该假设。

Method: The authors analyze two-dimensional angular distributions of Luminous Red Galaxies (LRGs) from DESI DR1 within narrow redshift slices over $0.4 < z < 1.1$, separately for the North and South Galactic Caps. They measure the angular homogeneity scale $θ_H$, defined as the angle above which the galaxy distribution becomes statistically homogeneous. The analysis avoids full 3D modeling to minimize reliance on cosmological assumptions (e.g., distance-redshift relations). Results are compared with ΛCDM-based mock simulations and prior measurements from SDSS-IV eBOSS DR16.
\n\n
作者分析了 DESI DR1 中 Luminous Red Galaxies (LRGs) 在红移区间 $0.4 < z < 1.1$ 内多个窄红移切片中的二维角分布，并分别处理北天和南天银冠区域。他们测量角均匀性尺度 $θ_H$（定义为超过该角度后星系分布呈现统计均匀性的尺度），避免使用完整的三维建模以最小化对宇宙学假设（如距离-红移关系）的依赖。结果与基于 ΛCDM 的模拟以及 SDSS-IV eBOSS DR16 的先前测量进行对比。

Result: The angular homogeneity scale $θ_H$ is robustly detected in all redshift bins and both sky regions. The measured values agree with ΛCDM mock simulations and are consistent between the North and South Galactic Caps of DESI DR1. Furthermore, they align well with earlier results from SDSS-IV eBOSS DR16, reinforcing the reliability of the findings across surveys and methodologies.
\n\n
在所有红移区间和两个天区中均稳健地探测到角均匀性尺度 $θ_H$。测量值与 ΛCDM 模拟一致，且 DESI DR1 的北天与南天银冠区域结果相互吻合。此外，结果与 SDSS-IV eBOSS DR16 的早期测量高度一致，表明不同巡天和方法下结论的可靠性。

Conclusion: The detection of a consistent angular homogeneity scale across redshifts, sky regions, and independent surveys provides strong empirical support for the Cosmological Principle. This validates a key pillar of the $Λ$CDM model and reinforces the use of the FLRW metric for interpreting cosmological observations, especially in anticipation of Stage-IV redshift surveys.
\n\n
在不同红移、天区及独立巡天中一致探测到角均匀性尺度，为 Cosmological Principle 提供了强有力的观测支持。这验证了 $Λ$CDM 模型的关键支柱，并强化了在解释宇宙学观测（尤其是面向 Stage-IV 红移巡天）时使用 FLRW 度规的合理性。

Abstract: The study of the large-scale distribution of galaxies provides essential information for testing the standard cosmological model, namely the $Λ$CDM paradigm. This scenario is based upon two foundations: General Relativity as the theory of gravity, and the Cosmological Principle, which states that the Universe is statistically homogeneous and isotropic on large scales -- so that we can measure distances and ages in the Universe assuming the FLRW metric.
  In this work, we perform a test of the Cosmological Principle by probing the angular homogeneity scale, $θ_H$, using the state-of-the-art observational data of Luminous Red Galaxies (LRGs) from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument Data Release 1 (DESI DR1). Our analysis is performed exclusively in two dimensions, across narrow redshift ranges inside a larger redshift sample of $0.4 < z < 1.1$, in two different surveyed regions of the sky (North and South Galactic Caps), as we want to minimize a priori dependences on an underlying cosmological model.
  We obtain that such a scale is indeed identified in all redshift ranges, and that they are consistent with mock simulations assuming the $Λ$CDM model. Moreover, our results are in great agreement with previous measurements using Sloan Digital Sky Survey IV extended Baryon Oscillation Spectroscopic Survey Data Release 16 (SDSS-IV eBOSS DR16), as well as between the north and south galactic caps of the DESI DR1 survey. These findings help underpinning statistical isotropy and homogeneity of the Universe as a physically valid hypothesis in light of upcoming stage-IV redshift surveys, hence are consistent with one of the fundamental pillars of the standard cosmological model.

</details>


### [94] [Faraday Complexity and Depolarisation in a High-Rotation-Measure Radio Galaxy from the Spectra and Polarisation In Cutouts of Extragalactic Sources (SPICE-RACS) DR2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07919)
*Debajyoti Mondal,Abhik Ghosh*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper presents a detailed broadband spectropolarimetric analysis of the extragalactic radio source RACS_0900-28_7036 using ASKAP SPICE-RACS DR2 data, revealing a complex Faraday structure best modeled by one Burn slab and two external Faraday dispersion components. The study demonstrates ASKAP's capability to resolve turbulent magnetized media along the line of sight.
\n\n
本文利用ASKAP SPICE-RACS DR2数据对河外射电源RACS_0900-28_7036进行了详细的宽带谱偏振分析，揭示其复杂的法拉第结构，最佳模型包含一个Burn slab和两个外部法拉第弥散（EFD）成分。该研究展示了ASKAP解析视线方向上湍动磁化介质的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The source was selected due to its unusually high rotation measure (RM = $345.7 \pm 0.2~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$), significant excess over the local foreground ($Δ{\rm RM} \approx 171~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$), and strong indication of Faraday complexity ($σ_{\rm add}/δσ_{\rm add} \approx 8.6$), making it an ideal candidate to probe magnetized turbulent environments and test broadband polarimetric decomposition techniques.
\n\n
该源因其异常高的旋转测度（RM = $345.7 \pm 0.2~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$）、显著超出本地前景的超额值（$Δ{\rm RM} \approx 171~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$）以及强烈的法拉第复杂性迹象（$σ_{\rm add}/δσ_{\rm add} \approx 8.6$）而被选中，是探测磁化湍流环境和测试宽带偏振分解技术的理想候选体。

Method: The authors employed broadband spectropolarimetry from 803–1083 MHz across 36 spectral channels using ASKAP SPICE-RACS DR2 data. They performed one-dimensional $q$–$u$ plane fitting combined with Bayesian model selection to compare different Faraday emission models (e.g., Burn slabs, external Faraday dispersion). This allowed them to statistically identify the optimal multi-component model describing the observed polarization spectrum.
\n\n
作者利用ASKAP SPICE-RACS DR2数据在803–1083 MHz范围内、共36个频谱通道进行宽带谱偏振观测。他们结合一维$q$–$u$平面拟合与贝叶斯模型选择方法，比较不同法拉第辐射模型（如Burn slab、外部法拉第弥散EFD），从而统计识别出最优的多成分模型以描述观测到的偏振谱。

Result: The preferred model consists of one Burn-slab component and two external Faraday dispersion (EFD) components (1 Slab + 2 EFD). The dominant component has ${\rm RM} \approx 345.5~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$ with modest dispersion ($σ_{\rm RM} \approx 3~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$); a secondary broader component at ${\rm RM} \approx 131.5~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$ shows strong depolarization ($σ_{\rm RM} \approx 19.5~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$). The fractional polarization spectrum and $q$–$u$ trajectory confirm multiple Faraday-active regions along the line of sight.
\n\n
最优模型由一个Burn-slab成分和两个外部法拉第弥散（EFD）成分组成（1 Slab + 2 EFD）。主导成分具有${\rm RM} \approx 345.5~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$和较小的弥散（$σ_{\rm RM} \approx 3~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$）；次要的宽成分位于${\rm RM} \approx 131.5~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$，表现出强退偏振（$σ_{\rm RM} \approx 19.5~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$）。偏振分数谱和$q$–$u$轨迹进一步证实视线方向上存在多个法拉第活跃区域。

Conclusion: ASKAP’s broadband spectropolarimetric capabilities enable robust decomposition of complex Faraday structures, offering a powerful framework for systematic studies of depolarization and Faraday complexity across large extragalactic source samples like the full SPICE-RACS catalog.
\n\n
ASKAP的宽带谱偏振能力能够稳健地分解复杂的法拉第结构，为系统性研究退偏振现象及法拉第复杂性提供了有力框架，适用于SPICE-RACS等大型河外射电源样本的统计分析。

Abstract: We present a broadband spectro-polarimetric analysis of the extragalactic radio source \texttt{RACS\_0900-28\_7036} using SPICE-RACS DR2 observations with the Australian Square Kilometre Array Pathfinder (ASKAP). The source was selected for its large rotation measure (${\rm RM}=345.7\pm0.2~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$), substantial excess relative to the local foreground ($Δ{\rm RM}\approx171~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$), and strong evidence of Faraday complexity ($σ_{\rm add}/δσ_{\rm add}\approx8.6$). Observations span 803--1083~MHz in 36 spectral channels, enabling detailed characterization of Faraday rotation and wavelength-dependent depolarization. One-dimensional $q$-$u$ fitting and Bayesian model selection identify a multi-component model comprising one Burn-slab component and two external Faraday dispersion components (1 Slab + 2 EFD) as the preferred description. The dominant astrophysical component exhibits ${\rm RM}\approx345.5~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$ with modest Faraday dispersion ($σ_{\rm RM}\approx3~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$), while a secondary broader component at ${\rm RM}\approx131.5~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$ shows strong depolarization ($σ_{\rm RM}\approx19.5~{\rm rad~m^{-2}}$). The fractional polarization spectrum and $q$--$u$ plane evolution further confirm multiple Faraday-active regions along the line of sight. These results demonstrate that ASKAP broadband spectropolarimetry can resolve complex Faraday structures and probe turbulent magnetized environments, providing a framework for systematic depolarization studies across the full SPICE-RACS catalog and enabling statistical investigations of Faraday complexity in diverse extragalactic radio sources.

</details>


### [95] [Effective Bayesian ranking of low order monomial potentials in low temperature warm inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07958)
*Xin Peng,Xiang Cheng,Wei Cheng,Yi-Rong Ma,Pan Yu,Jun Zeng*

Main category: astro-ph.CO
