TL;DR: The paper performs an effective Bayesian evidence comparison among monomial inflationary potentials $V_p(\\phi) = \\lambda_p \\phi^p / p$ with $p = 2, 3, 4$ in the context of low-temperature warm inflation. Using a compressed likelihood for CMB observables $(A_s, n_s, r_{0.05})$ and integrating over viable parameter volumes, it finds strong statistical preference for the quartic potential ($p=4$), with $\\Delta \\ln Z_{\\rm eff}(p=2) = -32.18$ and $\\Delta \\ln Z_{\\rm eff}(p=3) = -6.99$, indicating $p=4 > p=3 \\gg p=2$. This preference arises primarily from Bose-Einstein enhancement of scalar perturbations due to thermal occupation ($T_*/H_* > 1$), not strong dissipation.
\n\n
本文在低温warm inflation背景下，对单项式暴胀势 $V_p(\\phi) = \\lambda_p \\phi^p / p$（$p = 2, 3, 4$）进行了有效的贝叶斯证据比较。通过使用CMB观测量 $(A_s, n_s, r_{0.05})$ 的压缩似然函数并对可行参数空间积分，发现四次势（$p=4$）具有显著的统计优势：$\\Delta \\ln Z_{\\rm eff}(p=2) = -32.18$，$\\Delta \\ln Z_{\\rm eff}(p=3) = -6.99$，表明证据层级为 $p=4 > p=3 \\gg p=2$。这一偏好主要源于热占据（$T_*/H_* > 1$）导致的Bose-Einstein增强效应，而非强耗散摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In cold single-field slow-roll inflation, monomial potentials with $p \\geq 2$ are tightly constrained by the observational upper limit on the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$, which disfavors higher $p$. Warm inflation offers a mechanism to alleviate this tension by enhancing the scalar power spectrum $\\mathcal{P}_{\\mathcal{R}}$ through thermal effects, thereby lowering the predicted $r$ for a given $n_s$. The key question is: when jointly considering the observed amplitude $A_s$, spectral index $n_s$, tensor-to-scalar ratio $r_{0.05}$, and the volume of viable model parameters, which monomial power $p$ is statistically preferred? This motivates a Bayesian model comparison within the low-temperature warm inflation framework with dissipative coefficient $\\Upsilon = C_\\phi T^3 / \\phi^2$.
\n\n
在冷单场慢滚暴胀中，单项式势（$p \\geq 2$）受到张量标量比 $r$ 观测上限的严格限制，高次势被强烈排除。Warm inflation通过热效应增强标量功率谱 $\\mathcal{P}_{\\mathcal{R}}$，从而在给定 $n_s$ 下降低预测的 $r$，缓解这一张力。核心问题是：当同时考虑观测到的振幅 $A_s$、谱指数 $n_s$、张量标量比 $r_{0.05}$ 以及可行模型参数体积时，哪个单项式幂次 $p$ 在统计上更受青睐？这促使作者在耗散系数为 $\\Upsilon = C_\\phi T^3 / \\phi^2$ 的低温warm inflation框架内进行贝叶斯模型比较。

Method: The authors solve the full warm inflation background equations including radiation backreaction for each monomial potential $V_p(\\phi)$. They adopt structure-conditioned priors that cover the viable parameter space for low-temperature warm inflation (where $T < m_\\chi$, ensuring validity of the $\\Upsilon \\propto T^3/\\phi^2$ form). A broadened compressed likelihood based on Planck/BICEP constraints for $(A_s, n_s, r_{0.05})$ is used. The effective Bayesian evidence $Z_{\\rm eff}^{(A_s,n_s,r)}$ is computed by integrating this likelihood over the prior volume for each $p$. Robustness is tested against variations in $N_*$ (e-folds), prior ranges, random seeds, and treatment of the $r$ upper bound. The primordial power spectrum is decomposed to isolate contributions from thermal occupation (Bose-Einstein enhancement) versus dissipative friction.
\n\n
作者对每个单项式势 $V_p(\\phi)$ 求解包含辐射反作用的完整warm inflation背景方程。采用结构条件先验（structure-conditioned priors）覆盖低温warm inflation（$T < m_\\chi$，确保 $\\Upsilon \\propto T^3/\\phi^2$ 形式的有效性）的可行参数空间。使用基于Planck/BICEP对 $(A_s, n_s, r_{0.05})$ 约束的展宽压缩似然函数。通过对每个 $p$ 的先验体积积分该似然，计算有效贝叶斯证据 $Z_{\\rm eff}^{(A_s,n_s,r)}$。通过改变 $N_*$（e-fold数）、先验范围、随机种子及 $r$ 上限处理方式检验结果稳健性。此外，分解原初功率谱以分离热占据（Bose-Einstein增强）与耗散摩擦的贡献。

Result: For $N_* = 55$ and $\\sigma_r = 0.005$, the Bayesian evidence strongly favors the quartic potential: $\\Delta \\ln Z_{\\rm eff}(p=2) = -32.18$ and $\\Delta \\ln Z_{\\rm eff}(p=3) = -6.99$, establishing the hierarchy $p=4 > p=3 \\gg p=2$. This result is stable under various robustness checks. A representative best-fit trajectory for $p=4$ yields $n_s = 0.96420$, $r_{0.05} = 0.02663$, dissipation ratio $Q_* = 4.68 \\times 10^{-3}$, and thermal ratio $T_*/H_* = 10.67$, placing it in the weakly dissipative but thermally occupied regime ($T_*/H_* > 1$). Spectral decomposition shows the preference for $p=4$ is driven by Bose-Einstein enhancement of scalar fluctuations due to thermal occupation, not by strong dissipation ($Q \\ll 1$).
\n\n
当 $N_* = 55$ 且 $\\sigma_r = 0.005$ 时，贝叶斯证据强烈支持四次势：$\\Delta \\ln Z_{\\rm eff}(p=2) = -32.18$，$\\Delta \\ln Z_{\\rm eff}(p=3) = -6.99$，确立了 $p=4 > p=3 \\gg p=2$ 的层级。该结果在多种稳健性检验下保持稳定。一个代表性的 $p=4$ 最佳拟合轨迹给出 $n_s = 0.96420$，$r_{0.05} = 0.02663$，耗散比 $Q_* = 4.68 \\times 10^{-3}$，热比 $T_*/H_* = 10.67$，处于弱耗散但热占据状态（$T_*/H_* > 1$）。功率谱分解表明，对 $p=4$ 的偏好主要源于热占据导致的标量扰动Bose-Einstein增强，而非强耗散（$Q \\ll 1$）。

Conclusion: Within the specific class of low-temperature warm inflation models with dissipative coefficient $\\Upsilon = C_\\phi T^3 / \\phi^2$, and using a compressed CMB likelihood incorporating $A_s$, $n_s$, and $r_{0.05}$, the quartic monomial potential ($p=4$) is decisively favored over quadratic and cubic potentials by Bayesian evidence. This preference is robust and physically attributed to thermal enhancement of scalar perturbations via Bose-Einstein statistics when $T_*/H_* > 1$, even in the weak dissipation regime. The work demonstrates that warm inflation can reverse the cold inflation hierarchy of monomial potentials, highlighting the critical role of thermal effects in early universe model selection.
\n\n
在耗散系数为 $\\Upsilon = C_\\phi T^3 / \\phi^2$ 的低温warm inflation模型类中，结合 $A_s$、$n_s$ 和 $r_{0.05}$ 的压缩CMB似然，贝叶斯证据明确支持四次单项式势（$p=4$），优于二次和三次势。这一偏好具有稳健性，其物理根源在于当 $T_*/H_* > 1$ 时，Bose-Einstein统计导致的标量扰动热增强效应，即使在弱耗散区域亦成立。该工作表明，warm inflation可逆转冷暴胀中单项式势的优劣层级，凸显了热效应对早期宇宙模型选择的关键作用。

Abstract: An effective Bayesian evidence ranking is performed for the monomial potentials \(V_p(φ)=λ_pφ^p/p\), with \(p=2,3,4\), in low temperature warm inflation with the dissipative coefficient fixed as \(Υ=C_φT^3/φ^2\). In cold single field slow roll inflation, these branches are strongly constrained by the observational upper bound on the tensor to scalar ratio \(r=\mathcal P_T/\mathcal P_{\mathcal R}\), whereas warm inflation can reduce this tension by enhancing the scalar spectrum. The relevant question is therefore which monomial power is favored once \(A_s\), \(n_s\), \(r_{0.05}\), and the viable parameter volume are considered simultaneously. For each branch, the warm background equations including radiation backreaction are solved, and a broadened compressed likelihood for \((A_s,n_s,r_{0.05})\) is integrated over the prior volume to obtain \(Z_{\rm eff}^{(A_s,n_s,r)}\). For \(N_*=55\), \(σ_r=0.005\), and structure conditioned priors covering viable warm branches, the quadratic and cubic potentials are disfavored relative to the quartic branch: $Δ\ln Z_{\rm eff}(p=2)=-32.18,~ Δ\ln Z_{\rm eff}(p=3)=-6.99.$ This hierarchy is stable under changes in \(N_*\), prior ranges, random seeds, and the $r$ bound treatment. A representative quartic trajectory gives \(n_s=0.96420\), \(r_{0.05}=0.02663\), \(Q_*=4.68\times10^{-3}\), and \(T_*/H_*=10.67\), corresponding to a weakly dissipative but thermally occupied CMB window. Decomposing the primordial spectrum shows that the quartic preference is driven mainly by Bose Einstein occupation enhancement for \(T_*/H_*>1\), not by strong dissipative friction. Within the low temperature dissipative effective class and compressed likelihood adopted here, the evidence hierarchy is \(p=4>p=3\gg p=2.\)

</details>


### [96] [XRISM Observations of Abell 1795: Evidence for Low Turbulence and Resonant Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08097)
*Arnab Sarkar,Eric D. Miller,Brian McNamara,Helen Russell,Kotaro Fukushima,Mark Bautz,Yutaka Fujita,Catherine E. Grant,François Mernier,Michael A. McDonald,Naomi Ota,Ayşegül Tümer,Daniel Wik*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper reports high-resolution X-ray spectroscopy of the galaxy cluster Abell 1795 using XRISM/Resolve, revealing a radial decline in velocity dispersion, low bulk motions between the BCG and ICM, decreasing nonthermal pressure fraction with radius, evidence for multiphase gas in the core, and anomalies in Fe XXV line fluxes possibly linked to atomic data uncertainties.
\n\n
本文利用XRISM/Resolve对星系团Abell 1795进行了高分辨率X射线光谱观测，揭示了视线方向速度弥散随半径递减、最亮团星系（BCG）与星系团内介质（ICM）之间整体运动微弱、非热压力占比随半径下降、核心区域存在多相气体的证据，以及Fe XXV谱线流量异常，后者可能与原子数据不确定性有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The study aims to resolve long-standing questions about the dynamical state and thermodynamic structure of cool-core galaxy clusters—particularly whether observed cool gas features arise from cooling-wake scenarios or AGN feedback—and to characterize turbulence, bulk motions, and nonthermal pressure support using unprecedented spectral resolution from XRISM/Resolve.
\n\n
本研究旨在解决关于冷核星系团动力学状态和热力学结构的长期问题——特别是观测到的冷气体结构是源于“冷却尾迹”（cooling-wake）情景还是AGN反馈机制——并利用XRISM/Resolve前所未有的光谱分辨率来刻画湍流、整体运动及非热压力支撑。

Method: The authors analyze deep XRISM/Resolve observations (225 ks central + 113 ks northern pointings) of Abell 1795 out to 320 kpc. They perform single-temperature, two-temperature, and split energy-band (2–4 keV and 6–7 keV) spectral fits to infer velocity dispersion, bulk velocities, nonthermal pressure fraction $P_{\\rm NT}/P_{\\rm T}$, and gas phase structure. Line ratios of Fe XXV ($w$, $y$) are compared with theoretical models, accounting for optical depth and atomic data uncertainties.
\n\n
作者分析了Abell 1795的深度XRISM/Resolve观测数据（中心225 ks + 北部113 ks指向），覆盖至320 kpc投影半径。他们采用单温度、双温度及分能段（2–4 keV 和 6–7 keV）谱拟合方法，推断速度弥散、整体速度、非热压力占比 $P_{\\rm NT}/P_{\\rm T}$ 及气体相结构。同时将Fe XXV谱线（$w$、$y$）的流量比与理论模型对比，并考虑光学深度和原子数据不确定性。

Result: Key results include: (1) a radial decrease in line-of-sight velocity dispersion from $114 \\pm 11$ km/s to $68 \\pm 39$ km/s; (2) very low bulk velocities ($\\sim$7–22 km/s), ruling out significant BCG–ICM motion; (3) nonthermal pressure fraction declining from $\\sim2\\%$ to $\\sim0.6\\%$ with radius; (4) clear detection of multiphase gas within the central $<1.5'$; (5) $\\sim14\\%$ resonant suppression of the optically thick Fe XXV $w$ line; and (6) excess Fe XXV $y$ line flux, partially reconciled by atomic data uncertainties.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 视线方向速度弥散从 $114 \\pm 11$ km/s 径向下降至 $68 \\pm 39$ km/s；(2) 整体速度极低（约7–22 km/s），排除了BCG与ICM之间显著相对运动；(3) 非热压力占比从约 $2\\%$ 下降至 $0.6\\%$；(4) 在中心 $<1.5'$ 区域明确探测到多相气体；(5) 光学厚Fe XXV $w$ 线出现约 $14\\%$ 的共振抑制；(6) Fe XXV $y$ 线流量超出模型预测，部分可通过原子数据不确定性解释。

Conclusion: The findings disfavor the cooling-wake model for the cool gas tail in Abell 1795 and instead support an AGN-uplift origin. The low turbulence and bulk motions, combined with a radially declining nonthermal pressure fraction, indicate a quiescent ICM, especially in the north. Observed Fe XXV line anomalies highlight the need for improved atomic databases for high-resolution X-ray spectroscopy.
\n\n
研究结果不支持Abell 1795中冷气体尾迹的“冷却尾迹”模型，而更倾向于AGN抬升（AGN-uplift）起源。低湍流与整体运动，加上随半径下降的非热压力占比，表明ICM（尤其是北部）处于宁静状态。观测到的Fe XXV谱线异常凸显了高分辨率X射线光谱学对改进原子数据库的需求。

Abstract: We present high-resolution X-ray spectroscopic observations of the cool-core galaxy cluster Abell~1795 obtained with XRISM/Resolve. The cluster was observed with two deep pointings: a 225 ks central exposure and a 113 ks northern exposure, extending to a projected radius of 320 kpc from the cluster center. Single-temperature fits reveal a clear radial gradient in the line-of-sight velocity dispersion, decreasing from 114 $\pm$ 11 km/s in the core to 68 $\pm$ 39 km/s at 320 kpc. The bulk velocities in the central regions are very low (22 $\pm$ 12 and 7 $\pm$ 21 km/s), indicating no significant relative motion between the brightest cluster galaxy (BCG) and the intracluster medium (ICM). Given that the central region includes the southward extending cool gas tail, this result disfavors the ``cooling-wake'' scenario and instead supports an AGN-uplift origin. We find that the nonthermal pressure fraction decreases with radius, from $P_{\rm NT}/P_{\rm T}\approx2\%$ in the core to $\sim0.6\%$ at 330 kpc, suggesting that the northern ICM of A1795 is largely quiescent. Two-temperature and split energy-band (2--4 keV and 6--7 keV) fits identify two gas phases within the central $<1.5'$ region, providing strong evidence for multiphase gas in the cluster core. We detect a $\sim14\%$ resonant suppression of the optically thick Fe XXV $w$ line in the center. Additionally, we observe a significant excess in the Fe XXV $y$ line-flux relative to models. Accounting for uncertainties in the atomic data reduces this discrepancy, suggesting that atomic data uncertainties may contribute to the observed residual flux.

</details>


### [97] [Reconciling large-scale Lyman-$α$ correlations with the SCRIPT Semi-numerical Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08368)
*Saptarshi Sarkar,Tirthankar Roy Choudhury*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: Recent observations of Lyman-$\\alpha$ forest at $z=6$ show unexpectedly large correlations beyond 200 cMpc. While standard reionization simulations underpredict this signal, the authors show that rare, highly transmissive sightlines—particularly a single influential pair—can account for the discrepancy. Including such outliers in mock spectra from the SCRIPT model boosts agreement with data from 17.5% to 74.1%. Cosmic variance and fluctuations in the ionizing mean free path are crucial for reproducing these correlations.
\n\n
近期在红移 $z=6$ 处的 Lyman-$\\alpha$ 森林观测显示出超过 200 cMpc 的异常大尺度关联。尽管标准再电离模拟低估了该信号，作者指出稀有的高透射视线（尤其是一对具有显著影响的视线）可解释这一差异。在 SCRIPT 模型生成的模拟光谱中引入此类异常值后，与观测数据的一致性从 17.5% 显著提升至 74.1%。宇宙学方差（cosmic variance）和电离平均自由程（ionizing mean free path）的空间涨落对重现这些关联结构至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the tension between recent high-redshift Lyman-$\\alpha$ forest data (e.g., XQR-30 sample) showing strong large-scale ($>200$ cMpc) correlations at $z=6$, and the inability of current large-volume reionization simulations to reproduce such signals. This discrepancy challenges our understanding of the Epoch of Reionization and demands a careful assessment of statistical outliers, cosmic variance, and physical modeling assumptions.
\n\n
动机源于近期高红移 Lyman-$\\alpha$ 森林数据（如 XQR-30 样本）在 $z=6$ 处显示出强的大尺度（$>200$ cMpc）关联，而当前大体积再电离模拟无法重现此类信号。这一矛盾挑战了我们对再电离时期（Epoch of Reionization）的理解，亟需仔细评估统计异常值、宇宙学方差以及物理建模假设。

Method: The authors employ mock Lyman-$\\alpha$ forest spectra generated from the extended SCRIPT semi-numerical reionization model. They compare ensemble predictions against the observed correlation length from 67 sightlines in the XQR-30 sample. A delete-2 jackknife analysis identifies the influence of specific sightline pairs. They then artificially insert two highly transmissive sightlines into mock realizations to test their impact. The role of spatial fluctuations in the ionizing mean free path is also examined as a key physical ingredient.
\n\n
作者使用扩展的 SCRIPT 半数值再电离模型生成模拟 Lyman-$\\alpha$ 森林光谱，并将其集合预测与 XQR-30 样本中 67 条视线观测到的关联长度进行比较。通过 delete-2 jackknife 分析识别特定视线对的影响。随后，他们在模拟实现中人为插入两条高透射视线以检验其影响。同时，考察了电离平均自由程的空间涨落作为关键物理成分的作用。

Result: The fiducial SCRIPT model ensemble underpredicts the observed correlation length. However, a small subset of individual realizations naturally matches the data. The delete-2 jackknife reveals that a single rare pair of highly transmissive sightlines disproportionately drives the observed large-scale correlation. Inserting such sightlines into mocks increases the fraction of models consistent with observations from 17.5% to 74.1%. Spatial fluctuations in the ionizing mean free path are shown to be essential for matching the correlation structure.
\n\n
基准 SCRIPT 模型集合低估了观测到的关联长度，但少数个别实现能自然匹配数据。delete-2 jackknife 分析表明，一对稀有的高透射视线对观测到的大尺度关联起主导作用。在模拟中引入此类视线后，与观测一致的模型比例从 17.5% 提升至 74.1%。电离平均自由程的空间涨落被证明对匹配关联结构至关重要。

Conclusion: The unexpectedly large Lyman-$\\alpha$ correlations at $z=6$ do not necessarily require new physics beyond standard reionization scenarios. Instead, they can be explained by cosmic variance and the outsized contribution of rare, highly transmissive sightlines. Accurate modeling must include realistic fluctuations in the ionizing mean free path and account for the non-Gaussian statistics inherent in small observational samples.
\n\n
$z=6$ 处异常大的 Lyman-$\\alpha$ 关联不一定需要超出标准再电离场景的新物理机制。它们可通过宇宙学方差和稀有高透射视线的不成比例贡献来解释。精确建模必须包含电离平均自由程的真实涨落，并考虑小样本观测中固有的非高斯统计特性。

Abstract: Recent analyses of high-redshift Lyman-$α$ forest observations have revealed strong correlations on scales exceeding 200 cMpc at redshift z = 6. Reproducing these large-scale correlations has proven challenging for current large-volume reionization simulations. In this work, we investigate these large-scale correlations using mock spectra generated from the extended SCRIPT semi-numerical reionization model. We find that while the fiducial model ensemble systematically predicts smaller correlation lengths than those inferred from the 67 sightlines in the extended XQR-30 sample, a small fraction of individual mock realizations can naturally reproduce the observed signal. Using a delete-2 jackknife analysis, we demonstrate that the observed large-scale correlation length is disproportionately driven by a rare pair of highly transmissive sightlines associated with high-redshift transmission spikes. By inserting two such highly transmissive sightlines into our mock realizations, the fraction of models consistent with the observed redshift evolution and correlation length increases significantly from 17.5% to 74.1%. Furthermore, we show that spatial fluctuations in the ionizing mean free path remain an essential physical ingredient for reproducing the observed correlation structure. Our results suggest that the unexpectedly large Lyman-$α$ correlations can be reconciled with existing reionization models when accounting for cosmic variance and the outsized statistical impact of rare, highly transmissive sightlines.

</details>


### [98] [$δ$-CDM: A Minimal Deformation of $Λ$CDM with Scalar Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08383)
*Phichayoot Baisri,Nandan Roy,Prasanta Sahoo,Soumya Chakrabarti,Jackson Levi Said*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: The paper proposes the $δ$-CDM framework—a minimal extension of $Λ$CDM—where dark energy deviations from $w = -1$ are encoded in a redshift-dependent function $δ(z)$ via $w_{\rm de}(z) = -1 + δ(z)$. Using thawing scalar field dynamics, the authors reconstruct $δ(z)$ in a model-independent way and test it against Planck CMB-SPA, DESI DR2 BAO, and Pantheon+ data. They find that the standard $\tilde{w}_0\tilde{w}_a$ parametrization is statistically favored over this specific thawing realization, but $δ$-CDM remains a flexible and robust framework for exploring dynamical dark energy.
\n\n
本文提出了 $δ$-CDM 框架——一种对 $Λ$CDM 的极简扩展——其中暗能量偏离 $w = -1$ 的行为通过红移依赖函数 $δ(z)$ 编码，定义为 $w_{\rm de}(z) = -1 + δ(z)$。作者利用 thawing 类型的标量场动力学以模型无关的方式重构 $δ(z)$，并结合 Planck CMB-SPA、DESI DR2 BAO 和 Pantheon+ 数据进行检验。结果表明，标准的 $\tilde{w}_0\tilde{w}_a$ 参数化在此特定 thawing 实现下具有更好的统计支持，但 $δ$-CDM 仍是一个灵活且稳健的框架，可用于探索动力学暗能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivated by recent DESI BAO observations suggesting possible deviations from a cosmological constant ($w = -1$), the authors aim to construct a theoretically robust and observationally testable framework to explore dynamical dark energy. The goal is to go beyond ad hoc parametrizations like $w_0w_a$ and instead anchor deviations in physical scalar field models while maintaining generality.
\n\n
受近期 DESI BAO 观测暗示宇宙学常数（$w = -1$）可能存在偏差的启发，作者旨在构建一个理论稳健且可观测检验的动力学暗能量框架。其目标是超越如 $w_0w_a$ 等特设性参数化，转而将偏差锚定在物理标量场模型中，同时保持普适性。

Method: The authors introduce the $δ$-CDM framework where $w_{\rm de}(z) = -1 + δ(z)$. They consider thawing-type effective scalar field dynamics (covering both quintessence and phantom regimes) to reconstruct $δ(z)$ without dependence on a specific Lagrangian. This reconstruction yields a universal form for $δ(z)$ dictated by initial conditions and background expansion. They then perform a Bayesian MCMC analysis using Planck CMB-SPA, DESI DR2 BAO, and Pantheon+ supernova data to compare this model against the standard $\tilde{w}_0\tilde{w}_a$ parametrization.
\n\n
作者引入 $δ$-CDM 框架，其中 $w_{\rm de}(z) = -1 + δ(z)$。他们考虑 thawing 类型的有效标量场动力学（涵盖 quintessence 和 phantom 区域），以不依赖于特定拉格朗日量的方式重构 $δ(z)$。该重构产生了一个由初始条件和背景膨胀决定的普适 $δ(z)$ 形式。随后，他们利用 Planck CMB-SPA、DESI DR2 BAO 和 Pantheon+ 超新星数据进行贝叶斯 MCMC 分析，将此模型与标准 $\tilde{w}_0\tilde{w}_a$ 参数化进行比较。

Result: The Bayesian analysis shows that the $\tilde{w}_0\tilde{w}_a$ parametrization is statistically preferred over the thawing-type $δ(z)$ realization. However, the $δ$-CDM framework successfully provides a model-independent, physically motivated description of deviations from $w = -1$, demonstrating its utility as a flexible extension of $Λ$CDM for capturing late-time dark energy dynamics.
\n\n
贝叶斯分析表明，$\tilde{w}_0\tilde{w}_a$ 参数化在统计上优于 thawing 类型的 $δ(z)$ 实现。然而，$δ$-CDM 框架成功提供了一种模型无关且物理动机明确的 $w = -1$ 偏离描述，证明了其作为 $Λ$CDM 的灵活扩展在捕捉晚期暗能量动力学方面的实用性。

Conclusion: The $δ$-CDM framework offers a minimal, robust, and physically grounded approach to testing dynamical dark energy, with the reconstructed $δ(z)$ independent of specific scalar field realizations. While current data favor the conventional $\tilde{w}_0\tilde{w}_a$ model, $δ$-CDM remains a powerful tool for future high-precision cosmological surveys aiming to distinguish between different dark energy scenarios.
\n\n
$δ$-CDM 框架提供了一种极简、稳健且物理基础扎实的动力学暗能量检验方法，其重构的 $δ(z)$ 不依赖于特定标量场实现。尽管当前数据更支持传统的 $\tilde{w}_0\tilde{w}_a$ 模型，$δ$-CDM 仍是未来高精度宇宙学巡天区分不同暗能量情景的有力工具。

Abstract: Recent DESI BAO observations provide intriguing hints that dark energy may be dynamical in nature. To investigate deviations of the dark energy equation of state (EoS) from $w = -1$, we introduce the $δ$-CDM framework, a controlled deformation of $Λ$CDM in which deviations from a cosmological constant are parametrized by a redshift-dependent function $δ(z)$, defined through $w_{\rm de}(z) = -1 + δ(z)$. As an illustrative example, we reconstruct $δ(z)$ using effective scalar field dynamics of thawing type, encompassing both quintessence and phantom regimes within a unified description. Notably, the reconstructed $δ(z)$ is independent of the specific scalar field realization, ensuring theoretical robustness. Using Planck CMB-SPA data, DESI DR2 BAO measurements, and the Pantheon+ supernova sample within a Bayesian Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis, we find that the $\tilde{w}_0\tilde{w}_a$ parametrization is preferred over this thawing-type realization of deviations from $w = -1$. Overall, the $δ$-CDM framework provides a minimal yet flexible extension of $Λ$CDM, capable of capturing late-time dynamical features of dark energy.

</details>


### [99] [Bayesian Reconstruction of the Local Universe from 2MRS: Testing the Gravitational Flow with Cosmicflows-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08593)
*Adi Nusser*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: We perform a Bayesian field-level reconstruction of the local cosmic density and velocity fields using the 2MASS Redshift Survey (2MRS) within a Zel'dovich-approximation forward model, validated against Cosmicflows-4 (CF4) peculiar velocities. The method employs an unbinned Poisson likelihood, accounts for observational systematics, and yields constrained initial conditions that reproduce large-scale flows and enable high-resolution N-body simulations.
\n\n
我们基于Zel'dovich近似前向模型，利用2MASS红移巡天（2MRS）对局部宇宙的密度场和速度场进行了贝叶斯场级重建，并通过Cosmicflows-4（CF4）星系群本动速度进行验证。该方法采用无分箱泊松点过程似然，考虑了观测系统性效应，并生成了能复现大尺度引力流、适用于高分辨率N-body模拟的约束初始条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to reconstruct the full phase-space (density and velocity) structure of the local Universe from discrete galaxy observations while rigorously accounting for selection effects, redshift-space distortions, and nonlinear evolution. Validating the inferred gravitational flow against independent peculiar velocity data (CF4) tests the fidelity of the reconstruction and its utility for generating constrained cosmological initial conditions.
\n\n
本文旨在从离散星系观测中重建局部宇宙的完整相空间（密度与速度）结构，同时严格处理选择函数、红移空间畸变和非线性演化等效应。通过与独立的本动速度数据（CF4）对比验证所推断的引力流，以检验重建的保真度及其在生成约束宇宙学初始条件方面的实用性。

Method: The authors use a Bayesian forward model based on the Zel'dovich approximation, assuming Gaussian initial conditions. The likelihood is an unbinned Poisson point-process model applied to the 2MRS redshift-space galaxy distribution, incorporating the survey’s selection function, the Zone of Avoidance, redshift-space distortions, and a scale- and distance-dependent galaxy bias. The maximum-a-posteriori (MAP) solution provides the fiducial reconstruction, while Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) generates posterior samples and constrained realizations. These initial conditions are further evolved using the Gadget-4 N-body code to assess nonlinear structure formation.
\n\n
作者采用基于Zel'dovich近似的贝叶斯前向模型，假设初始条件为高斯型。似然函数为应用于2MRS红移空间星系分布的无分箱泊松点过程模型，其中包含巡天选择函数、回避带（Zone of Avoidance）、红移空间畸变以及依赖尺度和距离的星系偏置。最大后验概率（MAP）解作为基准重建结果，而哈密顿蒙特卡洛（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo, HMC）用于生成后验样本和约束实现。这些初始条件进一步通过Gadget-4 N-body代码演化，以评估非线性结构形成。

Result: The reconstructed velocity field shows excellent agreement with CF4 in three independent tests: object-by-object comparison, density–velocity correlation, and shell-by-shell reflex dipole analysis—all performed at CF4 redshift-space positions without smoothing. The Gadget-4 simulations show that the real-space density retains the large-scale Zel'dovich features while developing nonlinear small-scale structure, and the redshift-space map exhibits realistic Fingers of God.
\n\n
重建的速度场在三项独立检验中与CF4高度一致：逐目标对比、密度–速度相关性分析、以及壳层反射偶极子（reflex dipole）检验——所有检验均在CF4红移空间位置上进行，无需对观测速度进行平滑处理。Gadget-4模拟显示，实空间密度场保留了大尺度Zel'dovich结构，同时发展出非线性小尺度结构，而红移空间分布则呈现出真实的“上帝之指”（Fingers of God）效应。

Conclusion: The Bayesian field-level inference framework successfully captures the large-scale gravitational flow of the nearby Universe from 2MRS data and produces physically consistent constrained initial conditions suitable for high-resolution N-body simulations. This validates the combination of Zel'dovich dynamics, proper treatment of observational systematics, and modern sampling techniques for cosmic structure reconstruction.
\n\n
该贝叶斯场级推断框架成功地从2MRS数据中捕捉到邻近宇宙的大尺度引力流，并生成了物理自洽、适用于高分辨率N-body模拟的约束初始条件。这验证了将Zel'dovich动力学、对观测系统性效应的恰当处理以及现代采样技术相结合用于宇宙结构重建的有效性。

Abstract: We present a Bayesian reconstruction of the local density and velocity fields traced by the 2MASS Redshift Survey (2MRS) and test the inferred gravitational flow against independent Cosmicflows-4 (CF4) galaxy-group peculiar velocities. The fiducial reconstruction is the maximum-a-posteriori (MAP) solution of a Zel'dovich-approximation forward model, constrained by the 2MRS redshift-space distribution through an unbinned Poisson point-process likelihood. The model assumes Gaussian initial conditions and includes the 2MRS selection function, the Zone of Avoidance, redshift-space distortions, and a distance-dependent galaxy-bias prescription. Hamiltonian Monte Carlo provides posterior samples and constrained realizations within the same framework. The reconstructed velocity field agrees well with CF4 in object-by-object, density--velocity-correlation, and shell-by-shell reflex-dipole tests. These comparisons are made at the CF4 redshift-space positions and do not require smoothing the observed CF4 velocities to the MAP resolution. We also evolve constrained initial conditions with Gadget-4. The real-space density retains the large-scale Zel'dovich structure while developing additional nonlinear small-scale structure, and the redshift-space distribution develops nonlinear Fingers of God. The results show that the 2MRS field-level reconstruction captures the large-scale gravitational flow of the nearby Universe and provides initial conditions suitable for constrained simulations.

</details>


### [100] [Mock Catalogs of Strongly Lensed Gravitational Waves via a Halo Model Approach with Space-borne Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08899)
*Mingqi Sun,Kai Liao,Youkai Li,Tonghua Liu,Hengyu Wu,Shaoqi Hou,Tao Yang,Xilong Fan,Marek Biesiada*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper constructs a mock catalog (GW-LMC-Space) of strongly lensed gravitational-wave events for space-borne detectors like LISA and DECIGO, predicting up to 131 lensed massive black hole binary (MBHB) events for LISA over four years (lensing probability ~0.3%) and up to 44 lensed stellar-mass compact binary events for DECIGO in one year (lensing probability ~0.15%). It emphasizes that overlapping lensed signals are common and significantly impact SNR estimation and event identification.
\n\n
本文构建了一个面向LISA和DECIGO等空间引力波探测器的强引力透镜引力波事件模拟星表（GW-LMC-Space），预测LISA在四年观测中最多可探测131个强透镜大质量黑洞双星（MBHB）事件（透镜概率约0.3%），而DECIGO在一年内最多可探测44个强透镜恒星级致密双星事件（透镜概率约0.15%）。文章强调，透镜信号重叠是空间探测器中的普遍现象，会显著影响信噪比（SNR）估计和事件识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Future space-borne gravitational-wave observatories like LISA and DECIGO will detect numerous GW events, some of which may be strongly lensed by galaxies or clusters. Understanding the rate, properties, and observational signatures of such lensed events is crucial for accurate data analysis, cosmological inference, and avoiding misidentification. Current models lack realistic joint simulations of source populations, lens distributions, and detector response—including signal overlap effects.
\n\n
未来的空间引力波天文台（如LISA和DECIGO）将探测到大量引力波事件，其中部分可能被星系或星系团强引力透镜化。准确理解此类透镜事件的发生率、性质及观测特征，对数据分析、宇宙学推断以及避免误认至关重要。现有模型缺乏对源种群、透镜分布与探测器响应（包括信号重叠效应）的联合真实模拟。

Method: The authors develop a comprehensive simulation framework called GW-LMC-Space that combines realistic astrophysical models for massive black hole binaries (MBHBs) and stellar-mass compact binaries (BBHs, BNSs, NSBHs) with empirical or semi-analytic models for galaxy and cluster lens populations. They generate mock catalogs of strongly lensed GW events by computing lensing optical depths and cross-correlating source and lens redshift distributions. The analysis incorporates the instrumental response of LISA and DECIGO, including waveform modeling and the effect of temporal overlap between multiple lensed images or distinct events.
\n\n
作者开发了一个名为GW-LMC-Space的综合模拟框架，结合了大质量黑洞双星（MBHBs）和恒星级致密双星（BBHs、BNSs、NSBHs）的真实天体物理模型，以及基于观测或半解析的星系与星系团透镜种群模型。通过计算透镜光学深度并关联源与透镜的红移分布，生成强透镜引力波事件的模拟星表。分析中纳入了LISA和DECIGO的仪器响应，包括波形建模以及多个透镜像或不同事件之间的时间重叠效应。

Result: For a four-year LISA mission, the expected number of strongly lensed MBHB events ranges from $0$ to $131$, corresponding to a lensing probability of up to $\sim 0.3\%$, depending on the MBHB formation model. For DECIGO operating for one year, the expected number of lensed stellar-mass binary events (BBHs, BNSs, NSBHs) ranges from $0$ to $44$, with a lensing probability of $\sim 0.15\%$. Crucially, the study finds that overlapping lensed signals are frequent in space-borne detectors, which distorts signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) estimates and complicates event identification.
\n\n
对于四年期的LISA任务，强透镜MBHB事件的预期数量在$0$到$131$之间，对应透镜概率最高达$\sim 0.3\%$，具体取决于所采用的MBHB形成模型。对于运行一年的DECIGO，强透镜恒星级双星事件（BBHs、BNSs、NSBHs）的预期数量为$0$至$44$，透镜概率约为$\sim 0.15\%$。关键发现是：在空间探测器中，透镜信号重叠十分常见，会扭曲信噪比（SNR）估计并使事件识别复杂化。

Conclusion: Strong gravitational lensing of GWs will be a non-negligible phenomenon in future space-borne observations. The developed GW-LMC-Space framework provides essential mock data for preparing detection pipelines. Importantly, signal overlap must be explicitly modeled in data analysis to avoid biases in SNR, parameter estimation, and lensing identification—highlighting a critical challenge for upcoming missions like LISA and DECIGO.
\n\n
引力波的强引力透镜效应将在未来的空间观测中成为不可忽略的现象。所开发的GW-LMC-Space框架为探测流程的准备提供了关键的模拟数据。尤为重要的是，数据分析中必须显式建模信号重叠效应，以避免在信噪比、参数估计和透镜识别中引入偏差——这对LISA和DECIGO等即将开展的任务构成了关键挑战。

Abstract: Future space-borne gravitational-wave (GW) detectors, such as LISA and DECIGO, are expected to detect a large number of GW events, a fraction of which may be strongly lensed by intervening galaxies or galaxy clusters. In this work, we develop a comprehensive framework to simulate strongly lensed GWs in the context of space-borne detectors. Based on realistic astrophysical models for both the source population and the lens distribution, we construct mock catalogs of lensed GW events, referred to as \textbf{GW-LMC-Space}. Our results show that, for a four-year LISA observation, the expected number of lensed events ranges from $0$ to $131$, depending on the adopted formation model of massive black hole binaries (MBHBs). The corresponding lensing probability for MBHBs can reach up to $\sim 0.3\%$. For DECIGO, we find that the number of lensed events in a one-year observation is expected to lie in the range of $0$--$44$, with a lensing probability of $\sim 0.15\%$ for stellar-mass binary black holes (BBHs), binary neutron stars (BNSs), and neutron star--black hole binaries (NSBHs). We further show that the overlap of lensed signals is a common feature in space-borne detectors, which can significantly affect both the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) estimation and event identification. These results highlight the importance of accounting for signal overlap in the analysis of strongly lensed GW events in future space-borne GW observations.

</details>


### [101] [Optimising ultra-light dark matter searches with ground-based interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09210)
*Paola C. M. Delgado,Ornella J. Piccinni,Federico R. Urban*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: Ultra-light dark matter (ULDM) with couplings to the Standard Model generates nearly monochromatic gravitational-wave detector signals whose spectral shape is modulated by Earth's rotation. This sidereal modulation enables spin discrimination for ULDM below ~30 Hz and improves LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA excess-power constraints by up to ~36%. An optimized cross-correlation statistic in the Band-Sampled-Data framework boosts sensitivity by up to ~42% (low-f) and ~35% (high-f).
\n\n
具有与Standard Model耦合的Ultra-light dark matter（ULDM）会在引力波探测器中产生近单色信号，其谱形受地球自转引起的sidereal modulation调制。这种调制使得在~30 Hz以下可区分ULDM的spin-1与spin-2信号，并将LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA的excess-power约束提升最多~36%。此外，在Band-Sampled-Data框架中优化的cross-correlation统计量可将低频和高频灵敏度分别提升最多~42%和~35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current searches for ultra-light dark matter (ULDM) using gravitational-wave interferometers often assume featureless monochromatic signals, neglecting the rich spectral structure induced by Earth’s rotation (sidereal modulation). Incorporating this modulation can enhance detection sensitivity and enable discrimination between different ULDM spins—particularly crucial at low frequencies where terrestrial and instrumental noise dominate.
\n\n
当前利用引力波干涉仪对ultra-light dark matter（ULDM）的搜寻通常假设信号为无特征的单色波，忽略了由地球自转（sidereal modulation）引起的丰富谱结构。纳入这一调制效应不仅能提升探测灵敏度，还能在以地面和仪器噪声为主的低频段有效区分不同spin的ULDM信号。

Method: The authors model the time-dependent signal from vector (spin-1) and tensor (spin-2) ULDM fields coupled to Standard Model fields, accounting for Earth’s rotation which induces a time-varying Doppler shift and anisotropic antenna response. They incorporate the resulting sidereal sidebands into the excess-power search pipeline of LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA. Furthermore, they reformulate the cross-correlation statistic within the Band-Sampled-Data (BSD) framework to optimally weight frequency bins based on expected signal morphology, maximizing signal-to-noise ratio.
\n\n
作者对与Standard Model场耦合的vector（spin-1）和tensor（spin-2）ULDM场所产生的时变信号进行建模，考虑地球自转导致的时变Doppler频移和各向异性antenna response。他们将由此产生的sidereal sidebands纳入LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA的excess-power搜寻流程。此外，他们在Band-Sampled-Data（BSD）框架内重构cross-correlation统计量，根据预期信号形态对频率bin进行最优加权，以最大化signal-to-noise ratio。

Result: Including sidereal modulation features improves existing low-frequency excess-power constraints on ULDM couplings by up to ~36%. The optimized BSD cross-correlation method enhances sensitivity across almost the full band: by up to ~42% at low frequencies (<30 Hz) and ~35% at high frequencies (>30 Hz), compared to conventional methods.
\n\n
纳入sidereal modulation特征可将现有对ULDM耦合的低频excess-power约束提升最多~36%。优化后的BSD cross-correlation方法几乎在整个频带内提升灵敏度：与传统方法相比，低频段（<30 Hz）提升最多~42%，高频段（>30 Hz）提升最多~35%。

Conclusion: Exploiting the spectral signatures of sidereal modulation significantly sharpens the reach of gravitational-wave detectors in probing ultra-light dark matter. The proposed enhancements to both excess-power and cross-correlation analyses demonstrate that careful modeling of signal morphology—beyond simple monochromatic assumptions—is essential for maximizing discovery potential in current and future detector networks.
\n\n
利用sidereal modulation的谱特征可显著增强引力波探测器对ultra-light dark matter的探测能力。所提出的对excess-power和cross-correlation分析的改进表明，超越简单单色假设、精细建模信号形态对于最大化当前及未来探测器网络的发现潜力至关重要。

Abstract: Ultra-light dark matter fields induce nearly monochromatic signals in gravitational-wave detectors through their coupling to the Standard Model. Their spectral morphology exhibits features caused by sidereal modulation that, for frequencies below $\sim 30~$Hz, enable discrimination between spin-1 and spin-2 ultra-light dark matter signals, provided sufficient signal-to-noise ratio. In the context of LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA search techniques, we show that incorporating these spectral features can improve current excess-power constraints at low frequencies by up to $\sim36\%$. Additionally, we propose an optimised implementation of the cross-correlation statistics within the Band-Sampled-Data framework, enhancing the sensitivity of cross-correlation searches across nearly the entire frequency range, reaching up to $\sim42\%$ at low frequencies and $\sim35\%$ at high frequencies.

</details>


### [102] [Modifying $Λ$CDM dynamics via out-of-equilibrium axions: reconciling SH0ES and DESI $H_0$ values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09427)
*Giovanni Montani,Luis A. Escamilla,Nakia Carlevaro,Francesco Cianfrani*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper proposes a late-Universe cosmological model where a small fraction of axion dark matter drives the system out of thermal equilibrium, modifying $Λ$CDM dynamics at $z \lesssim 1$. The model yields $H_0 \simeq 73~{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, aligning with SH0ES while remaining consistent with DESI BAO data.
\n\n
本文提出了一种晚期宇宙学模型，其中一小部分轴子暗物质使系统偏离热平衡，在 $z \lesssim 1$ 处修正了 $Λ$CDM 动力学。该模型给出 $H_0 \simeq 73~{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$，与 SH0ES 局域测量一致，同时与 DESI 的重子声学振荡（BAO）数据保持良好吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the persistent Hubble tension between early-Universe inferences (e.g., Planck CMB within $Λ$CDM) and local distance-ladder measurements (e.g., SH0ES), which report $H_0 \approx 73~{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$. The authors aim to resolve this by introducing non-thermal, collisional axion dark matter that modifies late-time expansion without spoiling early-Universe success.
\n\n
动机源于早期宇宙推断（如基于 $Λ$CDM 的 Planck CMB 数据）与局域距离阶梯测量（如 SH0ES）之间持续存在的哈勃张力，后者报告 $H_0 \approx 73~{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$。作者旨在通过引入非热、具有碰撞效应的轴子暗物质来解决这一问题，该成分在不破坏早期宇宙成功描述的前提下修正晚期宇宙膨胀历史。

Method: The authors employ two complementary theoretical descriptions—kinetic theory and classical field theory—for the axion component. Both approaches lead to the same macroscopic evolution equation for the dark matter energy density. They embed this modified dark matter sector into a cosmological background and confront it with late-Universe observational data, including SH0ES $H_0$ calibration and DESI Baryon Acoustic Oscillation (BAO) measurements, using Bayesian statistical analysis.
\n\n
作者采用了两种互补的理论描述——动力学理论和经典场论——来刻画轴子组分。两种方法导出了相同的暗物质能量密度宏观演化方程。他们将这一修正后的暗物质组分嵌入宇宙学背景中，并利用贝叶斯统计分析，将其与晚期宇宙观测数据（包括 SH0ES 的 $H_0$ 校准和 DESI 的重子声学振荡（BAO）测量）进行对比。

Result: The collisional axion model is statistically preferred over standard $Λ$CDM when SH0ES data are included, yielding a best-fit Hubble constant $H_0 \simeq 73~{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$. The model remains consistent with DESI BAO data and recovers standard $Λ$CDM behavior at high redshift ($z \gg 1$), ensuring compatibility with early-Universe constraints.
\n\n
当包含 SH0ES 数据时，具有碰撞效应的轴子模型在统计上优于标准 $Λ$CDM 模型，其最佳拟合哈勃常数为 $H_0 \simeq 73~{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$。该模型与 DESI BAO 数据保持一致，并在高红移（$z \gg 1$）下恢复标准 $Λ$CDM 行为，从而确保与早期宇宙约束相容。

Conclusion: A small non-thermal axion dark matter component can reconcile the Hubble tension by enhancing late-time expansion while preserving agreement with both early- and late-Universe probes. This demonstrates that physics beyond $Λ$CDM in the dark sector—specifically through collisional axion dynamics—offers a viable resolution to current cosmological tensions.
\n\n
一小部分非热轴子暗物质组分可通过增强晚期宇宙膨胀来调和哈勃张力，同时保持与早期和晚期宇宙探针的一致性。这表明暗物质 sector 中超出 $Λ$CDM 的物理——特别是通过具有碰撞效应的轴子动力学——为当前宇宙学张力提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate late-Universe dynamics in which the dark matter component is described by axion particles. The proposed framework departs from the standard $Λ$CDM paradigm due to a small fraction of axions driving the system away from thermal equilibrium. We analyze the evolution of the axion energy density using both a kinetic and a classical field approach, yielding an identical macroscopic evolution equation for the dark matter density. The resulting scenario modifies $Λ$CDM dynamics in the late Universe (specifically at $z \lesssim 1$), while asymptotically recovering the standard baseline at earlier cosmic epochs. We compare the theoretical predictions of our formulation against a comprehensive suite of late-Universe datasets. Our statistical analysis reveals that when the SH0ES local calibration is included, the collisional axion model becomes significantly favored over $Λ$CDM, yielding a best-fit Hubble constant of $H_0 \simeq 73~{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$. Ultimately, this cosmological scenario successfully accommodates local distance-ladder measurements while maintaining excellent agreement with Baryon Acoustic Oscillation data from the DESI Collaboration.

</details>


### [103] [Spectral distortion anisotropies from photon to dark photon conversions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09491)
*Sara Evangelista,Jens Chluba,Bryce Cyr*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper investigates anisotropic spectral distortions in the CMB induced by resonant conversions of photons into dark photons, using the Frequency Hierarchy (FH) framework within CosmoTherm. It shows that the dark photon mass governs the multipole structure of the distortion power spectra, while the kinetic mixing parameter sets the amplitude. Planck data yield constraints nearly as strong as COBE/FIRAS monopole limits, and high-redshift conversions may generate isocurvature perturbations.
\n\n
本文利用CosmoTherm中的Frequency Hierarchy（FH）框架，研究了光子-暗光子共振转换在CMB中诱导的各向异性谱畸变。研究表明，暗光子质量决定了畸变功率谱的多极结构，而动能混合参数决定信号幅度。Planck数据给出的限制仅略弱于COBE/FIRAS对单极畸变的限制；此外，高红移（$z>2\\times 10^6$）的转换可能产生等曲率型扰动，在畸变各向异性热化的情形下可带来新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous studies of dark photon-induced CMB spectral distortions focused only on the isotropic (monopole) component, primarily constrained by COBE/FIRAS. However, anisotropic spectral distortions—arising from spatial variations in the conversion process—contain additional information about the dark photon mass and coupling. The recent development of the Frequency Hierarchy (FH) framework enables a consistent treatment of these anisotropies, motivating a more complete analysis that can leverage modern CMB anisotropy data (e.g., from Planck) to place complementary constraints on dark photon models.
\n\n
以往关于暗光子诱导CMB谱畸变的研究主要集中于各向同性（单极）成分，并主要受COBE/FIRAS数据限制。然而，由转换过程空间涨落引起的各向异性谱畸变包含了关于暗光子质量和耦合的额外信息。近期发展的Frequency Hierarchy（FH）框架使得对这些各向异性的自洽处理成为可能，从而激励利用现代CMB各向异性数据（如Planck）对暗光子模型施加互补性限制。

Method: The authors employ the Frequency Hierarchy (FH) framework implemented in the CosmoTherm code to compute anisotropic CMB spectral distortions from resonant photon–dark photon oscillations. They calculate photon transfer functions across the dark photon parameter space (mass $m_{A'}$ and kinetic mixing $\epsilon$), and derive the resulting distortion cross-power spectra between different frequency channels. These spectra are then compared with Planck High-Frequency Instrument (HFI) data to set observational bounds. Additionally, they analyze the induced corrections to the standard CMB temperature field, particularly focusing on potential isocurvature perturbations from conversions at $z > 2\times 10^6$.
\n\n
作者采用CosmoTherm代码中实现的Frequency Hierarchy（FH）框架，计算由光子–暗光子共振振荡引起的各向异性CMB谱畸变。他们在暗光子参数空间（质量$m_{A'}$和动能混合参数$\epsilon$）中计算光子传递函数，并推导不同频率通道之间的畸变交叉功率谱。随后将这些谱与Planck高频仪器（HFI）数据对比以设定观测限制。此外，他们还分析了对标准CMB温度场的修正，特别关注红移$z > 2\\times 10^6$处转换可能产生的等曲率扰动。

Result: The dark photon mass $m_{A'}$ directly determines the multipole dependence (i.e., angular scale) of the anisotropic distortion power spectra, while the kinetic mixing parameter $\epsilon$ controls the overall signal amplitude. Using Planck HFI data, the authors obtain constraints on the minimal dark photon model that are only marginally weaker than those from COBE/FIRAS monopole measurements. Furthermore, they show that photon–dark photon conversions at redshifts $z \gtrsim 2\times 10^6$ can imprint isocurvature-type perturbations on the CMB, potentially offering new constraints if such anisotropies do not fully thermalize.
\n\n
暗光子质量$m_{A'}$直接决定了各向异性畸变功率谱的多极依赖性（即角尺度），而动能混合参数$\epsilon$控制信号的整体幅度。利用Planck HFI数据，作者对最小暗光子模型获得的限制仅略弱于COBE/FIRAS单极测量的结果。此外，他们指出在红移$z \\gtrsim 2\\times 10^6$处的光子–暗光子转换可在CMB上留下等曲率型扰动，若此类各向异性未完全热化，则可能提供新的约束。

Conclusion: Anisotropic CMB spectral distortions provide a powerful and complementary probe of dark photon models beyond traditional monopole analyses. The Frequency Hierarchy framework enables precise computation of these signals, revealing a clear mapping between dark photon parameters and observable spectral features. Current Planck data already offer competitive constraints, and future CMB spectrometers (e.g., PIXIE/PRISM) could dramatically improve sensitivity. Moreover, high-redshift conversions may link dark photon physics to primordial isocurvature modes, opening another observational window.
\n\n
各向异性CMB谱畸变为暗光子模型提供了超越传统单极分析的强大且互补的探针。Frequency Hierarchy框架使得对这些信号的精确计算成为可能，揭示了暗光子参数与可观测谱特征之间的清晰映射。当前Planck数据已提供具有竞争力的限制，而未来的CMB光谱仪（如PIXIE/PRISM）可显著提升灵敏度。此外，高红移转换可能将暗光子物理与原初等曲率模联系起来，开辟另一观测窗口。

Abstract: Dark photons are a gauge boson of a hypothetical dark sector, representing one of the most-studied minimal extensions of the Standard Model, with wide-ranging theoretical and observational implications. Here, we consider scenarios in which an initially unpopulated dark photon sector is populated via resonant photon to dark photon conversions. This process leads to observable spectral distortions in the cosmic microwave background (CMB), that can be used to constrain these models. We extend previous spectral distortion studies of the monopole spectrum to anisotropic spectral distortions, using the newly developed Frequency Hierarchy (FH) framework of CosmoTherm. We illustrate the physics by presenting detailed computations of the photon transfer functions and distortion cross power spectra throughout the dark photon parameter space. We find that the dark photon mass explicitly controls the shape (i.e., multipole-dependence) of the signal power spectra, while the overall amplitude of the signal is determined by the kinetic mixing parameter of the model. Using these results, we place complementary limits on the minimal dark photon model using data from Planck, finding that the constraints are only marginally weaker than those obtained with COBE/FIRAS data for the average (monopole) distortion. In addition, we compute the corrections to the standard temperature field, arguing that conversions at redshifts larger than $2\times 10^6$ may add iso-curvature type perturbations, which could lead to novel constraints in regimes where distortion anisotropies thermalize.

</details>


### [104] [Pantheon+ supernovae corrected for progenitor age indicate the universe is decelerating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09650)
*Animesh Sah,Mohamed Rameez,Subir Sarkar*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: The paper investigates how progenitor age-dependent luminosity evolution in Type Ia supernovae affects the cosmographic measurement of the deceleration parameter $q_0$. Applying redshift-dependent age corrections to the Pantheon+ dataset shifts the large-scale monopole of $q_0$ to positive values (indicating cosmic deceleration), while preserving the previously detected local dipole anisotropy aligned with the bulk flow.
\n\n
本文研究了Ia型超新星前身星年龄依赖的光度演化对减速度参数$q_0$宇宙学测量的影响。将红移依赖的年龄修正应用于Pantheon+数据集后，大尺度单极$q_0$被移至正值（表明宇宙减速），而先前探测到的与整体流对齐的局部偶极各向异性基本保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from a recent finding that the deceleration parameter $q_0$ exhibits a strong local dipole anisotropy aligned with the bulk flow and only a small monopole at distances beyond a few hundred Mpc. This challenges the standard assumption of isotropy in cosmography. The authors aim to test whether systematic effects—specifically, progenitor age-dependent luminosity evolution of Type Ia supernovae—could alter the inferred monopole component of $q_0$, potentially reconciling observations with expectations from the standard cosmological model.
\n\n
动机源于近期发现减速度参数$q_0$在局部呈现与整体流对齐的强偶极各向异性，而在数百Mpc以外仅剩微弱单极成分。这挑战了宇宙学中各向同性的标准假设。作者旨在检验系统性效应——特别是Ia型超新星前身星年龄依赖的光度演化——是否会改变推断出的$q_0$单极分量，从而可能使观测结果与标准宇宙学模型预期相一致。

Method: The authors apply redshift-dependent luminosity corrections to the Pantheon+ Type Ia supernova catalogue, accounting for the evolutionary effect where older progenitor systems produce intrinsically fainter supernovae. They then perform a cosmographic analysis using redshift tomography to separately extract the monopole and dipole components of the deceleration parameter $q_0$ as a function of distance scale.
\n\n
作者对Pantheon+ Ia型超新星目录应用红移依赖的光度修正，考虑了较老前身星系统产生本征更暗超新星的演化效应。随后，他们采用红移层析方法进行宇宙学分析，以分离提取减速度参数$q_0$在不同距离尺度上的单极和偶极分量。

Result: After applying progenitor age-dependent corrections, the monopole component of $q_0$ at large scales ($\\gtrsim$ few hundred Mpc) shifts from near-zero or slightly negative values to positive values, indicating cosmic deceleration consistent with expectations in a matter-dominated universe prior to dark energy domination. The local dipole anisotropy remains robust and largely unaffected by these corrections.
\n\n
在应用前身星年龄依赖修正后，大尺度（$\\gtrsim$ 数百Mpc）上$q_0$的单极分量从接近零或略负值转变为正值，表明宇宙处于减速状态，这与暗能量主导前物质主导宇宙的预期一致。局部偶极各向异性则保持稳健，基本不受这些修正影响。

Conclusion: Progenitor age-dependent luminosity evolution is a non-negligible systematic effect in cosmographic analyses using Type Ia supernovae. Correcting for this effect restores a positive deceleration parameter on cosmological scales, supporting the standard $\\Lambda$CDM picture, while confirming that the local dipole anisotropy in $q_0$ is likely a genuine kinematic feature tied to large-scale structure rather than an artifact of supernova systematics.
\n\n
前身星年龄依赖的光度演化是在使用Ia型超新星进行宇宙学分析中不可忽略的系统效应。对该效应进行修正确保了在宇宙学尺度上减速度参数为正值，支持标准$\\Lambda$CDM图像；同时证实$q_0$中的局部偶极各向异性很可能是与大尺度结构相关的真正运动学特征，而非超新星系统误差所致。

Abstract: We examine the impact of progenitor age-dependent luminosity evolution of Type Ia supernovae on a cosmographic measurement of the deceleration parameter $q_0$. Our recent redshift tomographic analysis showed that locally $q_0$ has a strong dipole anisotropy aligned approximately with the bulk flow, and only a small monopole component remains at distances exceeding a few hundred Mpc. Applying redshift-dependent corrections for progenitor age to the Pantheon+ catalogue, we find that this shifts the monopole component of $q_0$ to positive values (i.e. deceleration), while leaving the local dipole component essentially unchanged.

</details>


### [105] [Stochastic constant-roll inflation beyond the hilltop with the spectral method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09690)
*Eemeli Tomberg*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper studies stochastic inflation for a quadratic hilltop potential (constant-roll inflation) using the spectral method to solve the Fokker-Planck equation. It finds that rare trajectories crossing the hilltop and reflecting dominate the mean first-passage time, rendering the mean unrepresentative; the median is preferred, yielding a non-monotonic coarse-grained $\\Delta N$ distribution with a peak at maximal $\\Delta N$, relevant for primordial black hole formation.
\n\n
本文利用谱方法求解Fokker-Planck方程，研究了二次hilltop势（对应constant-roll inflation）下的随机暴胀。研究发现，那些罕见的穿越hilltop并在反射边界附近滞留的轨迹主导了平均首达时间（mean first-passage time），使得平均值无法恰当描述暴胀背景；采用中位数后，得到的粗粒化$\\Delta N$分布呈现出非单调行为——其著名的指数尾部先变平，随后在最大$\\Delta N$处形成一个峰值，这对原初黑洞（primordial black hole）模型具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous analyses of stochastic constant-roll inflation assumed trajectories remain near the hilltop without considering those that cross it and interact with a reflecting boundary. This leads to an incomplete understanding of the perturbation statistics and potentially misleading predictions for observables like the mean number of e-folds. The author aims to resolve this by fully solving the Fokker-Planck dynamics and examining the role of rare but dominant trajectories.
\n\n
以往对随机constant-roll暴胀的分析假设轨迹始终停留在hilltop附近，未考虑那些穿越hilltop并与反射边界相互作用的轨迹。这导致对扰动统计的理解不完整，并可能对诸如e-fold数均值等可观测量给出误导性预测。作者旨在通过完整求解Fokker-Planck动力学并考察这些罕见但起主导作用的轨迹的作用来解决这一问题。

Method: The author employs the spectral decomposition of the Fokker-Planck operator associated with stochastic inflation in a quadratic hilltop potential. Explicit eigenvalues and eigenfunctions are computed, enabling exact evaluation of the probability distribution for the coarse-grained number of e-folds $\\Delta N$. The analysis distinguishes between mean and median statistics, showing that the lowest eigenmode governs the long-time asymptotics and dominates rare tunneling-like events across the potential barrier.
\n\n
作者采用与二次hilltop势下随机暴胀相关的Fokker-Planck算符的谱分解方法，显式计算了本征值和本征函数，从而精确评估粗粒化e-fold数$\\Delta N$的概率分布。分析区分了均值与中位数统计量，表明最低本征模主导长时间渐进行为，并支配穿越势垒的类隧穿稀有事件。

Result: The mean first-passage time is dominated by extremely rare trajectories that cross the hilltop and reflect, making the mean unrepresentative of typical inflationary histories. Using the median instead, the coarse-grained $\\Delta N$ distribution exhibits a modified tail: the standard exponential decay first flattens and then develops a sharp peak near a maximum $\\Delta N$, indicating a cutoff in achievable super-horizon fluctuations.
\n\n
平均首达时间由极罕见的穿越hilltop并反射的轨迹主导，使得均值无法代表典型的暴胀历史。若改用中位数，则粗粒化$\\Delta N$分布展现出修正的尾部行为：标准的指数衰减首先变平，随后在最大$\\Delta N$附近形成一个尖锐峰值，表明超视界涨落存在一个上限。

Conclusion: The standard use of the mean in stochastic inflation can be misleading when rare barrier-crossing trajectories dominate statistical measures. A median-based approach reveals richer structure in the $\\Delta N$ distribution, including a peak at maximal $\\Delta N$, which has implications for models of primordial black hole formation where large fluctuations are essential.
\n\n
在随机暴胀中，当罕见的越垒轨迹主导统计量时，使用均值可能是误导性的。基于中位数的方法揭示了$\\Delta N$分布中更丰富的结构，包括在最大$\\Delta N$处的峰值，这对依赖大涨落的原初黑洞形成模型具有重要启示。

Abstract: Stochastic inflation can be used to study large inflationary perturbations. This paper presents such a study for a quadratic hilltop potential, corresponding to constant-roll inflation. I solve the perturbation distribution using the spectral method, with detailed solutions of the eigenvalues and eigenfunctions of the Fokker-Planck operator. Contrary to previous studies of stochastic constant-roll inflation, the solution allows trajectories that cross the hilltop and get stuck near a reflecting boundary on the other side, tunneling out slowly in a way dictated by the lowest eigensolution. Despite their rarity, these trajectories turn out to dominate the mean first-passage time. For this reason, I argue the mean does not properly describe the inflationary background. Using the median instead, I compute the distribution of the coarse-grained $ΔN$ distribution and show that its well-known exponential tail first flattens out and then forms a peak near a maximal $ΔN$ value. I argue similar intricacies arise in primordial black hole models.

</details>


### [106] [Universal Suppression of Gravitational Waves from Black Hole Evaporation Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09804)
*Xin-Chen He,Xiao-Han Ma,Misao Sasaki,Volodymyr Takhistov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: Evaporating primordial black holes (PBHs) with broad initial mass distributions universally produce a late-time gravitational wave (GW) background suppressed by a characteristic power law dictated by Hawking evaporation dynamics, independent of the initial mass function details.
\n\n
具有宽初始质量分布的蒸发原初黑洞（Primordial Black Holes, PBHs）在晚期普遍产生一个引力波（Gravitational Wave, GW）背景，其被霍金蒸发动力学所决定的特征幂律所压低，与初始质量函数的具体形式无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding how the stochastic gravitational wave background induced by evaporating primordial black holes depends on their initial mass distribution is crucial for interpreting potential GW signals and constraining early Universe physics; prior works observed suppression in specific cases (e.g., critical collapse), but a universal mechanism was lacking.
\n\n
理解由蒸发原初黑洞（PBHs）诱导的随机引力波背景如何依赖于其初始质量分布，对于解释潜在的引力波信号和限制早期宇宙物理至关重要；以往工作在特定情形（如临界坍缩）中观察到压低效应，但缺乏普适机制。

Method: The authors analyze the time evolution of PBH populations undergoing Hawking evaporation with generic finite-width mass functions during an early matter-dominated era. They track the induced scalar-induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) sourced by curvature perturbations reentering the horizon during radiation domination, and study the asymptotic behavior of the GW spectrum as PBHs evaporate.
\n\n
作者分析了在早期物质主导时期内、经历霍金蒸发且具有任意有限宽度质量函数的原初黑洞（PBH）种群的时间演化。他们追踪由曲率扰动在辐射主导时期重新进入视界所诱导的标量诱导引力波（Scalar-Induced Gravitational Waves, SIGWs），并研究当PBH蒸发时引力波谱的渐近行为。

Result: For a broad class of initial PBH mass distributions, the late-time GW spectrum exhibits a universal power-law suppression $\\Omega_{\\rm GW}(f) \\propto f^{-n}$, where the exponent $n$ is determined solely by the black hole evaporation law ($M \\propto t^{1/3}$ for Hawking radiation). This explains the previously observed suppression in critical collapse models as a special case of this general phenomenon.
\n\n
对于一大类初始原初黑洞（PBH）质量分布，晚期引力波谱表现出普适的幂律压低 $\\Omega_{\\rm GW}(f) \\propto f^{-n}$，其中指数 $n$ 仅由黑洞蒸发定律决定（霍金辐射下 $M \\propto t^{1/3}$）。这解释了先前在临界坍缩模型中观测到的压低现象是该普适机制的一个特例。

Conclusion: The asymptotic shape of the gravitational wave background from evaporating PBHs is universally governed by evaporation dynamics rather than initial conditions, establishing a direct observational link between the GW spectrum and the fundamental law of black hole evaporation.
\n\n
蒸发原初黑洞（PBHs）产生的引力波背景的渐近形状普适地由蒸发动力学而非初始条件所决定，从而在引力波谱与黑洞蒸发基本定律之间建立了直接的观测联系。

Abstract: Evaporating black holes can leave distinct imprints on gravitational wave (GW) backgrounds. We show that black hole populations with finite width mass distributions exhibit a universal late time evolution governed by the evaporation dynamics rather than the details of the initial mass distribution, leading to a characteristic power law suppression of the induced GWs. We demonstrate this for a broad class of mass functions in primordial black hole (PBH) scenarios featuring an early Universe matter-dominated era, and identify the suppression of PBH-induced GWs found for critical collapse distributions as a manifestation of this general phenomenon. Our results establish a direct connection between the asymptotic GW spectrum and the underlying law of black hole evaporation.

</details>


### [107] [Inflationary interpretation of the gravitational-wave signal in the European Pulsar Timing Array DR2 with constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09810)
*Philippe Turgeon,Chiara Caprini,Anton Chudaykin,Martin Kunz,Delphine Perrodin,Ismael Cognard,Lucas Guillemot,Gilles Theureau*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: The EPTA's second data release hints at a gravitational-wave background; this paper investigates whether inflationary models can explain it. By parametrizing the tensor power spectrum with $r$, $n_t$, $T_{\text{rh}}$, and $f_{\text{end}}$, and combining constraints from CMB, BBN, and LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA, the authors narrow viable parameter space. The best-fit scenario involves tensor modes re-entering during radiation domination, requiring a large $r$, blue-tilted $n_t$, but very low reheating temperatures that are theoretically challenging.
\n\n
欧洲脉冲星计时阵列（EPTA）第二次数据发布暗示存在引力波背景；本文探讨暴胀模型能否解释该信号。通过以张量功率谱参数$r$、$n_t$、$T_{\text{rh}}$和$f_{\text{end}}$进行参数化，并结合CMB、BBN及LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA的观测约束，作者缩小了可行参数空间。最佳拟合情形涉及张量模在辐射主导时期重新进入哈勃半径，要求较大的$r$、蓝倾斜的$n_t$，但极低的再热温度在理论上难以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The recent detection of a common-spectrum process by the EPTA collaboration may correspond to a stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB). While astrophysical sources like supermassive black hole binaries are leading candidates, a cosmological origin—particularly from primordial tensor perturbations generated during inflation—is also plausible. The motivation is to assess whether standard or extended inflationary scenarios can produce a SGWB consistent with the EPTA signal while satisfying all other cosmological and gravitational-wave bounds.
\n\n
EPTA合作组最近探测到一个共谱过程，可能对应于随机引力波背景（SGWB）。尽管超大质量黑洞双星等天体物理源是主要候选者，但宇宙学起源——特别是暴胀期间产生的原初张量扰动——同样合理。本文旨在评估标准或扩展的暴胀模型是否能在满足所有其他宇宙学与引力波观测限制的前提下，产生与EPTA信号一致的SGWB。

Method: The authors adopt a phenomenological parametrization of the inflationary tensor power spectrum: ${\\cal P}_h(f) \\propto r \\left(f/f_*\\right)^{n_t}$, truncated above a cut-off frequency $f_{\\text{end}}$. They vary four key parameters: the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$ (at pivot scale), the tensor spectral index $n_t$, the reheating temperature $T_{\\text{rh}}$ (which sets the transition between reheating and radiation domination and thus affects the GW spectrum shape at nHz scales), and $f_{\\text{end}}$ (related to the energy scale of inflation or the end of the production mechanism). They perform a Bayesian likelihood analysis incorporating EPTA DR2 data alongside constraints from Planck (CMB), BBN (via $N_{\\rm eff}$ and light element abundances), and LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA upper limits on the SGWB at higher frequencies.
\n\n
作者采用暴胀张量功率谱的现象学参数化形式：${\\cal P}_h(f) \\propto r \\left(f/f_*\\right)^{n_t}$，并在高于截断频率$f_{\\text{end}}$处截断。他们变动四个关键参数：张量标量比$r$（在基准尺度）、张量谱指数$n_t$、再热温度$T_{\\text{rh}}$（决定再热结束与辐射主导时期的过渡，从而影响nHz频段的GW谱形），以及$f_{\\text{end}}$（与暴胀能标或产生机制终止相关）。他们结合EPTA DR2数据，同时纳入Planck（CMB）、BBN（通过$N_{\\rm eff}$和轻元素丰度）以及LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA在高频段对SGWB的上限约束，进行贝叶斯似然分析。

Result: After imposing all observational constraints, the viable region for interpreting the EPTA signal cosmologically is significantly reduced. At 95% CL, they find: $-11.66 \\lesssim \\log_{10}r \\lesssim -1.45$, $1.32 \\lesssim n_t \\lesssim 2.47$, $1.78\\text{ MeV} \\lesssim T_{\\text{rh}} \\lesssim 28.2\\text{ GeV}$, and $75.86\\text{ nHz} \\lesssim f_{\\text{end}} \\lesssim 14.45\\text{ Hz}$. The preferred scenario has $n_t > 0$ (blue spectrum) and relatively large $r$, implying that the observed GWs correspond to tensor modes that re-entered the Hubble radius during the radiation-dominated era. However, the required $T_{\\text{rh}}$ is extremely low (MeV–GeV range), posing challenges for standard reheating mechanisms.
\n\n
在施加所有观测约束后，用宇宙学解释EPTA信号的可行参数空间被显著压缩。在95%置信水平下，他们得到：$-11.66 \\lesssim \\log_{10}r \\lesssim -1.45$，$1.32 \\lesssim n_t \\lesssim 2.47$，$1.78\\text{ MeV} \\lesssim T_{\\text{rh}} \\lesssim 28.2\\text{ GeV}$，以及$75.86\\text{ nHz} \\lesssim f_{\\text{end}} \\lesssim 14.45\\text{ Hz}$。优选情形具有$n_t > 0$（蓝谱）和相对较大的$r$，表明所观测的引力波对应于在辐射主导时期重新进入哈勃半径的张量模。然而，所需的$T_{\\text{rh}}$极低（MeV–GeV量级），对标准再热机制构成理论挑战。

Conclusion: While an inflationary origin for the EPTA signal is not ruled out, it requires a strongly blue-tilted tensor spectrum ($n_t \\gtrsim 1.3$) and very low reheating temperatures ($T_{\\text{rh}} \\sim \\text{MeV–GeV}$), which are difficult to realize in conventional single-field slow-roll inflation. Such a scenario would demand non-standard inflationary dynamics or alternative mechanisms for generating primordial tensor modes. Thus, although cosmologically possible, the inflationary interpretation faces significant theoretical tension, making astrophysical explanations more plausible unless new physics is invoked.
\n\n
尽管EPTA信号的暴胀起源尚未被排除，但它要求强烈的蓝倾斜张量谱（$n_t \\gtrsim 1.3$）和极低的再热温度（$T_{\\text{rh}} \\sim \\text{MeV–GeV}$），这在传统的单场慢滚暴胀中难以实现。此类情形需要非标准的暴胀动力学或产生原初张量模的替代机制。因此，尽管宇宙学上可能，暴胀解释面临显著的理论张力，除非引入新物理，否则天体物理解释更为合理。

Abstract: The second data release of the European Pulsar Timing Array (EPTA) collaboration provides evidence for the presence of a gravitational-wave (GW) background. In this work, we explore a potential cosmological interpretation of this signal in terms of inflationary scenarios. We parametrize the tensor power spectrum in terms of the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$, the tensor spectral index $n_t$, the reheating temperature $T_{\text{rh}}$, and the cut-off frequency $f_{\text{end}}$. We incorporate all relevant observational constraints, including those from the Cosmic Microwave Background, Big Bang Nucleosynthesis, and LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observations. We demonstrate that imposing these constraints consistently reduces the region of parameter space that provides a viable interpretation of the EPTA signal, to $-11.66 \lesssim \log_{10}r \lesssim -1.45$, $1.32 \lesssim n_t \lesssim 2.47$, $1.78\text{ MeV} \lesssim T_{\text{rh}} \lesssim 28.2\text{ GeV}$, and $75.86\text{ nHz} \lesssim f_{\text{end}} \lesssim 14.45\text{ Hz}$ at the 95% confidence level. This favours the scenario in which the GW spectrum in the EPTA frequency band originates from tensor modes that re-entered the Hubble radius during the radiation-dominated era, allowing for a higher $r$ and a flatter spectrum. However, $T_{\text{rh}}$ must take very low values, which are challenging to explain theoretically.

</details>
